<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48546294' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48546294' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A backdoor in a LinkedIn job offer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LinkedIn招聘广告中的后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1573</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48546294' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A backdoor was discovered embedded in a LinkedIn job offer, illustrating how the recruiting process can be weaponized to compromise targets. The post explains how attackers used the job posting to deliver malware or harvest credentials, highlighting the risk of social engineering on professional networks. It underscores the need for stricter validation of job postings and better phishing defenses by platforms and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 LinkedIn 的一个招聘信息中发现了后门，暴露了招聘流程如何被用来对目标进行入侵。攻击者可能通过该职位发布来投放恶意软件或窃取凭据，凸显了职业社交网络中的钓鱼风险。强调平台和用户都需加强对招聘信息的验证与安全防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/splash.png' data-full='https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/splash.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48555993' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48555993' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vickiboykis.com/2026/06/15/running-local-models-is-good-now/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running local models is good now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地运行模型现在已经可行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 595</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48555993' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent advances in open-source models, tooling, and consumer hardware have made running local models practical again. Local inference offers privacy, lower latency, and offline reliability, though trade-offs in performance and updatability remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>近来开源模型、工具链和硬件水平的提升让本地运行模型重新成为现实，带来隐私、更低延迟和离线可用性等优势，同时在性能与可更新性方面仍存在权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vickiboykis.com/images/refactor_gemma.png' data-full='https://vickiboykis.com/images/refactor_gemma.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48579650' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.midjourney.com/medical/blogpost' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Midjourney Medical</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Midjourney 医疗应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48579650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48579650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 860</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48579650' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Midjourney extends its generative AI into medical contexts, exploring potential uses for imaging, diagnostics, and patient communication. The discussion highlights the tradeoffs between creative flexibility and the stringent safety, privacy, and accuracy requirements in healthcare.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Midjourney 将其生成式 AI 引入医疗场景，探索在影像、诊断与病人沟通中的潜在应用。讨论强调在医疗领域对安全、隐私与准确性等高要求与创意灵活性之间的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48542100' data-tags='AI,Programming,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48542100' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Has anyone replaced Claude/GPT with a local model for daily coding?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：有没有人用本地模型替代 Claude/GPT 进行日常编码？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48542100' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Users discuss replacing cloud-based copilots with locally hosted models for everyday coding tasks. Local models can improve privacy and reduce latency, but may require beefier hardware and careful tuning to approach Claude/GPT capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区在讨论是否可用本地部署的模型来替代云端的助理进行日常编码。虽然本地模型提升隐私与降低延迟的潜力很大，但往往需要更强大的硬件与精心调优以接近 Claude/GPT 的能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48571081' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48571081' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lore.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lore – Open source version control system designed for scalability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lore：为可扩展性设计的开源版本控制系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 670</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48571081' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lore is an open-source version control system built to scale with large codebases and teams. It emphasizes performance and architecture designed to handle millions of files, making collaboration smoother for enterprises and big projects. The takeaway: it positions itself as a scalable alternative to traditional VCS in environments where Git can struggle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lore 是一个为大型代码库和团队协作而设计的开源版本控制系统。它强调高性能和可扩展的架构，能够处理海量文件，从而为企业级大项目提供更顺畅的协作。核心在于它试图在 Git 可能吃力的环境中提供一个可扩展的替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cms-assets.unrealengine.com/AEOpzOpKNRbioUBp6s1Yjz/resize=width:1200/cmpy4vcov0my207oh3rrbztmu' data-full='https://cms-assets.unrealengine.com/AEOpzOpKNRbioUBp6s1Yjz/resize=width:1200/cmpy4vcov0my207oh3rrbztmu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1099</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1630</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48553224' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX拟以约600亿美元收购Cursor，显示其在AI计算能力和高级软件资产方面扩张的策略性动向。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48569278' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48569278' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wpvip.com/future-of-the-web-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sixty percent of US consumers say &#x27;AI&#x27; in brand messaging is a turnoff</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>60% 的美国消费者认为品牌信息中的 AI 会让人反感</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1054</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 564</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48569278' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many US consumers are turned off by AI buzzwords in brand messaging. Brands should emphasize authentic value and real capabilities rather than hype. This trend underscores the need for responsible, outcome-focused AI marketing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>六成美国消费者对品牌信息中的 AI 概念感到反感，认为这是噱头。品牌应聚焦真实价值与实际能力，而非空洞的技术炒作。此趋势凸显在 AI 营销中避免过度承诺、追求可验证结果的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wpvip.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/FOW-REPORT_f53628.jpg' data-full='https://wpvip.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/FOW-REPORT_f53628.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48543475' data-tags='Gaming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48543475' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tinywind.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TinyWind: A pixel pirate sailing game with real wind physics (380k+ kms sailed)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TinyWind：一款具备真实风力物理的像素海盗航海游戏（航行里程超过38万公里）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1000</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48543475' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TinyWind delivers a pixel-art sailing experience driven by plausible wind physics, giving a tactile sense of sailing. With over 380,000 kilometers sailed by players, it demonstrates strong engagement with physics-based gameplay and sparks ideas for indie developers to model wind without heavy graphics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款像素风帆船游戏以贴近真实的风力物理为核心，带来更具手感的航海体验。玩家累计航行里程已超38万公里，体现了对基于物理的玩法的强烈投入，并为独立开发者在无需繁重美术资源的前提下实现可信风场提供了思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tinywind.io/opengraph-image?ca63dcafdaa67b1f' data-full='https://tinywind.io/opengraph-image?ca63dcafdaa67b1f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48561654' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48561654' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/36469-grapheneos-has-been-ported-to-android-17-and-official-releases-are-coming-soon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS has been ported to Android 17</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 已移植至 Android 17，官方发布即将到来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 999</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 587</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48561654' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS has reached a port to Android 17, bringing its hardened, privacy-first Android fork to the latest base. Expect updated security hardening, better device compatibility, and official builds rolling out soon. This signals ongoing momentum for privacy-focused Android variants as core Android updates proceed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 已实现对 Android 17 的移植，延续其以隐私和安全为核心的 Android 派生系统。官方版本预计不久就会发布，带来更新的安全增强和更广的设备兼容性，标志着隐私取向的 Android 生态持续增长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' data-full='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48583928' data-tags='Security,Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48583928' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://orchidfiles.com/github-repositories-distributing-malware/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I found 10k GitHub repositories distributing Trojan malware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我发现一万多个 GitHub 仓库在分发木马恶意软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 951</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48583928' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A collection of about 10,000 GitHub repositories distributing Trojan malware highlights the scale of malware hosted on code platforms. It underscores the importance of supply-chain security and rigorous review of dependencies when building software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大约一万多个 GitHub 仓库被发现用于传播木马恶意软件，揭示代码托管平台上恶意分发的规模。这凸显了在构建软件时加强供应链安全和依赖审查的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600312' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48600312' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://startupfortune.com/hyundai-takes-full-control-of-boston-dynamics-as-softbank-exits-for-325-million/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hyundai buys Boston Dynamics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代集团全面控股 Boston Dynamics</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 945</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48600312' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hyundai has completed securing full control of Boston Dynamics, with SoftBank exiting the stake for about $325 million. This move bets big on Hyundai’s robotics ambitions, signaling a push to integrate robot hardware, AI, and mobility platforms across its business units. Boston Dynamics gains a more stable automotive-aligned parent with resources to scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代集团完成对 Boston Dynamics 的全面控股，软银退出，交易额约3.25亿美元。这笔交易巩固了现代在机器人领域的长期野心，便于在其业务线里整合机器人硬件、人工智能与智能出行生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://startupfortune.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/sf-14065-1781845702426.jpg' data-full='https://startupfortune.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/sf-14065-1781845702426.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48550779' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48550779' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/ID_AA_Carmack/status/2064095424420487226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I admire Fabrice Bellard. He is almost certainly a better overall programmer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我钦佩Fabrice Bellard，他几乎可以算是更出色的全能程序员</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 905</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 443</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48550779' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fabrice Bellard is praised as perhaps the most well-rounded programmer, illustrating how broad expertise across systems software yields outsized impact. His work—behind core projects like QEMU and FFmpeg—embodies the deep, practical engineering that powers modern computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fabrice Bellard在系统软件领域的广泛成就让他被视为最全面的程序员之一，其作品影响广泛。诸如QEMU和FFmpeg等核心项目体现了极致优化与落地工程的力量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1560764938083352577/B1X3m4NN_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1560764938083352577/B1X3m4NN_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48533848' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48533848' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andreklein.net/your-epub-is-fine-kobo-disagrees-blame-adobe/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your ePub Is fine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的 ePub 没问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48533848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 890</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48533848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48533848' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kobo pushes back against blaming Adobe for ePub issues, arguing the format remains solid and that rendering problems are often caused by apps or readers rather than the standard. The post reframes the controversy as a shared interoperability challenge across devices, tools, and publishers, not a single vendor fault. This has practical implications for developers and distributors aiming for broad ePub compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在围绕 ePub 兼容性的争议中，Kobo 反对把问题归咎于 Adobe，认为该格式本身仍然稳健，渲染问题往往来自具体应用与设备实现。该观点将难题定位为读写器、工具和出版商共同面临的互操作挑战，而非某个厂商的错。此事对开发者和出版方在跨设备兼容性上的决策具有现实意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andreklein.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/gandalf-1024x625.jpg' data-full='https://andreklein.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/gandalf-1024x625.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48557530' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48557530' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/942854/apple-vehicle-motion-cues-review-really-work' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s weird anti-nausea dots cured my car sickness</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的抗晕点真的治愈我的车晕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 880</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48557530' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>User tests indicate Apple&#x27;s anti-nausea motion cues dramatically reduced car sickness, with a perception of smoother rides. The piece explains the underlying motion cueing concept and discusses whether it foreshadows broader comfort improvements in mobile or AR displays.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实测显示苹果的抗晕点显著缓解车载晕动，带来更平稳的乘车体验。文中解释了相关的运动提示原理，并讨论它对移动端或 AR 显示的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/268530_SUMMER_UPGRADE_WEEK__NAUSEA_DOTS_ADemidova.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/268530_SUMMER_UPGRADE_WEEK__NAUSEA_DOTS_ADemidova.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48471638' data-tags='Gaming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48471638' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://screen.toys/firewood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firewood Splitting Simulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>劈柴分裂模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 863</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48471638' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lighthearted, physics-based simulator that lets you chop virtual firewood, delivering satisfying feedback loops and simple rules. It’s a tiny reminder that well-tuned mechanics can be more engaging than complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一款以物理为基础的趣味模拟器，玩家通过虚拟劈柴获得直观的反馈与上手简单的玩法。简洁的游戏机制往往比复杂性更具吸引力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48567759' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48567759' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://artificialanalysis.ai/articles/glm-5-2-is-the-new-leading-open-weights-model-on-the-artificial-analysis-intelligence-index' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.2 is the new leading open weights model on Artificial Analysis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.2 成为 Artificial Analysis 上的开源权重新领先者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 862</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 424</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48567759' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.2 has emerged as the leading open-weight model on the Artificial Analysis index, outperforming its predecessors in benchmark tests. It demonstrates strong generalization with competitive inference speed, illustrating how open-weight ecosystems continue to close the gap with closed models. This trend lowers barriers for researchers to experiment with powerful LLMs at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.2 已成为开源权重模型的领先者，领先于前代模型并在基准测试中表现突出。它在推理速度和泛化能力方面表现有竞争力，标志着开源权重生态的成熟。此趋势降低了研究人员在大模型实验中的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/6vfeftx9/articles/204f6bd201e215286fd69d162a84dfdf4a24d197-768x768.webp?w=1200&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/6vfeftx9/articles/204f6bd201e215286fd69d162a84dfdf4a24d197-768x768.webp?w=1200&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48568058' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48568058' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/americas-compact-between-science-and-politics-is-broken/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. science is in chaos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国科学界陷入混乱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 852</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1038</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48568058' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis arguing the U.S. science-politics compact is broken, threatening long-term funding, independent oversight, and scientific consensus.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇分析指出，美国科学与政治之间的默契已破裂，长期资助、独立监督和科学共识受到威胁。文章强调政治极化和短期政策周期削弱了研究生态系统，也影响着科技创新的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/14de7018-3a11-43b8-8392-6e83ccd5b0a3/saw070826Roge01.jpg?m=1779983432.668&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/14de7018-3a11-43b8-8392-6e83ccd5b0a3/saw070826Roge01.jpg?m=1779983432.668&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48516251' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48516251' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tonsky.me/blog/every-frame-perfect/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every Frame Perfect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每一帧都要完美</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 831</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516251' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discourse on achieving pixel-perfect rendering across frames, weighing visual fidelity against performance in modern graphics pipelines. The piece offers practical perspectives on how engineers balance quality, predictability, and resource use to reach consistently polished results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于在每一帧中实现像素级完美渲染的讨论，权衡在现代图形管线中视觉保真度与性能的关系。文中提出工程师在质量、可预测性和资源使用之间的平衡策略，以实现持续一致的高质量输出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dynogee.com/gen?id=24m2qx9uethuw6p&amp;title=Every+Frame+Perfect' data-full='https://dynogee.com/gen?id=24m2qx9uethuw6p&amp;title=Every+Frame+Perfect' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600093' data-tags='Politics,Education,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48600093' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/technology/norway-imposes-near-ban-ai-elementary-school-2026-06-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Norway imposes near ban on AI in elementary school</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>挪威对小学生使用AI几近全面禁令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 801</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 575</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48600093' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Norway is moving toward a near-ban on AI use in elementary schools, tightening rules on what students and teachers can access on school devices. The policy underscores safety concerns and data privacy while grappling with how to responsibly integrate AI into early education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>挪威正推动对小学生在校使用AI的几近全面禁令，严格限制师生在校设备上的AI工具使用。此举凸显对安全与数据隐私的关注，以及在早教阶段如何负责任地引入AI的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48585746' data-tags='Energy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48585746' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bluewin.ch/en/news/switzerland/parliament-lifts-ban-on-new-nuclear-power-plants-3257535.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swiss parliament lifts ban on new nuclear power plants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士议会解除对新核电厂的禁令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48585746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 799</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48585746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 944</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48585746' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Switzerland&#x27;s parliament voted to lift the ban on new nuclear power plants, signaling a pivot in the country&#x27;s energy strategy. The move could affect climate goals, energy security, and the pace of nuclear innovation in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士议会投票解除对新核电厂的禁令，标志着能源战略的转折。此举可能影响碳目标、能源安全以及欧洲在核能创新上的推进速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://production-livingdocs-bluewin-ch.imgix.net/2025/06/02/2a912d11-f11a-4284-b588-adbfeade059b.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;crop=faces' data-full='https://production-livingdocs-bluewin-ch.imgix.net/2025/06/02/2a912d11-f11a-4284-b588-adbfeade059b.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;crop=faces' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48571526' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48571526' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/35949-volkswagen-app?page=3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Volkswagen started blocking GrapheneOS users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大众汽车开始屏蔽 GrapheneOS 用户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 775</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 462</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48571526' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Volkswagen has started blocking GrapheneOS users from its apps. The move highlights ongoing frictions between privacy-focused mobile OS choices and corporate app ecosystems, raising questions about platform-level access and user rights. It underscores how vendor policies can limit privacy-aware users and the ecosystems around open-source mobile OSes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大众汽车开始屏蔽 GrapheneOS 用户使用其应用的行为凸显了隐私导向的移动系统与厂商应用生态之间的矛盾。此举引发对平台访问和用户权益的讨论，也提醒隐私友好型操作系统的使用者可能面临被排除的风险。厂商政策对开源移动生态的影响再次成为焦点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' data-full='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48537165' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48537165' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/2026/06/15/curl-summer-of-bliss/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Curl will not accept vulnerability reports during July 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Curl 将在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 773</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48537165' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The curl project announces a July 2026 hiatus from vulnerability submissions to coordinate disclosures and focus on fixes, with researchers advised to submit reports before or after the window. The move highlights the realities of coordinating security work in a large, open-source project and sets expectations for researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Curl 项目宣布在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告，以便协调披露时间并集中处理修复工作，建议研究人员在此窗口前后提交报告。此举凸显了在大型开源项目中协同安全工作的现实挑战，并为研究人员设定预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/curl-summer-of-bliss.jpg' data-full='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/curl-summer-of-bliss.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48518684' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 releases bring updates to the graphics math library, with API refinements and performance tweaks for modern rendering pipelines. Useful for developers targeting OpenGL and Vulkan workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 发布，带来 API 精炼和性能优化，帮助开发者在现代渲染管线（OpenGL/Vulkan 等）中更高效地使用图形数学工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48553550' data-tags='Hardware,Energy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48553550' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ciechanow.ski/mechanical-watch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mechanical Watch (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机械表（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 715</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48553550' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mechanical Watch (2022) breaks down the fundamentals: a mainspring stores energy, a gear train transmits it, and the escapement and balance wheel regulate time. It also discusses sources of error—friction, temperature, and wear—and why accuracy remains a balancing act between durability and precision.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《机械手表（2022）》分解原理：发条储存能量，通过齿轮传动，靠擒纵机构和摆轮来调控走时。文中还讨论了摩擦、温度和磨损等误差来源，以及在耐用性与精准度之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ciechanow.ski/images/og/watch.png' data-full='https://ciechanow.ski/images/og/watch.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48584207' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48584207' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowslatest.com/2026/06/15/microsofts-new-outlook-takes-10-seconds-to-do-what-outlook-classic-does-instantly-on-windows/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft new Outlook takes 10 seconds to do what Outlook Classic does instantly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软新版 Outlook 比经典版慢十秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 696</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 490</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48584207' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The new Outlook design takes about ten seconds to complete tasks that the classic app did instantly, highlighting a productivity hit from modernization. This reflects a broader tension between feature-rich updates and user experience in enterprise software, fueling calls for an option to revert to a familiar workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新版 Outlook 在完成一些经典版能瞬间完成的任务时需约十秒，暴露出现代化改造带来的生产力损失。这反映了企业软件在功能增强与用户体验之间的博弈，也推动了对保留熟悉工作流的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Clicking-Outlook-notifications-will-take-longer-to-load-the-email-than-if-you-open-the-app-and-find-the-mail-directly.jpg' data-full='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Clicking-Outlook-notifications-will-take-longer-to-load-the-email-than-if-you-open-the-app-and-find-the-mail-directly.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48529990' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48529990' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tamnd/kage' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Kage – Shadow any website to a single binary for offline viewing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Kage — 将任意网站打包为离线可用的单一二进制文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48529990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 688</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48529990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48529990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kage bundles a website into a single standalone binary, enabling offline viewing without a browser or network. It makes distribution and archiving of a site trivial for demos, edge devices, or offline access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kage 将任意网站打包成一个独立的可执行文件，离线也能浏览。提高了便携性，适合离线演示、备份与边缘部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f83b8cc479704de4a3c8bbe1bcfd5d564f74bba6dcf58631b629015428c9bc88/tamnd/kage' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f83b8cc479704de4a3c8bbe1bcfd5d564f74bba6dcf58631b629015428c9bc88/tamnd/kage' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.pragmaticengineer.com/p/why-is-meta-destroying-its-engineering' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is Meta destroying its engineering organization?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 Meta 正在削弱其工程组织？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558045' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 644</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558045' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 598</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48558045' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical take argues Meta&#x27;s engineering organization has weakened due to misaligned incentives, staffing changes, and leadership shifts, slowing delivery and harming morale. It offers organizational design lessons for large-scale tech firms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 Meta 的工程组织因激励错配、人事变动和领导层调整而受挫，导致交付变慢、士气下降。文中给出大型科技公司在组织设计方面的教训与建议。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48569954' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lutr.dev/want-your-images-back-sure-that-ll-be-5-dollars' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Want your images back? That&#x27;ll be $5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>要回你的图片？要收取5美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48569954' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A service charging a flat fee to retrieve your images highlights the economics of data recovery and ownership in an AI-enabled world. It spotlights the friction and costs users face when reclaiming their own media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于用固定费用（5 美元）来让用户找回自己的图片，折射出数据所有权、隐私与数据恢复成本在 AI 时代的经济性与伦理议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lutr.dev/assets/covers/want-your-images-back-sure-that-ll-be-5-dollars.png' data-full='https://lutr.dev/assets/covers/want-your-images-back-sure-that-ll-be-5-dollars.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48595511' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48595511' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jvm-weekly.com/p/project-valhalla-explained-how-a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Valhalla, Explained: How a Decade of Work Arrives in JDK 28</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valhalla 项目解读：十年研发在 JDK 28 落地</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48595511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 631</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48595511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 390</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48595511' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Project Valhalla brings value types and specialized generics to the JVM, enabling compact, allocation-free data representations and faster APIs. This explainer frames the decade-long effort, the tradeoffs involved, and what JDK 28 users can expect in terms of performance, memory efficiency, and backward compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valhalla 将为 JVM 引入值类型与专用泛型，带来更紧凑、无分配的数据表示和更快的 API。本文梳理了十余年的实现过程、设计取舍，以及 JDK 28 用户在性能、内存效率和向后兼容性方面将看到的变化。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48552687' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48552687' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/06/15/feds-freaked-over-fable-5-after-simple-fix-this-code-prompt-not-jailbreak-says-researcher/5255827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Feds freaked over Fable 5 after &#x27;fix this code&#x27;, not jailbreak, say researchers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究人员称，对Fable 5的担忧并非因越狱，而是源于修这段代码的提示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48552687' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers say the Fable 5 incident wasn’t about jailbreaks but a simple fix-this-code prompt that triggered automated defenses. The episode highlights how prompt patterns can provoke legal or policy alarms even when the underlying tech isn&#x27;t compromised.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员指出，对Fable 5的担忧并非源自越狱风险，而是一个简单的修这段代码提示触发的自动防护。此事揭示了提示模式如何诱发策略阻断与调查的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5255863.jpg?imageId=5255863&amp;x=0&amp;y=9.43&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=81.13&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=9.43&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=81.13&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5255863.jpg?imageId=5255863&amp;x=0&amp;y=9.43&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=81.13&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=9.43&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=81.13&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Wi‑Fi smart light bulb secretly hosts a library of banned or hard-to-find texts, turning a household device into a portable knowledge archive. The project provokes thoughts about censorship, distributed aesthetics, and the potential of IoT as a stealth repository.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一颗具备 Wi‑Fi 的智能灯泡成了一个迷你禁书库，将文本打包进常见的物联网设备。该项目展示了硬件与本地网络如何成为信息存储与传播的新载体，也引发对数字审查与数据持久性的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.5 的幻觉比 MIT 许可的 GLM-5.2 高出 3 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 548</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 280</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48600167' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical comparison shows GPT-5.5 hallucinates far more than GLM-5.2, raising reliability concerns for next-gen models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评测显示，在相同条件下，GPT-5.5 的幻觉率比 MIT 许可的 GLM-5.2 高出约三倍，这让人担忧大模型的可靠性。文中讨论可能的原因，以及这对依赖对话型助手的开发者意味着什么。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://therepublicofletters.substack.com/p/calvin-and-hobbes-and-the-price-of' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Calvin and Hobbes and the price of integrity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卡尔文与霍布斯：正直的代价</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 537</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48557079' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful essay uses Calvin and Hobbes to reflect on integrity in a world of incentives, exploring what staying true to one&#x27;s principles costs creators and communities. It illustrates how honesty can shape practice and culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以《卡尔文与霍布斯》为镜，思考在激励驱动的世界中坚持正直的代价，探讨创作者与社区对原则的坚持会带来何种影响。说明诚实如何影响实践与文化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600345' data-tags='Web,Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48600345' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tales.fromprod.com/2026/169/google-workspace-threatening-to-block-firefox.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google workspace threatening to block Firefox access</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Workspace 可能阻止 Firefox 访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48600345' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>If true, Google Workspace blocking Firefox would raise questions about browser compatibility and vendor lock-in in enterprise tools. It would push users toward Chromium-based ecosystems and complicate privacy-focused setups. The broader signal is about how much control platforms exert over client access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>若属实，Google Workspace 可能阻断 Firefox 访问，将引发对企业工具中的浏览器兼容性与厂商锁定的讨论。这会推动用户转向以 Chromium 为核心的生态，并影响偏好隐私的工作环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tales.fromprod.com/static/2026-06-18-google-workspace-threatening-to-block-firefox/warning-screen.png' data-full='https://tales.fromprod.com/static/2026-06-18-google-workspace-threatening-to-block-firefox/warning-screen.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48558018' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48558018' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mareksuppa.com/til/bash-dev-tcp-http-without-curl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TIL: You can make HTTP requests without curl using Bash /dev/TCP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>今天学到：可以用 Bash 的 /dev/TCP 发起 HTTP 请求，无需 curl</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 533</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48558018' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bash can issue HTTP requests by opening a TCP connection with /dev/tcp and writing raw HTTP, avoiding curl. This technique is handy in minimal environments or quick checks, but it&#x27;s brittle, lacks features like redirects and timeouts, and requires manual response parsing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过打开 /dev/tcp 的 TCP 连接并发送原始 HTTP 请求即可在 Bash 中完成网络请求，无需 curl。适合极简环境和快速诊断，但缺乏如重定向、超时等特性，需要手动解析响应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mareksuppa.com/og/bash-dev-tcp-http-without-curl.png' data-full='https://mareksuppa.com/og/bash-dev-tcp-http-without-curl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48599515' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48599515' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://overreacted.io/there-are-no-instances-in-atproto/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There are no instances in ATProto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ATProto 中不存在实例概念</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48599515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48599515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48599515' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ATProto’s architecture is often misunderstood as having &#x27;instances&#x27;. The author argues there are no such isolated &#x27;instances&#x27; in ATProto because federation uses accounts and servers differently, emphasizing interoperability and same-identity across servers. This reframes how developers should think about hosting and moderation in ATProto.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ATProto 的架构常被误解为‘实例’概念。作者指出在 ATProto 的联邦模型里并不存在真正的‘实例’，强调跨服务器的互操作性和统一身份的重要性，这对开发者在托管与 moderation 上的思路有显著影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://overreacted.io/there-are-no-instances-in-atproto/opengraph-image?5b0b970dfd19bb8c' data-full='https://overreacted.io/there-are-no-instances-in-atproto/opengraph-image?5b0b970dfd19bb8c' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48559935' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48559935' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arseniyshestakov.com/2026/06/16/apple-is-about-to-make-hide-my-email-useless/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple is about to make Hide My Email useless</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果即将让 Hide My Email 失效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48559935' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A forthcoming change could undermine Hide My Email, reducing its ability to shield users’ real addresses when signing up for services. Privacy-focused users may need to adapt or seek alternatives as Apple alters how aliases are exposed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传闻称苹果即将改变 Hide My Email 的工作方式，降低该功能对用户隐私的保护，可能使真实邮箱更易暴露。隐私导向的用户需要适应新的使用方式，或寻找替代方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48540844' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48540844' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.hetzner.com/general/infrastructure-and-availability/price-adjustment/#cloud-servers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hetzner Price Adjustment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hetzner 价格调整</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 709</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48540844' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner updates its pricing for cloud servers and related services, affecting cost calculations for small squads and hobby projects. Users should review the new rates and adjust budgets or workflows accordingly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner 调整云服务器及相关服务价格，可能影响小型团队和业余项目的成本计算。用户需要审阅新价格并相应调整预算与工作流。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48608223' data-tags='Web,Design,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48608223' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cssquake.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CSSQuake</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSSQuake</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608223' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608223' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48608223' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSSQuake is a browser-based project that demonstrates CSS-driven visuals in a quake-inspired demo. It showcases how far CSS alone can push interactive, game-like experiences without heavy JavaScript.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CSSQuake 是一个在浏览器中展示 CSS 驱动视觉效果的 quake 风格演示。它展示了仅用 CSS 就能实现接近游戏体验的交互能力。</p>
