<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600946' data-tags='Legal,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48600946' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/court-records-should-be-free' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Court Records Should Be Free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院记录应免费开放</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 513</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48600946' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EFF argues that court records should be freely accessible and machine-readable to improve transparency, research, and accountability. The movement pushes for open court data standards and reduces friction for journalists, researchers, and the public.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EFF 呼吁法院记录应免费向公众开放并可机器可读，以提升透明度、研究和问责。倡导建立公开的法院数据标准，降低记者、研究者和公众的使用成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/supreme-court-3b.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/supreme-court-3b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48608645' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48608645' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mysk-research/loupe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Loupe – A iOS app that raises awareness about what native apps can see</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Loupe：一款揭示原生应用可访问数据的 iOS 应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 505</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48608645' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Loupe is an iOS app that visually demonstrates what native apps can access on-device, helping users understand app permissions and data exposure. The project underscores the importance of privacy transparency in mobile ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Loupe 是一款用来直观展示原生应用能访问哪些数据的 iOS 应用，帮助用户理解应用权限与数据暴露。该项目凸显了移动生态系统中隐私透明度的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2baf64109f9489db61acbd445290b098b62ed91c8e4e7579835a356b629fe7e1/mysk-research/loupe' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2baf64109f9489db61acbd445290b098b62ed91c8e4e7579835a356b629fe7e1/mysk-research/loupe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48565498' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48565498' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/china/us-holds-off-blacklisting-chinas-deepseek-more-than-100-firms-deemed-security-2026-06-17/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US holds off blacklisting DeepSeek, more than 100 firms deemed security risks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国推迟将 DeepSeek 列入黑名单，超百家企业被视为安全风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 549</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48565498' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US deferred a blacklist decision on DeepSeek while revealing a broader security-risk sweep that flagged more than 100 firms. The move underscores tightening export controls and heightened vendor risk scrutiny across tech supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国推迟将 DeepSeek 列入黑名单，但披露对超百家企业进行安全风险评估的结果，反映出对技术供应链与出口管制的持续收紧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48550693' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48550693' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260615-00/?p=112419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The time the x86 emulator team found code so bad they fixed it during emulation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>X86 模拟器团队在仿真中直接修复糟糕代码的故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 492</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48550693' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>While running legacy x86 code, the emulator team uncovered a section so badly written that it risked correctness. They patched the behavior directly in the emulator during the debugging session, effectively fixing the issue without modifying the source. The story highlights how emulation can surface design flaws and enable quick, in-situ fixes for stubborn code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在仿真环境中运行遗留的 x86 代码时，团队发现了一段异常糟糕的实现，严重影响正确性。他们在仿真过程中直接对行为进行了修正，几乎是在现场完成了修复，而无需改动源代码。这个故事展示了仿真工具在暴露设计缺陷并实现快速就地修复方面的强大力量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48618455' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48618455' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.claude.com/en/articles/14328960-identity-verification-on-claude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Identity verification on Claude</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 的身份验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 490</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 444</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48618455' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude includes an identity verification flow to control access for sensitive features and enterprise usage. The guide covers when verification is required, what methods are supported, and how it affects access privileges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 提供身份验证流程以控制对敏感功能和企业用途的访问。指南说明何时需要验证、支持哪些方法，以及对权限访问的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' data-full='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48598586' data-tags='Education,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48598586' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vocabowl-870366514258.us-west1.run.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How many of the 170k English words do you know?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你认识多少个约17万的英语单词？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48598586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 487</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48598586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 548</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48598586' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful tool invites you to estimate how many English words you know from a list of about 170k, highlighting vocabulary size and variation among readers. It illustrates why fluency relies on context and frequency as much as memorization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款有趣的工具让你在约17万单词的清单中估算自己的词汇量，揭示不同读者之间的差异。它说明了流利程度更多依赖上下文与词频，而非记住所有单词。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48558147' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48558147' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/samsch/0d1f3d3b4745d778f78b230cf6061452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop Using JWTs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>停止使用 JWTs</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 481</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48558147' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise critique of JSON Web Tokens, highlighting common misuses like improper storage, algorithm pitfalls, and relying on long-lived tokens. The piece advocates safer authentication patterns and modern alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简要批评 JSON Web Token 的常见误用，如存储不当、算法陷阱和对长期令牌的依赖。文中提倡采用更安全的认证模式和新兴替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48581458' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48581458' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chat.deepseek.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek Introduces Vision</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 推出 Vision</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48581458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 480</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48581458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48581458' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek releases Vision, a new computer-vision platform meant to simplify building AI-powered visual apps. It emphasizes developer-friendly APIs and real-time performance across edge and cloud deployments. The launch could influence how teams prototype and deploy CV workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 发布 Vision，这是一款旨在简化构建AI驱动视觉应用的新型计算机视觉平台。它强调面向开发者友好的 API 和在边缘及云端的实时性能。此次发布可能影响团队在原型设计与CV工作负载部署上的方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48536776' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48536776' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/cli-sdks-libraries/libraries/apple-foundation-models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Foundation Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48536776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48536776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48536776' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple releases a suite of foundation models and accompanying SDKs to enable on-device AI workflows, signaling deeper AI integration in the Apple ecosystem. The post outlines available models and APIs, and explains how developers can leverage them for tasks like vision, language, and understanding while prioritizing privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出一系列基础模型及配套的 SDK，旨在在设备端实现 AI 工作流，标志着苹果生态对 AI 的更深度整合。文章列出可用的模型与 API，并解释开发者如何在保护隐私的前提下，利用它们完成视觉、语言和理解等任务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.claude.com/docs/og?locale=en&amp;path=cli-sdks-libraries/libraries/apple-foundation-models' data-full='https://platform.claude.com/docs/og?locale=en&amp;path=cli-sdks-libraries/libraries/apple-foundation-models' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48527700' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48527700' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gabrielweinberg.com/p/people-are-consuming-ai-like-they' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Not everyone is using AI for everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>并非人人都把 AI 用到极致</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 473</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 507</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48527700' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece challenges AI fever by arguing most people are not using AI for everything; adoption remains selective and task-specific. It emphasizes realistic expectations and practical limits of AI in everyday life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章对 AI 热潮提出质疑，表示大多数人并未把 AI 用来做所有事情，应用仍然是有选择性的。强调对 AI 的实际价值与局限要有清晰判断。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!n_r-!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F957dc207-33f4-4e66-a821-66f340c9a623_1424x1404.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!n_r-!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F957dc207-33f4-4e66-a821-66f340c9a623_1424x1404.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48580209' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48580209' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.alexellis.io/local-ai-is-not-opus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local Qwen isn&#x27;t a worse Opus, it&#x27;s a different tool</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地 Qwen 不是比 Opus 差，而是另一种工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48580209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 470</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48580209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48580209' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Local Qwen and Opus serve different needs in offline AI deployment; the post argues it&#x27;s not about one being worse, but about fitting the use case, latency, and autonomy requirements. It contrasts the tradeoffs between size, accuracy, and privacy in local models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本地版 Qwen 与 Opus 面向离线部署的需求不同，文章认为两者并非孰优孰劣，而是各自适配的使用场景、延迟和自主性需求。对比了在本地模型中尺寸、准确性与隐私之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.alexellis.io/content/images/2026/06/17/6000.jpg' data-full='https://blog.alexellis.io/content/images/2026/06/17/6000.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48589501' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48589501' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/elkjop-forced-consent-fine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I told them forced consent was unlawful. 5 years later it cost Elkjop €1.8M</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我曾告诉他们强制同意违法，5年后 Elkjøp 被罚 180 万欧元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48589501' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48589501' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48589501' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forcing user consent is unlawful and can trigger hefty penalties; this case shows a retailer paying €1.8M five years later. The outcome underscores the necessity of informed, freely given, and revocable consent, plus robust privacy-by-design practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>强制获取用户同意违反法律，最终导致高额罚款；该案五年后 Elkjøp 被处以 €180 万罚金，凸显知情、自由且可撤销同意的重要性，以及以隐私为设计核心的合规实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/assets/2026-06-18-001.png' data-full='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/assets/2026-06-18-001.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48602352' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48602352' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.legacy.com/legacy/robert-bobby-prince-lll' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bobby Prince, composer for Doom, Wolfenstein 3D, and Duke Nukem 3D, has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>悼念 Doom、Wolfenstein 3D 与 Duke Nukem 3D 作曲家 Bobby Prince</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 462</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48602352' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bobby Prince, the composer behind iconic soundtracks for Doom, Wolfenstein 3D, and Duke Nukem 3D, has died. His work helped define the sound of early FPS games and remains influential in game music history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为《毁灭战士》、Duke Nukem 3D 等经典游戏创作配乐的作曲家Bobby Prince去世。他的音乐塑造了早期FPS游戏的声音风格，并持续影响着游戏音乐史。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.tukioswebsites.com/social/facebook/fb_3/5d0135b3-c96e-43bd-bc85-f444720770cb/39975cfe7effdc1:0_' data-full='https://cdn.tukioswebsites.com/social/facebook/fb_3/5d0135b3-c96e-43bd-bc85-f444720770cb/39975cfe7effdc1:0_' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48606140' data-tags='Science,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48606140' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moultano.wordpress.com/2026/06/19/where-to-find-the-colors-your-screen-cant-show-you/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Where to Find the Colors Your Screen Can&#x27;t Show You</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>屏幕显示不到的颜色在哪里可以找到</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 462</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48606140' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Every display is limited by its color gamut, so some hues simply can&#x27;t be shown. The article surveys ways to explore or approximate those beyond-screen colors—from spectral imaging and printing to perceptual color spaces like CIELAB—and explains why designers should think beyond sRGB. It also offers practical tips for communicating color intent when screens fall short.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>屏幕的颜色范围有限，某些色调无法在显示器上呈现。文章探讨如何接近或再现这些超出屏幕能力的颜色，涵盖分光成像、印刷，以及感知为基础的颜色空间（如CIELAB），并提供在屏幕受限时传达颜色意图的实用方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://moultano.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/pxl_20260509_0046574427e2_20260618192953.jpg' data-full='https://moultano.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/pxl_20260509_0046574427e2_20260618192953.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48560847' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48560847' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.timescolonist.com/local-news/us-pulling-ocean-sensors-a-shock-for-canadian-research-as-el-nino-nears-12422874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. pulling ocean sensors a &#x27;shock&#x27; for Canadian research as El Niño nears</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国撤走海洋传感器让加拿大研究感到震惊，厄尔尼诺临近</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48560847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48560847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 270</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48560847' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With El Niño approaching, the US pulling ocean sensors reduces critical data for climate forecasting and marine research. The move could hamper Canadian research and cross-border collaboration that relies on shared sensor networks. Researchers may need alternative data sources or longer data lags to model upcoming climate patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在厄尔尼诺来临之际，美国撤走海洋传感器，削弱了用于气候预测和海洋研究的关键数据来源。此举可能影响加拿大及区域科研与跨境合作，研究人员或需寻找替代数据源。面对即将到来的气候变化，他们或将承担数据滞后与不确定性的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48584135' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48584135' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rahuljuliato.com/posts/emacs-31-around-the-corner' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emacs 31 is around the corner: The changes I&#x27;m daily driving</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Emacs 31 即将到来：我日常使用中的变更点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 452</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48584135' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As Emacs 31 approaches, the author details daily-driving changes that improve editing, navigation, and package management. These practical tweaks promise meaningful productivity gains for power users who customize their workflow. It’s a glimpse into how small iterates accumulate into a better editing experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 Emacs 31 的到来，作者分享日常使用中的改动点，涵盖编辑、导航和包管理等方面的提升。这些实际改动对定制工作流的高级用户可能带来显著的生产力提升，展示了小改动如何累积成更高效的编辑体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.rahuljuliato.com/assets/blog/posts/emacs-31-around-the-corner.png' data-full='https://www.rahuljuliato.com/assets/blog/posts/emacs-31-around-the-corner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48553388' data-tags='Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48553388' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.greybeam.ai/blog/duckdb-internals-part-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DuckDB Internals Part 1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 内部实现：第一部分</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 452</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48553388' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An introductory look into DuckDB&#x27;s internals, covering architecture, storage layout, and query execution approach. The post sets the stage for deeper dives into its columnar engine and optimization tricks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 内部实现的入门分析，涵盖架构、存储布局与查询执行方式，为后续对其列式引擎与优化技巧的深入探讨奠定基础。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/eb/37/eb37738a-77f2-4eaf-9de2-43f9b30fffd5/content/images/2026/05/image--16-.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/eb/37/eb37738a-77f2-4eaf-9de2-43f9b30fffd5/content/images/2026/05/image--16-.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.intheweights.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Are You in the Weights?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48591348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48591348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48591348' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN presents a project centered on model weights, inviting the community to explore, audit, or contribute to a weights-focused tool or dataset.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了一个聚焦模型权重的项目，邀请社区参与探索、审阅或为一个权重相关的工具或数据集贡献。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600107' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48600107' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aiclambake.com/clamtakes/linear-a/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Engineer Claims to Have Cracked Linear A</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 工程师声称破解线性 A</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 442</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48600107' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI engineer claims to have deciphered Linear A using machine learning. The claim is controversial and lacks independent verification, but if substantiated it would upend assumptions about ancient scripts and AI’s reach in decipherment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名 AI 工程师声称使用机器学习破解线性 A，但缺乏独立验证。若属实，这将颠覆对古代文字的认知和 AI 在解密领域的应用边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aiclambake.com/images/og-default.png' data-full='https://aiclambake.com/images/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48582320' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48582320' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/cpus/amd-silently-removes-memory-encryption-from-consumer-ryzen-cpus-leaving-users-unaware-that-they-may-be-vulnerable-security-feature-vanishes-after-newer-agesa-firmware-amd-engineers-go-radio-silent-when-pressed-about-the-change' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD silently removes memory encryption from consumer Ryzen CPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD悄然移除消费级 Ryzen CPU 的内存加密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48582320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 433</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48582320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48582320' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD quietly removed memory encryption from some consumer Ryzen CPUs via a firmware update, reducing protection against memory-snooping attacks. The change was not publicly announced, leaving users to discover it through firmware notes. The move has security implications for memory-resident data and enclaves, and affected users should consider staying on older AGESA firmware or applying compensating protections where available.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 通过固件更新悄然移除了部分消费级 Ryzen CPU 的内存加密，削弱了对内存窃取等攻击的保护。这一变更未公开宣布，用户需要通过固件说明才发现。此举对内存驻留数据和安全区域有影响，受影响的用户应考虑保留较旧的 AGESA 固件版本或在可用时应用替代保护措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/UdLqeSxzsPucb6GRFDjLm6-1600-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/UdLqeSxzsPucb6GRFDjLm6-1600-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48586631' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48586631' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.submission.directory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A website that lists websites to submit your website to</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个汇总可提交你的网站的目录网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 432</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48586631' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An index that aggregates websites you can submit your own site to, illustrating how directory submissions organized the early web. It highlights a form of link-building that has faded from mainstream SEO, while still offering discovery channels for small sites.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个集合了可提交自己网站的入口目录，展示了早期网页如何通过目录提交来提升曝光。虽然如今被视为过时的 SEO 策略，但对小型站点仍有发现新读者的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.submission.directory/og.png' data-full='https://www.submission.directory/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48592832' data-tags='Design,Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48592832' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theescapenewsletter.com/p/the-airpods-effect' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AirPods Effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AirPods 效应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48592832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48592832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 739</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48592832' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The AirPods effect reshaped consumer expectations around wireless audio, battery life, and device interoperability. It spurred rapid adoption of true wireless design, influenced accessory ecosystems, and shifted how tech brands think about on-device interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AirPods 效应改变了消费者对无线音频、续航与设备互操作性的预期，推动了真无线设计的快速普及并影响周边生态。它也促使厂商重新思考设备交互的设计与生态策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1695112956255-8878a4443236?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wzMDAzMzh8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwyfHxhaXJwb2RzJTIwd29tYW4lMjBidXN8ZW58MHx8fHwxNzc5MjcwNzA2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1695112956255-8878a4443236?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wzMDAzMzh8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwyfHxhaXJwb2RzJTIwd29tYW4lMjBidXN8ZW58MHx8fHwxNzc5MjcwNzA2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48583606' data-tags='Education,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48583606' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cs.cornell.edu/courses/cs6120/2025fa/self-guided/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CS 6120: Advanced Compilers: The Self-Guided Online Course (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高级编译器：自学在线课程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 420</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48583606' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cornell&#x27;s Advanced Compilers self-guided online course offers deep dives into optimization, intermediate representations, and code generation, with hands-on exercises you can tackle at your own pace. It&#x27;s a solid resource for researchers and senior developers aiming to master modern compiler tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>康奈尔大学的高级编译器自学课程提供对优化、中间表示和代码生成的深入讲解，并配有可自学的动手练习。对于研究人员和资深开发者而言，是掌握现代编译技术的扎实资源。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48588216' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48588216' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.independent.co.uk/us/money/craigslist-multimillionaire-craig-newmark-b2980681.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The founder of Craigslist has given away half a billion dollars</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Craigslist 创始人捐出 5 亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48588216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48588216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48588216' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Craig Newmark, Craigslist&#x27;s founder, has given away about $500 million, underscoring a notable philanthropic commitment from a tech founder. The donations likely support journalism and civil society causes, reflecting a trend of tech leaders funding public goods.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Craigslist 创始人 Craig Newmark 已捐出约 5 亿美元，展示了科技创始人对公益的长期承诺。这笔捐款大多用于支持新闻业、公民社会等领域，体现科技领袖在公共事务上的影响力与责任感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.independent.co.uk/s3fs-public/thumbnails/image/2009/06/10/22/185577.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' data-full='https://static.independent.co.uk/s3fs-public/thumbnails/image/2009/06/10/22/185577.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48585866' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48585866' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ui.com/article/introducing-enterprise-nas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ubiquiti: Enterprise NAS, Built on ZFS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ubiquiti：企业级 NAS，基于 ZFS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48585866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 410</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48585866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 347</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48585866' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ubiquiti announces an enterprise NAS built on ZFS, emphasizing data integrity, snapshots, and scalable storage for SMBs. The product signals growing interest in software-defined storage among cost-conscious businesses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ubiquiti 推出基于 ZFS 的企业级 NAS，强调数据完整性、快照功能与可扩展存储，面向中小企业。此举显示成本敏感型企业对软件定义存储兴趣上升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.blog.svc.ui.com/ENAS_Thumb_5f1852f45c.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.blog.svc.ui.com/ENAS_Thumb_5f1852f45c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48617990' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48617990' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.beyondallreason.info' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beyond All Reason (Free Total Annihilation Inspired RTS)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超越一切理性：免费 Total Annihilation 风格的 RTS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48617990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Beyond All Reason is a free, community-driven RTS inspired by Total Annihilation, offering large-scale battles and classic resource economics. Built with modern, mod-friendly architecture, it emphasizes open development and cross-platform play, sustaining retro RTS enthusiasm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Beyond All Reason 是一款免费、由社区驱动的实时策略游戏，受《Total Annihilation》启发，提供大规模战斗与经典资源系统。它采用现代化、便于修改的架构，强调开源开发与跨平台对战，延续对经典 RTS 的热爱。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5c68622246b367adf6f3041d/60d97aab6fdf2731bde767e8_BAR_Web_OG-media-2021_1200_V2.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5c68622246b367adf6f3041d/60d97aab6fdf2731bde767e8_BAR_Web_OG-media-2021_1200_V2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48558489' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48558489' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tim.blog/2026/06/12/has-ai-already-killed-nonfiction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Has AI already killed self-help nonfiction books?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 已经让自助类非小说类书籍没落了吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 405</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 476</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48558489' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-generated content is challenging mass-market self-help nonfiction by commodifying generic insights. Authors can survive by offering verifiable frameworks, interactive courses, and proprietary systems that AI can’t easily imitate. The piece questions whether AI accelerates the genre’s evolution or undermines it entirely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能生成的内容正在以同质化洞见压缩自助类非小说书籍的市场空间。作者若以可验证的框架、互动课程和专有体系提供难以被 AI 模仿的独特价值，仍有生存机会。文章在探讨 AI 是推动这一类型进化，还是让它变得不再有竞争力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tim.blog/wp-content/uploads/2020/09/socialmedia_timFerriss_200114_005.jpg' data-full='https://tim.blog/wp-content/uploads/2020/09/socialmedia_timFerriss_200114_005.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48570948' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48570948' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://charitydotwtf.substack.com/p/ai-demands-more-engineering-discipline' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI demands more engineering discipline. Not less</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 需要更强的工程化规范，而非放松</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48570948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48570948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48570948' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI systems scale, engineering discipline becomes essential for reliability and safety. The piece argues for stronger practices in testing, governance, and data provenance, not looser constraints. Hooding hype with rigorous engineering is presented as a competitive advantage in the long run.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 系统规模化后，需更强的工程化规范来确保可靠性和安全性。文中强调应加强测试、治理与数据溯源等环节，而不是降低标准。过度炒作容易掩盖风险，严格的工程实践才是长期竞争力的关键。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48620090' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48620090' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sandimetz.com/blog/2016/1/20/the-wrong-abstraction' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prefer duplication over the wrong abstraction (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宁可重复，也别用错的抽象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620090' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Duplicating code can keep intent clear when an abstraction would misfit. Premature, poorly fitting abstractions hurt maintainability; the post argues that clarity beats clever architecture and that refactoring can come later when the need is real.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在抽象不合适时，重复实现能保持意图清晰。过早的、与场景不符的抽象会降低可维护性；文章主张清晰胜过花哨的架构，真正需要时再进行重构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/537c0374e4b0f52ed92942e6/1406255585576-8V9OA9Q3VR7LM1P1NCAE/logo-color.png?format=1500w' data-full='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/537c0374e4b0f52ed92942e6/1406255585576-8V9OA9Q3VR7LM1P1NCAE/logo-color.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48573332' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48573332' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/17/only-16-percent-of-americans-think-ai-will-have-a-positive-impact-on-society-a-new-study-shows/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Only 16 Percent of Americans Think AI Will Have a Positive Impact on Society</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>只有16% 的美国人认为 AI 会对社会产生积极影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 492</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48573332' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study finds only 16% of Americans believe AI will benefit society. Public skepticism stems from concerns about jobs, bias, and privacy, challenging optimistic narratives. The data suggests policymakers and companies must address real fears to foster broader AI adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示只有 16% 的美国人认为 AI 会对社会带来积极影响。公众担忧集中在就业、偏见和隐私等方面，这一结果挑战乐观叙事。此数据提示政策制定者与企业需要直面公众担忧，推动更广泛的 AI 应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/spacexipo-nasdaq.jpg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/spacexipo-nasdaq.jpg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48568502' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48568502' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc10008/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFC 10008: The new HTTP Query Method</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RFC 10008：新的 HTTP 查询方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48568502' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RFC 10008 proposes a new HTTP Query Method to formalize complex data queries separate from resource retrieval. If standardized, it could influence API design, request semantics, and caching behavior, shaping how developers express data requests over HTTP.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RFC 10008 提出一种新的 HTTP 查询方法，以将复杂查询与资源检索分离的方式标准化。如果成为标准，可能影响 API 设计、请求语义和缓存行为，改变开发者在 HTTP 上表达数据请求的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.rfc-editor.org/api/v1/meta-thumbnail/rfc10008.png' data-full='https://www.rfc-editor.org/api/v1/meta-thumbnail/rfc10008.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48576838' data-tags='DevOps,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48576838' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/information-technology/2026/06/tesco-moving-40000-server-workloads-off-vmware-amid-broadcoms-abusive-conduct/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesco moving 40k server workloads off VMware amid Broadcom&#x27;s abusive conduct</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tesco 将 4 万台服务器工作负载从 VMware 转移，因 Broadcom 的不当行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48576838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48576838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 255</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48576838' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesco is migrating 40,000 server workloads away from VMware, citing Broadcom’s licensing and conduct as a push factor. The move signals enterprise preference for less punitive licensing and more flexible virtualization or cloud options. It reflects ongoing vendor tensions and cost containment in large retailers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tesco 将 4 万台服务器工作负载从 VMware 转移，原因指向 Broadcom 的许可策略和行为问题。这一举动透露大型企业对更灵活的虚拟化或云方案的偏好，以及对厂商定价与策略的担忧，折射出行业的成本管控压力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48528371' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48528371' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rio de Janeiro&#x27;s &quot;homegrown&quot; LLM appears to be a merge of an existing model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>里约热内卢的‘本土化’大模型似乎是现有模型的合并</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48528371' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early analysis suggests the Rio de Janeiro project reuses components from an existing LLM rather than training an original one from scratch. This provenance has implications for licensing, attribution, and how the model behaves in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>初步分析表明，该项目在现有大模型基础上混合组件，而非完全从头训练。这对许可、署名和实际表现等方面都可能产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/04f1551c5a62546c1dd9c9a478b1462b32444b66fbf27065a4fd61c3bcbeff59/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/04f1551c5a62546c1dd9c9a478b1462b32444b66fbf27065a4fd61c3bcbeff59/nex-agi/Nex-N2/issues/4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48611411' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waxy.org/2026/06/the-wholesale-plagiarism-of-obscure-sorrows/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Wholesale Plagiarism of Obscure Sorrows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Obscure Sorrows》的大规模抄袭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48611411' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of pervasive copying of Obscure Sorrows content, examining how fan fiction and derivative works undermine originality and the consequences for creators. The piece calls for attribution and respect for source material.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Obscure Sorrows 内容被广泛抄袭的现象进行批评，探讨衍生作品对原创性的冲击及对创作者的影响，呼吁署名与尊重原作。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48613555' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48613555' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cell.com/neuron/fulltext/S0896-6273(26)00339-9' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Slow breathing modulates brain function and risk behavior</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>缓慢呼吸调控大脑功能与风险行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48613555' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Slow breathing techniques modulate brain activity and influence risk-taking behavior, likely via autonomic and vagal pathways. The finding suggests simple breathwork could help regulate mood and decision-making in high-stakes settings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>缓慢呼吸技巧能调控大脑活动并影响风险偏好，可能通过自主神经与迷走神经通路实现。这一发现让人们相信简单的呼吸练习可在高压场景中调节情绪与决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I indexed 669 GB of my GoPro videos using my M1 Max computer and local ML models</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48528029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48528029' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal project indexing 669 GB of GoPro footage locally on an M1 Max using on-device ML, illustrating the viability of private, offline media indexing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在 M1 Max 上使用本地机器学习对 669 GB 的 GoPro 视频进行离线索引，展示私有化、离线处理的可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48616800' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48616800' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.apnic.net/2026/04/28/google-hits-50-ipv6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Hits 50% IPv6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌 IPv6 使用率达到了 50%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48616800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48616800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48616800' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google now serves about half of its traffic over IPv6, marking a milestone in the long drive toward IPv6 ubiquity. This shift reduces reliance on IPv4 and suggests major networks and clients are ready for native IPv6, with implications for routing, edge services, and future protocol deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 现在约有一半流量通过 IPv6 传输，标志着向 IPv6 普及的里程碑。这一转变表明主要网络与客户端已为原生 IPv6 做好准备，可能影响路由、边缘服务配置以及未来的协议部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/50-percent_ft.jpg' data-full='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/50-percent_ft.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/06/260614012006.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The brain was not designed for this much bad news</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大脑并非为如此大量的坏消息而设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48615569' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Research shows constant exposure to alarming news can overwhelm attention and degrade mental health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示持续暴露于让人不安的新闻会耗尽注意力并损害心理健康。文中讨论媒体摄取的影响，以及限制暴露、保护认知资源的实际策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/serious-woman-upset-by-bad-news-on-smartphone.webp' data-full='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/serious-woman-upset-by-bad-news-on-smartphone.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A home-lab enthusiast builds an AI development platform at home, sharing architecture, tooling, and workflows to run experiments privately. The project highlights how hobbyists can DIY scalable AI stacks with consumer hardware while maintaining control over data.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/ai/2026/06/leaked-financial-docs-show-openai-is-losing-billions-of-dollars-a-year/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaked financial docs show OpenAI is losing billions of dollars a year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泄露的财务文件显示 OpenAI 每年亏损数十亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48577208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 359</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48577208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48577208' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Leaked financial documents reveal OpenAI is losing billions annually, underscoring the expense of scaling cutting-edge AI. The numbers highlight the tension between ambitious capabilities and profitability, likely forcing pricing, partnerships, or efficiency improvements. The situation emphasizes the need for a sustainable financial strategy in high-cost AI initiatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泄露的财务文件显示 OpenAI 每年亏损数十亿美元，凸显扩展前沿 AI 成本之高。数字反映了愿景与盈利之间的张力，可能推动定价、合作与效率方面的调整。此事强调在高成本 AI 项目中建立可持续财政策略的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
