<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dexerto.com/gaming/stop-killing-games-fails-to-secure-eu-law-despite-1-3m-signatures-3376431/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop Killing Games fails to secure EU law despite 1.3M signatures</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stop Killing Games 未能在欧盟通过立法，尽管获得130万签名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48564696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48564696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 313</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48564696' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite 1.3 million signatures backing a campaign, the effort to secure EU-wide legislation stalled, illustrating the gap between public petition volume and actual policy outcomes. The development sheds light on the EU&#x27;s legislative process and the challenges of turning advocacy into law.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管有130万签名支持，该倡议在欧盟层面推动立法的努力未能通过，显示公众请愿量并不直接等同于政策结果。此事揭示了欧盟立法过程的复杂性以及将公众倡议转化为法律的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.dexerto.com/cdn-image/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/16/stop-killing-games-fails.jpg' data-full='https://www.dexerto.com/cdn-image/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/16/stop-killing-games-fails.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48563394' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48563394' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mll.sh/humiliating-iis-servers-for-fun-and-jail-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Humiliating IIS servers for fun and jail time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为乐趣羞辱 IIS 服务器可能招致监禁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48563394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 356</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48563394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48563394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security researcher’s prank involving IIS servers leads to serious legal trouble, illustrating how seemingly harmless testing can cross legal boundaries. The case underscores the importance of responsible disclosure and obtaining explicit permission before probing systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这起围绕 IIS 服务器的恶作剧案例揭示了看似无害的安全测试若未获授权就可能触及法律红线。强调了负责任披露与获得明确许可的重要性，以及合规测试的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mll.sh/assets/img/iis-shortname-github.png' data-full='https://mll.sh/assets/img/iis-shortname-github.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48535886' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48535886' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://karthinks.com/software/even-more-batteries-included-with-emacs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Even more batteries included with Emacs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Emacs 内置功能再升级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48535886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48535886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48535886' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emacs continues to expand its &#x27;batteries-included&#x27; philosophy, adding more built-in capabilities and sensible defaults that reduce the amount of external configuration needed. The piece surveys recent enhancements, from packages to core improvements, and argues the editor remains a capable, self-contained platform for developers and writers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Emacs 进一步扩展了“内置功能即服务”的理念，增加了更多内置能力和更合理的默认设置，减少外部配置的需求。文章梳理了最近的改进与核心提升，强调该编辑器仍是面向开发者与作者的强大、可自给自足的平台。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://karthinks.com/img/dictionary-tooltip-01.png' data-full='https://karthinks.com/img/dictionary-tooltip-01.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48542132' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48542132' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.monash.edu/news/articles/copper-drug-restores-memory-and-clears-toxic-alzheimers-proteins' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Copper transport drug restores memory and clears toxic Alzheimer&#x27;s proteins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>铜运输药物能恢复记忆并清除有毒的阿尔茨海默蛋白</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48542132' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A copper-transport drug showing memory restoration and clearance of toxic Alzheimer&#x27;s proteins in preclinical work points to a novel therapeutic angle. While early, the result underscores metal-homeostasis biology as a potential avenue for neurodegenerative treatment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>早期研究显示一种铜运输药物在前瞻性实验中能恢复记忆并清除与阿尔茨海默相关的有毒蛋白，提示金属稳态在神经退行性疾病治疗中的潜在方向，尽管仍处于初步阶段。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48540499' data-tags='Business,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48540499' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/business/deals/fox-roku-deal-f6e564f9' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fox to buy Roku</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>福克斯拟收购罗克</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48540499' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fox&#x27;s potential acquisition of Roku could reshape streaming distribution, advertising, and platform strategy. The deal would combine Fox&#x27;s content with Roku&#x27;s hardware and ad-tech reach, potentially accelerating consolidation in the streaming ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>若交易达成，福克斯收购罗克的举措可能重塑流媒体分发、广告与平台策略，将内容方与硬件、广告技术资源整合，或促成流媒体生态的进一步整合。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48614844' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48614844' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fosterelli.co/developers-dont-understand-cors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Developers don&#x27;t understand CORS (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开发者对 CORS 的误解（2019）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 349</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48614844' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CORS remains a frequent source of confusion for developers; the post clarifies how browsers enforce cross-origin requests and how servers should configure headers to enable safe access. Misconfigurations cause failed fetches and security risks; understanding CORS helps build robust APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者对 CORS 的误解常见；本文澄清浏览器如何强制跨域请求以及服务器应如何配置响应头以实现安全访问。常见的误配置导致请求失败和潜在的安全风险，理解 CORS 对构建健壮的 API 很有帮助。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fosterelli.co/image/social.jpg' data-full='https://fosterelli.co/image/social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48578913' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48578913' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/NoamShazeer/status/2067400851438932297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Noam Shazeer Joins OpenAI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Noam Shazeer 加入 OpenAI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48578913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48578913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 341</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48578913' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Noam Shazeer, a prominent figure in AI, has joined OpenAI, marking another high-profile talent move among leading research labs. The hire signals OpenAI&#x27;s ongoing effort to attract top researchers to advance its language and AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>著名 AI 研究者 Noam Shazeer 加入 OpenAI，再次凸显顶尖实验室之间的人才竞逐。此举可能影响团队的研究方向与协作模式，也体现出对更强大语言模型的持续追求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1991028016471027714/bHMv9VwR_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1991028016471027714/bHMv9VwR_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48543311' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48543311' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mit.edu/2026/to-study-how-chips-really-work-mit-researchers-built-their-own-operating-system-0610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>To study how chips work, MIT researchers built their own operating system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为研究芯片工作原理，MIT 研究者自建操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48543311' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MIT researchers built a custom OS to observe how chips behave under controlled microarchitectural experiments, removing some vendor constraints. The project serves as a hands-on tool for hardware researchers to test hypotheses about instruction scheduling, caches, and I/O paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MIT 研究者为在受控条件下研究芯片微架构行为，自己从零搭建了一个操作系统，降低对厂商的依赖。该项目提供了一个直接的硬件研究工具，用于检验指令调度、缓存与 I/O 路径等假设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202605/mit-csail-fractal.jpg' data-full='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202605/mit-csail-fractal.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48583897' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48583897' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/modos-e-paper-monitor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modos Color Monitor Pushes E-Paper Displays Further</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Modos 彩色显示器推动电子纸显示屏进一步发展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48583897' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modos Color Monitor introduces a color e-paper display, pushing low-power, high-contrast visuals toward more practical use cases like dashboards and signage. The development widens the scope of e-paper beyond static text.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Modos 彩色显示器带来彩色电子纸，实现低功耗、对比度高的显示，拓展了在仪表板和标牌等场景的应用。电子纸的用途因此更加丰富。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66946972&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C275%2C0%2C975' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66946972&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C275%2C0%2C975' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48613755' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48613755' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260618-the-weird-and-wonderful-libraries-of-finland' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Renting a sewing machine from the library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从图书馆租借缝纫机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48613755' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Finland libraries are experimenting with lending tools beyond books, including sewing machines, reflecting a shift toward practical, community-serving services. The program highlights how libraries can extend access to everyday equipment and reduce barriers to making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>芬兰图书馆正在扩展借用物品的范围，包括缝纫机，体现图书馆向实用、社区服务型转变。此举显示图书馆如何提供日常用品的访问权，降低动手制造的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nssr23.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nssr23.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48583386' data-tags='Health,Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48583386' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kcl.ac.uk/news/hospitals-and-universities-repurposing-drugs-at-90-lower-cost' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hospitals and universities repurposing drugs at lower cost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>医院与高校以约90%成本进行药物再利用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48583386' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hospitals and universities are repurposing existing drugs to cut treatment costs by up to about 90%, enabling broader access and faster deployment of therapies. The approach raises regulatory and safety considerations, but could reshape drug affordability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>医院与高校通过对现有药物进行再利用，治疗成本大幅下降，降幅可达约 90%，从而提升治疗可及性。该做法涉及监管与安全考量，但可能改变药物的可负担性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.kcl.ac.uk/newimages/folsm/main-article/bmb/immunotherapy.x188e82a2.png' data-full='https://www.kcl.ac.uk/newimages/folsm/main-article/bmb/immunotherapy.x188e82a2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48544396' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48544396' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://typst.app/docs/changelog/0.15.0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Typst 0.15.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Typst 0.15.0 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48544396' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Typst 0.15.0 brings new features and fixes to this lightweight document typesetting system, enhancing typography controls and rendering performance. The update tightens alignment, fonts, and automation capabilities for writers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Typst 0.15.0 带来新特性和修复，提升排版控制与渲染性能，改进对齐、字体和自动化能力，方便作者更高效地排版。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://typst.app/assets/social.png' data-full='https://typst.app/assets/social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48540126' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48540126' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/06/15/salesforce-signs-definitive-agreement-to-acquire-fin/?bc=HL' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Salesforce to Acquire Fin (formerly Intercom) for $3.6B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Salesforce 将以 36 亿美元收购 Fin（前 Intercom）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48540126' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Salesforce is acquiring Fin, a customer messaging platform formerly known as Intercom, for $3.6B, a move to deepen its customer engagement stack. The deal signals continued consolidation in CRM and AI-assisted support tools, potentially reshaping competition with Zendesk and other tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Salesforce 将以36亿美元收购 Fin（前 Intercom），以加强其客户参与工具组合。这笔交易标志着 CRM 与 AI 辅助支持工具领域的持续整合，未来可能对 Zendesk 等竞争对手构成压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.salesforce.com/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/Option-02.png' data-full='https://www.salesforce.com/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/Option-02.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48556561' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://browser-use.com/posts/firecracker-browser-infra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we run Firecracker VMs inside EC2 and start browsers in less than 1s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 EC2 内运行 Firecracker 虚拟机并让浏览器在 1 秒内启动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48556561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48556561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48556561' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AWS Firecracker microVMs run inside EC2 to boot browsers in sub-second time, enabling rapid automated testing and headless workflows. The approach highlights how lightweight virtualization can drastically cut cold-start latency for browser-based tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 EC2 内使用 Firecracker 微型虚拟机实现子秒级的浏览器启动，显著提升自动化与无头测试的吞吐。此方案展示了轻量化虚拟化在降低冷启动延迟方面的潜力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为何与他人边说边想比独自思考更有效。文章指出对话能暴露隐私、拓展视角，并加速问题解决。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48606619' data-tags='Data,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48606619' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.timwehrle.de/blog/i-stored-a-website-in-a-favicon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Stored a Website in a Favicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把一个网站存进了 favicon</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48606619' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental piece shows you can encode a website&#x27;s data into a favicon and retrieve it later. It probes the limits of favicon-based storage, browser caching, and decoding, turning a tiny image into a surprisingly capable data channel. Practical uses are limited, but the exercise yields lessons about data density, security, and edge-case behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者通过把网站数据编码到 favicon 中并解码还原，展示了在极小的图标中也能承载大量信息的可能性。文章分析浏览器缓存、数据 URI 与解码的限制，以及这种非常规数据通道在实用性和安全性上的含义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://michaelenger.com/blog/i-love-the-computer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Love the Computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我爱电脑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48546441' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal meditation celebrating the beauty and power of computers, weaving hardware, software, and tinkering into a narrative about craft and curiosity. The piece resonates with engineers and hackers who still find joy in solving problems at the metal and code level, not just apps. It serves as a reminder that computing remains a hands-on pursuit with tangible feedback.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇个人随笔，赞美电脑的美与力量，讲述硬件、软件和动手改造如何汇聚成工艺与好奇心的故事。对于仍然沉浸于硬件与代码之解题乐趣的工程师与黑客，这篇文字有共鸣。它提醒人们，计算仍是一门需要动手实践、能获得直接反馈的领域。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48552844' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48552844' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tck.mn/blog/correlated-randomness-sts2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Correlated randomness in Slay the Spire 2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Slay the Spire 2中的相关随机性设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48552844' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Slay the Spire 2 adopts correlated randomness to balance player agency and fairness, ensuring strategic decisions matter while avoiding extreme luck twists. The design concept helps maintain challenge and variety as the game evolves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在Slay the Spire 2中实现相关随机性设计，以在玩法多样性和公平性之间取得平衡，确保玩家策略决策真正影响结果，同时避免运气极端波动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tck.mn/blog/correlated-randomness-sts2/crng_chart.png' data-full='https://tck.mn/blog/correlated-randomness-sts2/crng_chart.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48609385' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48609385' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.birminghammail.co.uk/news/midlands-news/vpn-ban-update-uk-households-34141063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VPN ban update for UK households as government looks at &#x27;age-gate&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国家庭VPN禁令更新：政府拟引入年龄门槛</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 346</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48609385' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UK households could see new VPN restrictions as the government weighs an age-gate policy for online content. The plan would push providers and networks toward age-verification requirements, potentially limiting access for minors and tightening enforcement. It reflects ongoing debates about child safety, privacy, and circumvention tools in the UK.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府正在考虑一项年龄门槛政策，可能让英国家庭在部分在线内容上受限 VPN 的使用。该方案或促使服务商和网络运营商在年龄验证上加强要求，甚至在网路层面实施访问限制。这反映出英国在隐私、访问自由与未成年人保护之间的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i2-prod.birminghammail.co.uk/article34141068.ece/ALTERNATES/s1200d/0_vpn.jpg' data-full='https://i2-prod.birminghammail.co.uk/article34141068.ece/ALTERNATES/s1200d/0_vpn.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600950' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48600950' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/new-bill-takes-aim-government-pressure-silence-lawful-online-speech' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new bill takes aim at government pressure to silence lawful online speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新法案瞄准政府压制合法在线言论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48600950' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The proposed bill would curb government pressure on platforms to remove lawful online speech, establishing clearer standards, transparency, and redress. It signals a legislative push to protect free expression online while still addressing harm online through targeted tools and accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新法案拟限制政府对平台施压，要求对合法在线言论的删除设定清晰标准并提高透明度和救济途径。此举旨在保护网络表达自由，同时为治理网络有害内容提供更有针对性的工具与问责机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/intermediary-4.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/intermediary-4.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48609343' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48609343' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.extremetech.com/computing/windows-11s-new-media-player-uses-35x-more-ram-charges-for-popular-video' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11 New Media Player Uses 3.5x More RAM, Charges for Popular Video Codecs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11 新媒体播放器占用更多 RAM，并对常用视频编解码器收费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 293</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48609343' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Windows 11 Media Player reportedly uses 3.5x more RAM than its predecessor and imposes fees for widely used video codecs. The shift raises concerns about licensing costs and resource bloat in the OS’s built-in media stack, especially for low-end machines and media workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 11 新媒体播放器据称比前代占用多出约3.5倍的内存，并对常用视频编解码器收费。这引发关于编解码器授权成本及内置媒介堆栈资源占用的担忧，尤其对低配设备的性能与工作流影响更显著。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.extremetech.com/imagery/content-types/01mGj7FyKT3gXkONbTRW5di/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.png' data-full='https://i.extremetech.com/imagery/content-types/01mGj7FyKT3gXkONbTRW5di/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48555838' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48555838' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://parksb.github.io/en/article/32.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>But yak shaving is fun (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>但修修补补也能很有趣</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 290</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48555838' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yak shaving—a sequence of small tasks before a larger goal—often gets a bad rap, but the piece argues it can be enjoyable and productive. It celebrates curiosity-driven incremental progress and how nailing small dependencies clears the path to bigger outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>修修补补这些看似琐碎的小任务，尽管被视为拖累，却也能带来乐趣和成就感。文章倡导好奇心驱动的渐进式进步，以及解决小依赖带来更大目标的路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://og-image.parksb.vercel.app/api/parksb-github-io?title=But%20yak%20shaving%20is%20fun&amp;subtitle=The%20joy%20of%20building%20from%20scratch&amp;date=2019-07-31' data-full='https://og-image.parksb.vercel.app/api/parksb-github-io?title=But%20yak%20shaving%20is%20fun&amp;subtitle=The%20joy%20of%20building%20from%20scratch&amp;date=2019-07-31' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cvgd5g7d7gyo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX Is Buying Cursor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX正在收购Cursor</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48554215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48554215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48554215' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX confirms it is buying Cursor, a move that underscores growing convergence between space tech and AI software. The deal may unlock synergies in data handling, simulation, and autonomous systems across the company&#x27;s lineup.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX确认正在收购Cursor，凸显太空技术与AI软件日益趋同。交易或将为数据处理、仿真与自动化系统带来协同效应。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48526633' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨形式化方法在编程实践、验证和软件工程未来中的作用，权衡严格性与实际可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48555244' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48555244' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5google.com/2026/06/15/google-chromes-next-update-will-mark-the-end-of-popular-ad-blockers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Chrome update will close the door on ad blockers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌 Chrome 更新将关闭对广告拦截插件的支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 364</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48555244' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome&#x27;s upcoming update tightens extension APIs, effectively phasing out most ad blockers by restricting how they can intercept or modify page content. The move could degrade ad-blocking effectiveness for users while developers adapt to new constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome的最新更新将收紧扩展API，通过限制插件拦截或修改网页内容的能力来削弱大多数广告拦截工具的效果，用户体验可能受影响，开发者需适应新约束。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5google.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/4/2024/05/chrome-logo-sign-2.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5google.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/4/2024/05/chrome-logo-sign-2.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48547062' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48547062' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gmalandrakis.com/writings/ad-economicum.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Peopleless economy? Not technically impossible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无人经济？并非技术上不可能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 559</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48547062' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay argues that a &#x27;peopleless&#x27; economy powered by automation and AI isn&#x27;t technically impossible, though it would require radical shifts in productivity measurement, wealth distribution, and governance. It surveys practical challenges—from consumer demand to ownership models—and suggests policy levers to enable high-automation prosperity without sidelining society.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇文章称由自动化和人工智能驱动的“无人经济”并非不可能，但需要在生产率衡量、财富分配和治理方面进行根本性转变。文中分析了从需求端到所有权模式等实际挑战，并提出在高自动化时代实现繁荣的政策杠杆。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48610827' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48610827' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smpte.org/blog/smpte-makes-its-standards-freely-accessible-openingstandards-library-to-the-global-media-technology-community' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SMPTE Makes Its Standards Freely Accessible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SMPTE 将标准免费开放访问，面向全球媒体技术社区</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48610827' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SMPTE has opened its standards library to the public, removing paywalls for its technical specs. The move should accelerate interoperability and innovation across media tech, from streaming pipelines to device certification. It lowers barriers for startups and researchers to build compliant products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SMPTE 将其技术标准库向全球开放获取，技术规范不再设有付费门槛。此举将促进媒体技术领域的互操作性与创新，覆盖从流媒体管线到设备认证的各个环节。降低初创公司和研究人员打造合规产品的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.smpte.org/hubfs/1920%20x%201080.jpg' data-full='https://www.smpte.org/hubfs/1920%20x%201080.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48550936' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48550936' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bobdahacker.com/blog/fifa-hack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Could&#x27;ve Rickrolled the FIFA World Cup. All I Needed Was My ID</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我几乎可以把 FIFA 世界杯变成 Rickroll 的。只需要我的 ID</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48550936' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ID-based vulnerability around a major sporting site allowed simple manipulation of content to surface a Rickroll, illustrating how predictable identifiers can enable unexpected redirects or access. The post underscores the risks of URL-level identifiers and highlights the need for robust authentication and access controls in large public-facing apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>仅凭一个 ID 就能在 FIFA 世界杯相关页面触发跳转到 Rickroll，暴露了基于 ID 的访问控制薄弱和可预测行为的风险。这说明在面向公众的应用中，需加强认证与访问控制，避免通过简单标识符被滥用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bobdahacker.com/static/images/blogs/fifa/blogHeader.webp' data-full='https://bobdahacker.com/static/images/blogs/fifa/blogHeader.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48582374' data-tags='Science,Data,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48582374' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.not-ship.com/can-you-see-three-trees/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can you see three trees?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能看到三棵树吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48582374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 279</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48582374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48582374' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece examines a visual puzzle that asks viewers to spot three trees, using it to explore perceptual biases and the limits of detection. It sheds light on how humans differ from AI vision systems in parsing ambiguous scenes, with implications for computer vision datasets and evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过一个让人想看看是否能在图像中识别出三棵树的视觉谜题，揭示感知偏差与察觉极限。讨论人类视觉与人工智能在解析模糊场景方面的差异，以及这对计算机视觉数据集和评估的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/f0/60/f060ef3f-d4e0-4af4-9255-1373fbf0a44f/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/featured.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/f0/60/f060ef3f-d4e0-4af4-9255-1373fbf0a44f/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/featured.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48592163' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48592163' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.modelcontextprotocol.io/posts/enterprise-managed-auth/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-Touch OAuth for MCP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MCP 的零触控 OAuth</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48592163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48592163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48592163' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zero-Touch OAuth for MCP introduces an enterprise-friendly OAuth flow that provisions tokens and apps with minimal human intervention. It emphasizes automated onboarding, device registration, and policy-driven access controls to reduce friction while preserving security. This approach helps large organizations deploy cloud-native apps faster without compromising governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>零触控 OAuth 为 MCP 提供面向企业的简化认证流程，尽量减少人工配置即可完成令牌和应用的注册。强调自动化的上线流程、设备注册与基于策略的访问控制，在降低运维成本的同时保持安全性。对于需要快速部署云原生应用的大型组织尤为重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.modelcontextprotocol.io/og-image.png' data-full='https://blog.modelcontextprotocol.io/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48549118' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48549118' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jxmo.io/p/zen-and-the-art-of-machine-learning' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zen and the Art of Machine Learning Research</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机器学习研究的禅意与艺术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48549118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48549118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48549118' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ML research benefits from simplicity, reproducibility, and deliberate iteration rather than hype. This piece argues for a mindful approach to experimentation, open data, and robust evaluation to advance reliable models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>机器学习研究应追求简洁、可重复性与有目的的迭代，而非追逐热度。文章主张用心对待实验设计、开放数据与稳健评估，以推动更可靠的模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gBx6!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6477dc42-dd38-4b92-8fa0-e4e6ab7f2bc1_1918x820.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gBx6!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6477dc42-dd38-4b92-8fa0-e4e6ab7f2bc1_1918x820.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48609766' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48609766' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neuviemeporte.github.io/f15-se2/2026/06/20/needyou.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOS Game &quot;F-15 Strike Eagle II&quot; reversing project needs DOS test pilots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DOS 游戏 F-15 Strike Eagle II 的逆向工程项目正在招募 DOS 测试玩家</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reversing project for the DOS game F-15 Strike Eagle II is seeking &#x27;DOS test pilots&#x27; to run tests in DOS environments. The effort aims to preserve the game&#x27;s behavior and game logic for preservation and historical study.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款 DOS 游戏 F-15 Strike Eagle II 的逆向工程项目正在招募‘DOS 测试员’，在 DOS 环境中进行测试以还原游戏行为与逻辑，用于保护与研究之用。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 终结已废弃的 strncpy API，历时多年并合计数百次补丁以降低安全风险，推动开发者使用更安全的替代方案。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48576824' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It highlights how backend model choice shapes how a robot behaves in dynamic scenarios and under pressure. The takeaway is that model selection materially affects practical robotics performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章评估在实时机器人控制中使用 Claude 与 Grok 的差异，权衡延迟、安全性与决策质量。它强调后端模型的选择会直接影响机器人在动态场景中的表现与鲁棒性。核心结论是，模型选择会实质性地影响实际的机器人性能。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that public institutions and platforms like W Social shape Europe’s digital sovereignty narrative, highlighting governance, data governance, and the performative politics around sovereignty. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨像 W Social 这样的平台与公共机构在欧洲数字主权话语中的角色，聚焦治理、数据主权以及主权话语背后的张力。作者指出表述往往掩盖了现实中的监管冲突与政治博弈。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netherlands launches a sovereign language model project to reduce reliance on foreign AIs, safeguarding data sovereignty and tailoring capabilities to Dutch language and policy needs. <p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative look at epoll and io_uring on Linux, examining how traditional event-driven I/O (epoll) stacks up against the newer, coroutine-friendly io_uring interface.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Linux 中的 Epoll 与 io_uring 进行对比，评估传统事件驱动 I/O（epoll）与新型、协程友好的 io_uring 的性能与复杂度。文章指出两者各自的适用场景和优势。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48602817' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece critiques proposals to require Real ID for all internet traffic, arguing it would enable pervasive surveillance and infringe civil liberties while complicating technical implementation. It emphasizes the risk of chilling effects and overreach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评将 Real ID 强制用于所有互联网流量的构想，认为这会带来广泛监控、侵害隐私并增加实施难度，同时可能产生寒蝉效应与过度集成。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48569205' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48569205' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rxi/microui' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MicroUI – A tiny, portable, immediate-mode UI library written in ANSI C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MicroUI：用 ANSI C 编写的微型、便携即时模式 UI 库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48569205' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MicroUI is a tiny, portable immediate-mode UI library written in ANSI C. Its small footprint makes it attractive for embedded and cross-platform projects, and immediate-mode style can simplify rendering loops and minimize state management. It&#x27;s a handy option for lightweight UI when dependencies matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MicroUI 是一个用 ANSI C 编写的微型、便携的即时模式 UI 库，体积极小、便于嵌入式和跨平台使用。即时模式的实现简化了渲染循环，降低了状态管理复杂性，适合对依赖要求很高的轻量 UI 场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/256299c45c9654065dd209907bd4a980e79de677072333b8d99043b7a9f5387f/rxi/microui' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/256299c45c9654065dd209907bd4a980e79de677072333b8d99043b7a9f5387f/rxi/microui' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48608570' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://townsquare.cauenapier.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: TownSquare, a tiny presence layer for websites</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：TownSquare——一个极简存在感层用于网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48608570' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TownSquare is a tiny, lightweight presence layer for websites, designed to let visitors feel a site&#x27;s community and activity without heavy components. It aims to be frictionless to deploy, enabling quick social presence cues for creators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TownSquare 是一个极简、轻量级的网站存在感层，帮助访客感知站点的社区与活动，而不需要繁重的前端组件。目标是便于部署，为创作者提供快速的社交存在感信息。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48507039' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A zine-style comic argues writing for one reader can sharpen voice, clarity, and empathy by tailoring language, tone, and structure to a single audience. It offers practical examples and prompts to practice concise, intimate prose.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一本漫画风格的专刊主张为单一读者而写能磨炼语气、表达清晰度与共情能力，强调用一个目标读者来定调语言、语气与结构。提供可操作的练习与示例，帮助练习简洁而贴近的写作。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48601286' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48601286' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-01947-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is AI ruining our skills? Early results are in – and they&#x27;re not good</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 会毁掉我们的技能吗？早期结果显示不乐观</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48601286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48601286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 314</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48601286' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early studies summarized by Nature suggest AI assistance can erode core skills when used as a crutch, from arithmetic to writing and coding. The findings underline a trade-off between productivity and skill retention, urging new approaches to education and assessment in an AI-heavy era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>早期研究表明，若过度依赖 AI 提供帮助，部分核心技能（如算术、写作、编程）可能退化。研究强调生产力与技能维持之间的权衡，促使教育与评估在 AI 化时代进行重新设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01947-1/d41586-026-01947-1_52722978.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-01947-1/d41586-026-01947-1_52722978.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how Lisp-inspired ideas shaped Ruby&#x27;s flexibility, metaprogramming culture, and a penchant for DSLs, showing that Lisp’s legacy lives on in modern dynamic languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解析 Lisp 传统中的理念如何影响 Ruby 的灵活性、元编程文化以及对领域特定语言的偏好，展示 Lisp 的遗产在现代动态语言中的延续。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wilsoniumite.com/2026/06/19/the-room-the-economy-cant-see/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The room the economy can&#x27;t see</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>经济看不见的角落</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 237</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48596911' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores parts of the economy that traditional metrics fail to capture, such as informal work and shadow activity, arguing for richer data to reveal the true economic landscape. It invites reconsideration of how we measure growth and well-being.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论传统经济统计无法覆盖的领域，如非正式就业和影子经济，呼吁使用更丰富的数据来描绘真实的经济格局。文章促使人们重新思考经济增长与福利的衡量方式。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述打造 AI 原生初创公司的创始人手册，聚焦数据策略、架构设计以及以 AI 能力驱动的快速迭代。强调平台思维、高效的 ML 工作流，以及与 AI 驱动产品节奏相匹配的激励机制。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Inkwash 是一款水彩风格的绘画应用，并附有设计解读。它追求在数字环境中提供直观、质感丰富的绘画体验，展示了以简洁界面实现高创造力工具的可能性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48608394' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/temporary-accounts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Temporary Cloudflare accounts for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 AI 代理提供的临时 Cloudflare 账户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48608394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare introduces temporary accounts designed for AI agents, with time-limited lifespans and scoped permissions. The feature lowers setup friction for automated agents while preserving governance and security controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出临时账户，供 AI 代理使用，账户按时限和权限范围进行管理。此举降低了自动化接入门槛，同时保持治理与安全边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/60PB1NmcYFywT5TDlwDR0v/a9e72c980e108702e00b57343ea1ccb2/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__39_.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/60PB1NmcYFywT5TDlwDR0v/a9e72c980e108702e00b57343ea1ccb2/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__39_.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48584261' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bostonglobe.com/2026/06/17/business/lyme-disease-tick-test/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>15-minute at-home Lyme disease tick test</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在家用 15 分钟的莱姆病蜱虫检测</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48584261' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 15-minute at-home test promises to help identify Lyme disease ticks, enabling rapid risk assessment without clinic visits. The development points to accessible, consumer-friendly approaches to vector-borne disease monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款 15 分钟的家用检测工具可帮助识别携带莱姆病蜱虫的风险，减少就诊负担。此类检测反映了向大众提供易用的向量传播疾病监测工具的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bostonglobe-prod.cdn.arcpublishing.com/resizer/v2/KQQIESDPSUJAFAGRI4AX4XZ36Y.jpg?auth=bd3849d7f6e13cf8d76517df8af41be054f801c65a38cb7ae28844015f11c64f&amp;width=1440' data-full='https://bostonglobe-prod.cdn.arcpublishing.com/resizer/v2/KQQIESDPSUJAFAGRI4AX4XZ36Y.jpg?auth=bd3849d7f6e13cf8d76517df8af41be054f801c65a38cb7ae28844015f11c64f&amp;width=1440' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48546616' data-tags='Business,Energy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48546616' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fred.stlouisfed.org/series/IPG33591S' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US battery manufacturing output continues to break records</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国电池制造产出持续刷新纪录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48546616' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US battery manufacturing output is hitting new records, driven by accelerating EV production and stationary storage demand. The surge signals a ramp-up of domestic gigafactories and supply-chain scale, with implications for jobs, prices, and national energy security. Continued capacity expansion will depend on critical materials, policy support, and industrial strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国电池制造产出达到新高，受到电动车和储能需求快速增长的推动。产出激增反映了国内巨型工厂投资和供应链扩张的影响，涉及就业、成本和国家能源安全。未来产能的持续扩张将取决于关键材料、政策支持等因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fred.stlouisfed.org/graph/fredgraph.png?width=880&amp;height=440&amp;id=IPG33591S' data-full='https://fred.stlouisfed.org/graph/fredgraph.png?width=880&amp;height=440&amp;id=IPG33591S' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48526661' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48526661' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.destroyallsoftware.com/talks/the-birth-and-death-of-javascript' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Birth and Death of JavaScript (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JavaScript 的诞生与消亡（2014）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48526661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48526661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48526661' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective talk tracing JavaScript&#x27;s origins, design compromises, and how it evolved into a ubiquitous web language despite early flaws. It highlights the discipline of navigating language design trade-offs and the surprising longevity of a language born from constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这场回顾演讲梳理了 JavaScript 的起源、设计取舍，以及它如何在早期缺陷的背景下发展成无处不在的网页语言，凸显在语言设计取舍中的执着与长期生命力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://storiedcolors.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Storied Colors – A catalogue of named colors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Storied Colors – 命名颜色大全</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48577374' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Storied Colors curates an extensive catalogue of named colors with hex values and historical notes. For designers and UI developers, it’s a handy reference for palettes and accessible naming. It helps standardize color naming across teams and projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Storied Colors 汇集了大量带十六进制值与历史注释的命名颜色。对设计师和前端开发者而言，它是调色板和可访问命名的实用参考。它有助于在团队与项目之间统一颜色命名。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48558338' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48558338' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fastcompany.com/91558427/ghost-jobs-could-soon-be-illegal-in-new-york' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Ghost jobs&#x27; could soon be illegal in New York</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约或将禁止幽灵岗位招聘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48558338' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York is poised to ban ghost jobs—posting fake or misleading openings to harvest resumes or data. The policy could push recruiters toward authentic postings and improve labor-market transparency. It may force job boards to tighten listing integrity and protect jobseekers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约有望立法禁止虚假招聘信息，以打击欺骗性招聘并保护求职者。若落地，或促使招聘方发布真实岗位、提高信息质量，同时给招聘平台带来更严格的列表监管挑战。这也可能改变求职者的决策过程和对职业机会的信任。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48610853' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48610853' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lispm.net/apps/uhf-x11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UHF X11: X11 Built for VisionOS and Apple Vision Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>UHF X11：为 VisionOS 与 Apple Vision Pro 构建的 X11 实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48610853' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UHF X11 is an X11 implementation built for VisionOS and Apple Vision Pro. It enables running X11 apps in a spatial AR/VR context, lowering the barrier to port legacy Linux/Unix software to immersive devices. This could broaden toolchains for developers targeting Vision Pro.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>UHF X11 是为 VisionOS 与 Apple Vision Pro 构建的 X11 实现。它使在空间化的 AR/VR 场景中运行 X11 应用成为可能，降低了将遗留的 Linux/Unix 软件迁移到沉浸式设备的门槛，为开发者构建 Vision Pro 的工具链带来可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.lispm.net/apps/uhf-x11/screenshot_1.jpeg' data-full='https://www.lispm.net/apps/uhf-x11/screenshot_1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48547566' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.goodinternetmagazine.com/why-i-email-complete-strangers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I email complete strangers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何给陌生人发邮件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48547566' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outreach to strangers can unlock unexpected opportunities, insights, and collaborations that aren’t visible through typical networks. The piece argues for purposeful, respectful emailing and offers practical tips for proposing value, setting boundaries, and building genuine connections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主动给陌生人发邮件可以开启意想不到的机会、洞见与合作，这些往往在常规网络中难以发现。文章主张有目的、尊重的邮件沟通，并提供提出价值、设定边界与建立真诚人际关系的实用要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.goodinternetmagazine.com/content/images/2026/06/logo.png' data-full='https://www.goodinternetmagazine.com/content/images/2026/06/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48565130' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://runtimewire.com/article/openai-leaked-financials-altman-compute-burn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaked OpenAI financials show $38.5B loss and compute burn</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泄露的 OpenAI 财务数据披露 385 亿美元亏损与算力消耗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48565130' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Leaked OpenAI financials reportedly show a massive loss and a heavy compute burn, illustrating the scale of investment required to train and operate leading AI models. The numbers fuel debates about profitability, funding, and the sustainability of aggressive compute growth. The leak sounds a warning about the economics of AI leadership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泄露的财务数据披露了 OpenAI 的巨额亏损与持续的算力烧钱，凸显维持领先地位所需的高昂投入。此类数字引发关于盈利能力、融资与大规模算力增长可持续性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://runtimewire.com/og/openai-leaked-financials-altman-compute-burn.jpg' data-full='https://runtimewire.com/og/openai-leaked-financials-altman-compute-burn.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48563609' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://writings.stephenwolfram.com/2026/06/launching-version-15-of-wolfram-language-mathematica-built-in-useful-ai-lots-of-new-core-functionality/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wolfram Language and Mathematica version 15</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wolfram Language 与 Mathematica 15：AI 助手、符号音乐等</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48563609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48563609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48563609' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wolfram Language and Mathematica 15 introduce a built-in AI assistant, expanded symbolic capabilities, and new features like symbolic music. The update aims to streamline computational workflows and broaden accessibility for researchers and students alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wolfram Language 与 Mathematica 15 增添了内置 AI 助手、扩展的符号计算能力以及符号音乐等新功能，目标是简化计算工作流并提升研究者与学生的可及性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48580686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48580686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48580686' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stable release marks Steam Deck&#x27;s software parity with Linux ecosystem; includes improvements to gaming compatibility, driver support, and performance. The update could impact indie devs and users relying on SteamOS for headless boxes.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worksinprogress.co/issue/how-madrid-built-its-metro-cheaply/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Madrid built its metro cheaply (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马德里地铁如何以低成本建成（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48575997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48575997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48575997' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study of Madrid&#x27;s 2024 metro project explains how the city achieved lower costs through procurement choices, engineering simplifications, and efficient project management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马德里2024年地铁项目的案例研究，阐释了通过采购策略、工程简化和高效项目管理实现低成本的做法。文中给出面向大型城市基础设施的实用经验。</p>
