</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48533756' data-tags='Politics,Culture,AI,Education,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48533756' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/maattttbrown/status/2066215255987163246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stanford grads walk out on Google CEO Sundar Pichai speech</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stanford graduates walked out during Sundar Pichai&#x27;s speech at Google. The protest highlights growing student activism around AI ethics, worker rights, and leadership direction in Silicon Valley.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福毕业生在谷歌 CEO Sundar Pichai 的演讲中集体离场。抗议凸显了硅谷日益活跃的学生行动，聚焦于 AI 伦理、员工权利和领导方向。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.kentbeck.com/p/hey-n00b-we-didnt-hire-you-to-complete' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hey, n00b, we didn&#x27;t hire you to complete tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>嘿，新手，我们不是让你把任务全做完的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48604851' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pragmatic note arguing against overloading junior developers with every task, stresses mentorship, sustainable pace, and healthy delegation. It advocates balancing hands-on work with learning and collaboration to maintain quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇简讯反对把每一项任务都推给新手，主张通过指导、合理分工和可持续的工作节奏来提高团队效率。强调团队应在动手编码与共同协作之间取得平衡，以维持代码质量与学习成长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hzhP!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Ftidyfirst.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1150222451%26version%3D9' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hzhP!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Ftidyfirst.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1150222451%26version%3D9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ns.nl/en/season-tickets/dal-vrij' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dutch Railways offers unlimited off-peak train travel nationwide for €49/month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰铁路推出49欧/月的全国非高峰期无限乘车</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48543872' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nederland&#x27;s NS unveils a nationwide off-peak unlimited travel pass for 49 euros per month. The plan aims to shift commuting and travel patterns toward off-peak times, potentially easing congestion and boosting productivity, while impacting fare structures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰国家铁路宣布推出全国范围的非高峰期无限乘车月票，价格为49欧元。此举旨在引导公众将出行转向非高峰时段，可能缓解高峰拥堵、提升工作效率，同时对铁路票价结构产生影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48576395' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://loreline.app/en/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Loreline – Tools for writing interactive fiction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Loreline – 用于创作互动小说的工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48576395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48576395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48576395' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Loreline provides tools to author interactive fiction, including branching narratives, worldbuilding, and AI-assisted drafting. It lowers the barrier to crafting non-linear stories and could accelerate indie storytelling or game development. The takeaway: better tooling for interactive narratives can unlock more creative storytelling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Loreline 提供创作互动小说的工具，涵盖分支叙事、世界观构建以及 AI 辅助写作。它降低了非线性故事创作的门槛，或加速独立作者和游戏开发的流程。核心点在于更强的互动叙事工具能释放更多创意。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://startupwiki.tech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: StartupWiki – A Free Alternative to Crunchbase</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>StartupWiki：Crunchbase 的免费替代品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48610224' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>StartupWiki offers a free, community-maintained alternative to Crunchbase for startup data. By lowering access costs and potentially opening data, it could democratize startup intelligence, though data quality and coverage may vary.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>StartupWiki 提供一个免费、由社区维护的创业公司数据替代 Crunchbase 的方案。降低获取成本、可能开放数据，有望让创业情报更具普及性，但数据质量与覆盖范围可能参差。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48537641' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Openrouter releases a Fusion API to orchestrate multiple models and tools in a single, cohesive interface, simplifying multi-model prompts and data routing. This lowers integration friction for developers building AI-powered apps that need to combine strengths of different systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Openrouter 发布了融合 API，用于在一个统一接口内编排多种模型和工具，简化多模型提示和数据路由。此举降低了将多种系统组合在一起构建 AI 应用的集成难度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48539078' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stratechery.com/2026/anthropics-safety-superpower/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s Safety Superpower</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的安全护城河</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48539078' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48539078' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48539078' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic bets big on safety as a product differentiator, building guardrails, red-teaming, and policy frameworks into its AI stack. The argument is that safety-focused design may become a sustainable moat as models scale and policy risk increases; enterprises will pay a premium for reliability and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 将安全性作为产品核心差异化，嵌入模型体系的守护机制、对抗性测试和治理框架。随着模型规模扩大和政策风险上升，安全设计可能成为企业愿意为之付费的护城河，优先考虑可靠性与治理。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48537512' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48537512' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/neuroscience/articles/10.3389/fnins.2026.1813281/full' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improvement in advanced Alzheimer’s disease following high-dose psilocybin</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高剂量 psilocybin 改善晚期阿尔茨海默病</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48537512' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A neuroscience study reports improved symptoms in advanced Alzheimer&#x27;s after high-dose psilocybin, contributing to the emerging research on psychedelics for neurodegenerative diseases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项神经科学研究报告称，在高剂量的 psilocybin 治疗后，晚期阿尔茨海默病患者的症状有所改善，为迷幻药在神经退行性疾病治疗领域的研究提供新证据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/27A044CC-44D1-4E5E-A886893A3FE656F4/A72D78D9-73CD-4C75-910FB7C0D04825C0/WebsiteWebP_XL-FNINS_Main Visual_Purple_Website.webp' data-full='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/27A044CC-44D1-4E5E-A886893A3FE656F4/A72D78D9-73CD-4C75-910FB7C0D04825C0/WebsiteWebP_XL-FNINS_Main Visual_Purple_Website.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Adam-CAD/CADAM' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Adam (YC W25) – Open-Source AI CAD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN：Adam（YC W25）——开源 AI CAD</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48572553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48572553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48572553' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adam is an open-source AI-powered CAD tool from YC&#x27;s W25 batch. It aims to bring AI-assisted design into standard CAD workflows, with transparency and community governance through open source. This could accelerate feature development and lower the bar for researchers and hobbyists to experiment with AI in design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Adam 是 YC W25 阵营推出的开源 AI CAD 工具，意在把 AI 辅助设计引入常用 CAD 工作流。开源带来的透明性与社区治理可能加速功能迭代，降低研究者和爱好者在设计中尝试 AI 的门槛。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vinibrasil.com/when-i-reject-ai-code-even-if-it-works/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When I reject AI code even if it works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>即使 AI 代码能工作，我也拒绝使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48614631' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues for rejecting AI-generated code even when it appears to work, citing concerns about reliability, maintainability, and hidden biases. It challenges the tendency to overtrust automated code in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张即使 AI 生成的代码能工作，也应拒绝采用，原因包括可靠性、可维护性以及隐藏偏见。该观点挑战了在生产环境中过度信任自动化代码的倾向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vinibrasil.com/og/when-i-reject-ai-code-even-if-it-works.png' data-full='https://vinibrasil.com/og/when-i-reject-ai-code-even-if-it-works.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48554814' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen releases a foundation-model suite tailored for embodied and physical-world tasks, covering perception, manipulation, and control components. The suite aims to accelerate robotics research with standardized models and benchmarks.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://taoofmac.com/space/blog/2026/06/18/1845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running MicroVMs in Proxmox VE, the Easy Way</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48599555' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proxmox VE now offers a straightforward path to deploy lightweight microVMs, enabling fast startup and strong isolation for microservices and test workloads. The approach lowers the barrier to experimenting with microVMs in a familiar virtualization environment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Proxmox VE 现在提供在同一环境中部署轻量级 MicroVM 的简易路径，带来快速启动和强隔离，适合微服务和测试负载。该方法降低了在熟悉的虚拟化环境中尝试 MicroVM 的门槛。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48529149' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Swiss voters rejected a proposal to cap the population at ten million, illustrating limits of regulation aimed at growth control. The decision affects long-term planning, immigration policy, and sustainability debates in a small, wealthy democracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士选民否决将人口上限设为一千万的提案，揭示了以增长控制为目标的法规的局限性。此举将影响这座小而富裕民主国家的长期规划、移民政策和可持续性辩论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jacc.org/doi/10.1016/j.jaccas.2025.104885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ice water drowning survival of young patient (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>冰水溺水后年轻患者存活病例（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48594592' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case report documents a young patient who survived drowning after ice-water immersion with a favorable neurological outcome. It reinforces that cold exposure can extend the window for resuscitation and underscores the importance of rapid CPR and high-quality post-resuscitation care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本病例报道记录了一名年轻溺水者在冰水中生还并获得良好神经功能结果。研究强调寒冷水体的保护性影响可能延长复苏窗口，并凸显快速高质量 CPR 与事后护理的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mickael.canouil.fr/posts/2026-06-15-gribouille-0-3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gribouille 0.3.0: A Grammar of Graphics for Typst</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48541062' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gribouille 0.3.0 adds a grammar-of-graphics approach to Typst, enabling declarative plotting inside documents. This expands Typst’s data visualization capabilities and makes it easier to produce consistent charts alongside text.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gribouille 0.3.0 为 Typst 引入图形语法，允许在文档中以声明式方式绘制图表。此举拓展了 Typst 的可视化能力，便于在文本中直接生成一致的图形。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>吃豆人：你来扮演鬼魂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48524135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48524135' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reimagines Pac-Man by letting players control the pursuing ghost, flipping the usual chase dynamics. It highlights how changing the player&#x27;s role can illuminate core tropes of classic arcade design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把吃豆人游戏反向设定成玩家扮演追逐者的幽灵，颠覆了传统的追逐规则。介绍了新的机制、关卡设计，以及这一改动对策略和跑分潜力的影响。通过改变玩家角色来揭示经典街机设计的核心要素。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>图像压缩</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48522927' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion of image compression techniques from basics to modern perceptual coding, outlining tradeoffs between lossless and lossy approaches and how compression affects bandwidth, storage, and image quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>图像压缩概述了从基础方法到现代感知编码的技术，阐述了无损与有损之间的权衡，以及压缩对带宽、存储和图像质量的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48567877' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48567877' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cells2pixels.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: High-Res Neural Cellular Automata</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：高分辨率神经元胞自动机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48567877' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN introduces a high-resolution neural cellular automata project that generates textures and images efficiently. The approach promises scalable, fast generative content suitable for graphics, simulations, and tooling. It signals a direction for fast, data-light content creation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了一个高分辨率的神经元胞自动机项目，能够高效生成纹理和图像。这种方法具备可扩展、低数据需求的产出能力，适用于图形、仿真和工具开发，指向快速生成内容的方向。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48605355' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48605355' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ianbarber.blog/2026/06/19/llms-are-complicated-now/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs Are Complicated Now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型现在更复杂了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48605355' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Large language models are no longer stand-alone engines; they sit inside broader toolchains with multiple models and plugins. This complexity raises new challenges in safety, evaluation, latency, and cost. Builders must rethink pipelines, guardrails, and monitoring to ship reliable AI products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大型语言模型已不再是孤立的引擎，而是嵌入到包含多个模型和插件的更大工具链中。这种复杂性带来安全、评估、延迟和成本的新挑战。开发者需要重新设计流水线、风险防控和监控，才能发布可靠的 AI 产品。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48613732' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.esquire.com/news-politics/politics/a71619211/peter-thiel-dialog-club-wired-report/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turns Out, There Is a Cabal of Elite Crazies Trying to Control the World</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>原来存在一群试图控制世界的精英疯子/势力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48613732' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigative reporting details a covert circle of tech elites, including Peter Thiel&#x27;s Dialog Club, alleged to push policy and influence media behind the scenes. The report argues such informal networks can shape global affairs with limited transparency and oversight. For technologists, it underscores why governance and accountability in elite circles matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查报道揭露了一群包括彼得·蒂尔对话会在内的科技精英构成的隐秘圈子，据称通过私下运作影响政策与媒体。该机构化的影响网络缺乏透明度和外部监督，可能左右全球事务。对于技术从业者，这提醒我们必须关注精英圈层的治理与问责。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ankursethi.com/links/nobody-clicks-your-share-buttons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;ve always wondered if anyone used sharing buttons on news sites and blogs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我一直在想，新闻站点和博客上的分享按钮到底有多少人会点击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48561332' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sharing buttons on news sites attract little engagement and may be more noise than signal. The piece argues publishers should rethink or remove them to declutter UI and focus on meaningful engagement metrics. It invites a broader look at how social signals influence reader behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新闻网站和博客的分享按钮几乎没有人点击，属于低信号的界面元素。建议发布者重新思考是否保留或改造按钮，以提升用户体验并将关注点放在更具实效的互动上。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48479957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48479957' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video documents a hands-on hack of a notoriously unreliable e-bike, where the creator analyzes the firmware, identifies a flaw, and patches a fix to improve reliability and safety. The takeaway is how low-level tinkering and open hardware knowledge can turn an underperforming device into something usable, highlighting the value of firmware transparency and repair culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个视频展示对一辆最差的电动自行车进行返修：分析固件、定位缺陷并实施修补，使其变得更可靠和安全。此事强调了底层动手改装、开放硬件知识在提升设备可用性与修复文化方面的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48485527' data-tags='Culture,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48485527' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/these-italian-teenagers-stayed-overnight-at-their-school-they-found-ancient-roman-ruins-hidden-in-the-basement-180988917/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teenagers Stayed Overnight at Their School and Found Hidden Ancient Roman Ruins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在学校过夜的青少年发现隐藏的古罗马遗址</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485527' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485527' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48485527' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Overnighting at their Italian school, teenagers discover hidden ancient Roman ruins in a basement, a serendipitous archaeological find. The story highlights how curious students can uncover surprising history in everyday spaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在意大利一所学校过夜时，青少年在地下室发现隐藏的古罗马遗址，成为一次意外的考古发现。报道强调好奇心驱使下，日常空间也可能揭示意想不到的历史。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/bmxPJJXSL_tdp4fdpSH6RSwWXjA=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(750x500:751x501)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/fb/27/fb27c648-c54c-45b1-80a6-37fa6e338efc/roman-villa.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/bmxPJJXSL_tdp4fdpSH6RSwWXjA=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(750x500:751x501)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/fb/27/fb27c648-c54c-45b1-80a6-37fa6e338efc/roman-villa.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48611083' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48611083' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/06/260617032207.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The ability to regrow body parts is dormant in mammals, not lost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>哺乳动物的再生能力其实是潜在的，并非已消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48611083' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show mammals possess latent regenerative potential across various tissues, not a complete loss of ability. By decoding the signals that awaken this dormant capacity, scientists hope to improve wound healing and regenerative medicine.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究表明哺乳动物在多种组织中仍然保留潜在的再生能力，并非完全失去。通过解码唤醒这一潜在能力的信号，科学家或将推动创伤修复和再生医学的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/human-regeneration.webp' data-full='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/human-regeneration.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/06/17/technology/anthropic-trump-administration-fable.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic employees accuse Trump administration of targeting them</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic employees allege targeted government scrutiny under the Trump administration, potentially chilling AI safety research and raising concerns about political interference. The report underscores how regulatory pressure can interact with cutting-edge tech work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 员工指控特朗普政府对公司研究人员进行针对性打压，可能影响 AI 安全研究的独立性并引发监管干预的担忧。报道凸显了监管压力对前沿科技工作的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://planetscale.com/blog/the-only-scalable-delete' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The only scalable delete in Postgres is DROP TABLE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres 中唯一可扩展的删除操作是 DROP TABLE</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48492822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48492822' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that deleting vast amounts of data in Postgres scales best with dropping tables rather than row-level DELETEs; discusses implications for archival strategies and schema design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出在 Postgres 中，海量数据的删除最具可扩展性的做法是 DROP TABLE，而非逐行 DELETE；并探讨归档与模式设计的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://planetscale.com/assets/the-only-scalable-delete-social-DZ748lqJ.png' data-full='https://planetscale.com/assets/the-only-scalable-delete-social-DZ748lqJ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48606271' data-tags='Security,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48606271' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.space.com/space-exploration/satellites/its-quite-a-bit-more-than-we-expected-satellite-reveals-immense-scale-of-gps-signal-tampering' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Satellite reveals immense scale of GPS signal tampering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卫星揭示 GPS 信号篡改的惊人规模</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48606271' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A satellite-based analysis shows GPS signal tampering at a much larger scale than previously thought, raising concerns about navigation integrity for aviation, maritime, and mobile devices. The piece underscores the need for robust PNT (positioning, navigation, timing) and potential encrypted alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>卫星数据揭示 GPS 信号篡改的规模远超先前认知，威胁航空、海上导航与日常设备的定位准确性。强调需要更具鲁棒性的定位导航时钟系统，并探讨替代方案或加密信号的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/QKugvqvJaYaG649YYKJCDE-2000-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/QKugvqvJaYaG649YYKJCDE-2000-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Manhoff Archives release color photos from a US diplomat in Stalin-era USSR, offering rare visuals of daily life, infrastructure, and political atmosphere under total surveillance. The collection enriches historical perspective on Cold War-era diplomacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>曼霍夫档案公布了美国外交官在斯大林时期苏联拍摄的彩色照片，罕见地呈现日常生活、基础设施与高度监控下的政治氛围，为冷战时期的外交语境提供新维度。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48562662' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48562662' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mattmahoney.net/dc/dce.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Data Compression Explained (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数据压缩解释（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48562662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48562662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48562662' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This classic explainer distills core ideas in data compression: entropy, coding, and models, with approachable examples. It contrasts practical algorithms like LZ and arithmetic coding and discusses the trade-offs between compression ratio, speed, and memory usage. The piece helps readers appreciate why compression remains a balance between theory and engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇经典文章梳理了数据压缩的核心原理，包括熵、编码方案和统计模型，并通过直观示例帮助读者理解。它对比了常用算法如 LZ 与算术编码，并讨论了压缩比、速度与内存开销之间的权衡，揭示了压缩领域的挑战与魅力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48521236' data-tags='Show HN,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48521236' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://traceapp.info' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Trace – Offline Mac meeting transcripts you can flag mid-call</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Trace – 离线 Mac 会议记录，可在通话中标记要点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48521236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48521236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48521236' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trace is a Mac app that records and transcribes meetings offline, with an in-call flagging feature to mark important moments for later review. This offline-first approach reduces data exposure and enables fast, local search without cloud processing—useful for privacy-conscious teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Trace 是一款在 Mac 上离线记录并转写会议的应用，通话中就可标记关键时刻便于日后回顾。离线优先的设计降低数据暴露风险，并支持本地快速搜索，适合注重隐私的团队。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48584891' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48584891' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.realtor.com/advice/rent/wages-in-america-are-too-low-for-the-30-rule-to-work-for-renters-anymore/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wages in America Are Too Low for the 30% Rule to Work for Renters Anymore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国工资太低，30% 租房规则再也无力实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 511</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48584891' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that current wages in America are insufficient for renters once the 30% housing-cost guideline is considered, given rising rents and slow wage growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着租金上涨和工资增速放缓，30% 的租房规则在美国已不再适用。文中讨论对家庭、政策及市场的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://na.rdcpix.com/547c3a236e55374e19032ea19c9fb046w-c3945827297srd_q80.jpg' data-full='https://na.rdcpix.com/547c3a236e55374e19032ea19c9fb046w-c3945827297srd_q80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48559083' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48559083' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gilesthomas.com/2026/06/10g-ethernet-switching-to-broadcom-sfp-plus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>10Gb/s Ethernet: switching to a Broadcom SFP+ module</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>10 Gb/s 以太网：切换至 Broadcom SFP+ 模块</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48559083' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Upgrading to a Broadcom SFP+ module enables 10Gbps Ethernet, with tradeoffs around compatibility, DAC/AOC cabling, and driver support. The note walks through practical considerations for scaling network throughput in labs or data centers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>切换至 Broadcom SFP+ 模块即可实现 10Gbps 以太网，需权衡兼容性、DAC/AOC 线缆以及驱动支持等因素，文中给出在实验室或数据中心提升网络吞吐的实操要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gilesthomas.com/images/favicons/web-app-manifest-512x512.png' data-full='https://www.gilesthomas.com/images/favicons/web-app-manifest-512x512.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48609373' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/ubisofts-co-founder-claude-guillemot-dies-plane-crash-2026-06-20/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ubisoft co-founder Claude Guillemot has died in a plane crash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>育碧联合创始人克洛德·吉耶莫在飞机失事中去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48609373' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Guillemot, a co-founder of Ubisoft, died in a plane crash, marking a significant loss for the studio and the broader European gaming community. His passing is a notable moment for the industry’s leadership and historical trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>育碧联合创始人克洛德·吉耶莫在飞机失事中去世。此事对公司及更广泛的欧洲游戏社区都是重大损失，也影响着行业的领袖地位与历史走向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48586329' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfdxlabs.com/research/2026/robotics-setup/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a robotics research setup that lives next to my desk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>放在桌边的机器人研究系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48586329' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact robotics research setup sits near the author’s desk, emphasizing modular hardware and accessible control software for rapid experimentation. The setup illustrates how small, affordable rigs can accelerate learning and prototype iteration in robotics research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>桌边的小型机器人研究系统强调模块化硬件和易用的控制软件，便于快速进行实验与迭代。此类桌旁工作流展示了在机器人研究中，用低成本设备加速学习与原型开发的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dfdxlabs.com/research/2026/robotics-setup/social.jpg' data-full='https://dfdxlabs.com/research/2026/robotics-setup/social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48615680' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48615680' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/articles/reliable-llm-bayer.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building reliable agentic AI systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>构建可靠的代理型 AI 系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48615680' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler lays out architectural patterns for reliable agentic AI systems, stressing audits, containment, and composable components to keep AI behavior predictable. The piece argues for robust testing and governance to avert brittle systems as AI agents take more initiative.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fowler 提出面向可控、可审计的代理型 AI 系统的架构模式，强调可 containment、可组合组件，以及对 AI 行为进行可预测性设计。文章主张通过严格测试与治理来在 AI 代理主动性提升时避免脆弱系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinfowler.com/articles/reliable-llm-bayer/card.png' data-full='https://martinfowler.com/articles/reliable-llm-bayer/card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48522316' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48522316' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sighack.com/post/getting-creative-with-perlin-noise-fields' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Getting Creative with Perlin Noise Fields</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Perlin 噪声场实现创意效果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48522316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48522316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48522316' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores inventive uses of Perlin noise fields for procedural visuals and simulations. The piece dives into layering noise, vector fields, and dynamic mappings to generate organic textures and complex patterns that are cheap to compute.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍在 Perlin 噪声场上的创新用法，聚焦于在程序化视觉和模拟中的应用。通过叠加不同尺度的噪声、构造向量场以及动态映射，实现有机纹理和丰富的运动模式，提升视觉质感且计算成本低。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://sighack.com/public/images/2018-01-03-getting-creative-with-perlin-noise-fields/example-4.png' data-full='http://sighack.com/public/images/2018-01-03-getting-creative-with-perlin-noise-fields/example-4.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48601162' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>John Jumper joins Anthropic, signaling a boost in AI safety talent and expertise at the startup. The move could influence research directions and safety culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>John Jumper 将加入 Anthropic，标志着该初创公司在 AI 安全领域的人才与专长的增强，可能影响研究方向与安全文化。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48544451' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48544451' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://roszigit.com/en/blog/timescaledb-compression-hypercore' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How TimescaleDB compresses time-series data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TimescaleDB 如何压缩时序数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48544451' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TimescaleDB compresses time-series data by applying columnar encoding, chunking, and selective compression policies to reduce storage without sacrificing query performance. The approach helps scale IoT and monitoring workloads, often dramatically lowering costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TimescaleDB 通过列式编码、分块和有选择的压缩策略来降低时序数据的存储需求，同时尽量保持查询性能。此做法有助于扩展物联网与监控场景的负载，通常显著降低成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://roszigit.com/images/blog/timescaledb-hypercore-post-1.webp' data-full='https://roszigit.com/images/blog/timescaledb-hypercore-post-1.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48552767' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A satirical MCSweeneys piece riffing on the job of explaining that humans built the pyramids, poking fun at experts who explain obvious things and the absurdity of bureaucratic yet earnest explanations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 MCSweeneys 的讽刺短文以“有人需要解释人类确实建造了金字塔”为梗，讽刺那些解释显而易见事实的专家，以及官僚式又认真的解释风格。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48581070' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores why the author dislikes traditional compilers and the friction around vendoring WebAssembly binaries, highlighting pain points in building and distributing low-level software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论作者为何不喜欢编译器，以及在为 WebAssembly 二进制包进行 vendoring 时遇到的痛点，聚焦底层软件构建与分发的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48496233' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48496233' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/jun/11/arbuscular-mycorrhizal-fungi-plant-life-climate-global-mapping-study' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Subterranean fungi networks more than 100 quadrillion km in length</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>地下真菌网络长度超过10^17公里</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48496233' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study maps an immense subterranean fungal network that connects trees across ecosystems, enabling nutrient exchange and signaling beyond visible roots. The findings highlight fungi’s pivotal role in forest health, carbon cycling, and ecological resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究绘制出巨量的地下真菌网络，连接不同生态系统中的树木，促进养分交换与信号传递，超越地上根系的影响。结果凸显真菌在森林健康、碳循环与生态韧性中的关键作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/c42f62a12e1d873861e272945c78a8c6770aa211/30_0_3103_2482/master/3103.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=46eca663313f4731468ce30153e5a4c7' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/c42f62a12e1d873861e272945c78a8c6770aa211/30_0_3103_2482/master/3103.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=46eca663313f4731468ce30153e5a4c7' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48616017' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48616017' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lcamtuf.substack.com/p/the-100000-whys-of-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 100k whys of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的十万个为什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48616017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48616017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48616017' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay surveys big questions about AI, from capabilities and data use to alignment, safety, and societal impact. It blends practical warnings with curiosity-driven questions, urging readers to think critically about AI deployment and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章梳理了关于 AI 的一系列核心问题，包括能力、数据使用、对齐、安全与社会影响。它在提供务实警示的同时提出大量探索性问题，鼓励读者对 AI 的落地应用与治理保持批判性思维。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48594715' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48594715' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsencrypt.status.io/#2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt had a higher error rate for 90 minutes today</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt 今日出现约90分钟的高错误率</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48594715' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt logged a 90-minute window of elevated error rates, potentially affecting certificate issuance and renewals for many sites. The incident highlights the fragility of critical PKI services and the importance of robust failover and monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt 出现约 90 分钟的错误高峰，可能影响网站的证书颁发与续期。事件揭示关键公钥基础设施的脆弱性以及对故障转移与监控的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.status.io/ZsUlrVAwD16p.png' data-full='https://image.status.io/ZsUlrVAwD16p.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48612098' data-tags='Hardware,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48612098' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/cpus/amd-will-reinstate-memory-encryption-on-ryzen-9000-cpus-through-a-bios-update-in-july-tsme-is-coming-back-after-valuable-community-feedback' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD will reinstate memory encryption on Ryzen 9000 CPUs via BIOS update in July</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD 将在7月通过 BIOS 更新重新在 Ryzen 9000 系列 CPU 上启用内存加密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48612098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48612098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48612098' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD will reintroduce memory encryption on Ryzen 9000 CPUs via a July BIOS update after community feedback. The move restores a security feature intended to protect sensitive data in memory, though it may demand a BIOS update and potential compatibility considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 将在7月通过 BIOS 更新重新在 Ryzen 9000 系列 CPU 上启用内存加密，并在社区反馈驱动下回归。此举提升内存数据的安全性，但可能需要用户更新 BIOS，并留意兼容性问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/yQxDP9b5mwjEYZ4Y37kD7X-1999-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/yQxDP9b5mwjEYZ4Y37kD7X-1999-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48532178' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48532178' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/ai-and-ml/2026/06/14/ai-is-code-and-cant-be-prompted-into-being-smarter/5254141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is code – and can&#x27;t be prompted into being smarter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 是代码，不能靠提示变得更聪明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48532178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48532178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48532178' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI systems are software, and prompting alone cannot create real intelligence gains; improvements require better models, data, and training. It warns against over-reliance on clever prompts and encourages a focus on architecture, safety, and compositional capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 AI 是软件，单靠提示无法真正提升智能水平；改进需改进模型、数据和训练方式。警示不要过分依赖“聪明的提示”，应关注架构、安全性及系统性能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/227602.jpg?imageId=227602&amp;x=0&amp;y=11.03&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=88.97&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=11.03&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=88.97&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/227602.jpg?imageId=227602&amp;x=0&amp;y=11.03&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=88.97&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=11.03&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=88.97&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48619831' data-tags='Programming,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48619831' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://norvig.com/lispy.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>(How to Write a (Lisp) Interpreter (In Python)) (2010)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Python 编写 Lisp 解释器（如何实现）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48619831' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This classic tutorial shows how to build a Lisp interpreter in Python, from tokenization to evaluation. It reveals core concepts like environments, recursion, and evaluation in a compact, approachable example.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇经典教程展示了如何用 Python 构建一个 Lisp 解释器，从词法分析到求值的完整实现。通过简洁的示例，揭示环境、递归与求值等解释器核心理念，便于入门学习。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48605301' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48605301' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/film/2026/jun/09/how-to-feed-a-dictator-film' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to feed a dictator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何喂养独裁者（电影）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48605301' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The film examines the politics of aid and diplomacy under authoritarian regimes, revealing tactics and ethics of feeding dictatorial leaders. It analyzes how international aid, bargaining, and public perception shape power, offering warnings for policymakers and development practitioners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>影片聚焦在专制政权下的援助与外交政治，揭示喂养独裁者背后的策略与伦理。通过分析国际援助、谈判与舆论对权力的影响，为决策者与发展工作者提供警示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/afebb33a9421a47895c038e7149fef640186392f/163_0_1350_1080/master/1350.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=eda676424b9e30d77e74f66b47ba0d60' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/afebb33a9421a47895c038e7149fef640186392f/163_0_1350_1080/master/1350.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=eda676424b9e30d77e74f66b47ba0d60' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48581489' data-tags='Politics,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48581489' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.acma.gov.au/sms-sender-id-register' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Australian Government to Require SMS/MMS Sender ID Registraion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>澳大利亚政府将要求短信/彩信发件人ID注册</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48581489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48581489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48581489' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Australia plans to require registration of SMS/MMS sender IDs to curb spoofing and abuse, with implications for telcos and developers who rely on messaging. The move aims to improve trust and reduce scams across mobile messaging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>澳大利亚政府将要求短信/彩信发件人ID注册，以抑制伪装和滥用现象，对电信运营商和依赖短信服务的企业将产生影响，提升用户信任。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48609194' data-tags='Security,Politics,Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48609194' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/19/encryption-spyware-and-now-mythos-history-shows-why-cyber-export-control-doesnt-work/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From PGP to Mythos: a brief history of export controls that didn&#x27;t stop anyone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 PGP 到 Mythos：一部未能阻止任何人的出口管制简史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48609194' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Export controls on encryption and surveillance tech have repeatedly failed to stop adoption or misuse. The story traces how advances—from PGP to Mythos—outpaced policy, revealing enforcement gaps and unintended consequences. It suggests policy-makers still face difficult trade-offs between security, innovation, and freedom of expression.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于加密与间谍软件的出口管制历史屡次未能阻止相关技术的扩散与滥用；从 PGP 到 Mythos 的演变暴露了执法难点与政策的滞后。这篇报道强调，政策制定者在安全、创新与言论自由之间仍面临艰难的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/donald-trump-open-mouth.jpg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/donald-trump-open-mouth.jpg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48535118' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48535118' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theslowburningfuse.wordpress.com/2026/06/14/the-rich-arent-your-role-models-theyre-your-oppressors/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The rich aren&#x27;t your role models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>富人不是你的榜样</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48535118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48535118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48535118' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical essay argues that admiring wealthy elites as role models misleads, advocating for alternative metrics of virtue and success and calling for structural change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇批判性文章认为把富人当作榜样是误导，提倡以其他标准衡量美德与成功，并呼吁进行结构性变革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theslowburningfuse.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-1.png' data-full='https://theslowburningfuse.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48602918' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48602918' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brooker.co.za/blog/2020/08/06/erlang.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Surprising economics of load-balanced systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>负载均衡系统的出人意料的经济学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48602918' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article reveals counterintuitive cost dynamics in load-balanced systems, where scaling, replication, and idle capacity interact in surprising ways. It discusses how architectural choices impact total cost of ownership and performance for distributed services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭示负载均衡系统中的意外成本动态，扩展、复制与空闲容量之间的相互作用出人意料。讨论了架构选择如何影响分布式服务的总体拥有成本与性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mbrooker-blog-images.s3.amazonaws.com/erlang_c_plot.png' data-full='https://mbrooker-blog-images.s3.amazonaws.com/erlang_c_plot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48549918' data-tags='AI,Business,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48549918' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://runtimewire.com/article/microsoft-github-aws-ai-capacity-crunch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft turns to AWS as GitHub faces AI capacity crunch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在GitHub AI容量吃紧时，微软转向AWS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48549918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48549918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48549918' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With GitHub&#x27;s AI capacity strained, Microsoft is turning to AWS to provision extra compute and keep Copilot and other AI features humming. The cross-cloud approach reflects broader industry realities around capacity, redundancy, and developer productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub的AI容量吃紧，微软正转向AWS以获取额外算力，确保Copilot等AI功能持续运行。跨云策略反映出行业在容量、冗余和开发者生产力方面的现实需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://runtimewire.com/og/microsoft-github-aws-ai-capacity-crunch.jpg' data-full='https://runtimewire.com/og/microsoft-github-aws-ai-capacity-crunch.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48544429' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48544429' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://paulbuchheit.blogspot.com/2010/02/if-your-product-is-great-it-doesnt-need.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If your product is Great, it doesn&#x27;t need to be Good (2010)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你的产品很棒，它就不需要是“好”（2010）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48544429' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Buchheit argues that truly great products can outshine ordinary polish and that teams should focus on delivering extraordinary value rather than chasing generic &#x27;good&#x27; improvements. The note offers a counterpoint to feature creep and highlights the importance of a clear product thesis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·布赫特认为真正出色的产品能超越普通的精修，团队应专注于提供非凡的价值，而非追逐泛泛的“好”的改进。这一观点对避免功能膨胀、坚持清晰的产品定位具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://resources.blogblog.com/img/icon18_edit_allbkg.gif' data-full='https://resources.blogblog.com/img/icon18_edit_allbkg.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48549628' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48549628' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/by-invitation/2026/06/15/humanity-isnt-ready-for-the-coming-intelligence-explosion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Humanity isn&#x27;t ready for the coming intelligence explosion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类尚未为来临的智能爆炸做好准备</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48549628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48549628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48549628' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative piece argues that society and institutions are unprepared for a rapid &#x27;intelligence explosion&#x27; driven by AI, warning of governance, workforce, and ethical blind spots. It calls for proactive policy, risk management, and adaptable institutions to keep pace with accelerating AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇有争议的文章警告，社会与机构尚未为快速的‘智能爆炸’做好准备，涉及治理、就业与伦理等盲点。文章倡导前瞻性政策和可适应的治理框架，以应对日益增强的人工智能能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48544637' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48544637' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-corps' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Corps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Corps（Claude 队）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48544637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48544637' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic announces Claude Corps, a scalable framework to manage multiple Claude agents with policy and governance controls for enterprises. The initiative aims to help teams coordinate AI copilots, enforce safety constraints, and scale AI-assisted workflows across organizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Corps，为企业提供可扩展的多代理管理框架，具备策略与治理控制，便于协调 AI 助手并对安全约束进行执行。该方案旨在帮助企业在组织内扩展 AI 辅助工作流，同时保障合规性与安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jun/18/datasette-apps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Datasette Apps: Host custom HTML applications inside Datasette</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Datasette Apps lets you embed custom HTML apps inside a Datasette instance, enabling richer data dashboards without leaving the data platform. This reflects a broader trend toward portable, in-platform tooling for data teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Datasette Apps 允许在 Datasette 实例中嵌入自定义 HTML 应用，提升数据仪表板的能力而无需离开数据平台。此举反映了以数据为中心的可移植网页工具的趋势。</p>
