<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48604232' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48604232' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Will programmers write more efficient code during the memory shortage?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内存短缺时，程序员会写出更高效的代码吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48604232' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The discussion surveys whether memory scarcity pushes developers toward more memory-efficient patterns, such as streaming data, in-place algorithms, and careful resource management. It weighs evidence from practice and experiments, noting that efficiency gains can trade readability or development speed for memory footprint.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论探讨在内存紧张的情形下，开发者是否会倾向于更高效的内存使用策略，如流式处理、就地算法和更谨慎的资源管理。分析来自实践的证据，指出优化可能以降低可读性与执行速度换取更小的内存占用为代价。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eandt.theiet.org/2026/06/18/norway-greenlights-world-s-first-full-scale-ship-tunnel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Norway greenlights first full-scale ship tunnel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>挪威批准全球首个全尺度船舶隧道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48596910' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Norway approves the first full-scale ship tunnel to help vessels avoid treacherous routes, potentially reducing weather risk and emissions. The project embodies a high-cost, high-impact infrastructure bet with climate and logistics implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>挪威批准全球首个全尺度的船舶隧道，帮助船只绕过恶劣海况，或降低运营风险与排放。此项目是高成本高回报的基础设施尝试，兼具气候与物流影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://redactive--drupal--eandt--public-files.s3.eu-west-2.amazonaws.com/s3fs-public/styles/uncropped_small/public/2026-06/web_Stad-Ship-Tunnel-Project_CREDIT_Norwegian-Coastal-Administration_Multiconsult_Link-Architecture.jpg?VersionId=yU4Uqw61lqXGUw1...kbkM3jVCxFVHoO&amp;itok=ocEn-Y43' data-full='https://redactive--drupal--eandt--public-files.s3.eu-west-2.amazonaws.com/s3fs-public/styles/uncropped_small/public/2026-06/web_Stad-Ship-Tunnel-Project_CREDIT_Norwegian-Coastal-Administration_Multiconsult_Link-Architecture.jpg?VersionId=yU4Uqw61lqXGUw1...kbkM3jVCxFVHoO&amp;itok=ocEn-Y43' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://garden-of-flowers.heikkilotvonen.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Garden of Flowers – an archive of pictorial typography before ASCII art</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Garden of Flowers——ASCII艺术前的 pictorial typography 档案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48550569' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Garden of Flowers preserves a historical archive of pictorial typography, capturing visual language that predates ASCII art. The archive serves as a resource for designers and researchers studying the evolution of text as imagery and the folk typography of early digital culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Garden of Flowers 收集整理了 ASCII 之前的图像排版的历史档案，记录了以文本为视觉元素的早期设计语言。对设计师和研究者而言，这是研究文字作为图像的演变与早期数字文化民间排版的重要资源。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.com/news/magazine/2026/06/20/south-korea-weapons-dealer-trump-00959559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The rise of South Korea’s weapons business</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48608515' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>South Korea’s weapons business is expanding, driven by defense exports and regional demand. The rise reshapes global arms markets and highlights Korea&#x27;s tech prowess in advanced materials, avionics, and precision manufacturing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>韩国的武器产业正在扩张，受到军事出口和区域需求推动。这一崛起重塑全球军火市场，凸显韩国在高端材料、航空电子和精密制造等领域的技术实力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>柯克兰的环岛设计旨在提升交通流畅度并降低事故风险，文章探讨了这种基础设施的利弊以及在城市中的实际效果。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48547969' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48547969' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://americanexpress.io/cell-based-architecture-for-resilient-payment-systems/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cell-based architecture for resilient payment systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于单元格的架构提升支付系统弹性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48547969' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cell-based architecture is presented as a path to fault isolation and resilient payments. By partitioning state and operations into independent cells, outages in one cell won’t cascade, enabling faster recovery and safer experimentation. The approach aligns with fintech’s demand for 24/7 uptime and scalable, compliant architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将系统分割为独立的单元格以实现故障隔离和支付系统弹性。通过对状态和操作进行细粒度划分，单个单元的故障不会蔓延，从而更快恢复并便于安全试验。这一思路符合金融科技对全天候可用性、可扩展性和合规性的要求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://americanexpress.io/_post_assets/cell-based-architecture-for-resilient-payment-systems/img/main.jpg' data-full='https://americanexpress.io/_post_assets/cell-based-architecture-for-resilient-payment-systems/img/main.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48616713' data-tags='Programming,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48616713' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/namgyaaal/avoxelgame' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 3D voxel game engine written in APL</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental 3D voxel engine implemented in the array-oriented language APL demonstrates how unconventional tools can model space succinctly. It&#x27;s not a practical game engine, but it highlights creative language design and how data-parallel reasoning can apply to graphics workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用 APL 实现的三维体素引擎是对极端语言在图形任务中潜力的有趣演示。它并非实用的游戏引擎，但凸显了语言设计的创造性，以及数据并行思维在图形工作负载中的潜在应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/i-used-sound-waves-to-make-espresso-it-could-cut-coffee-brewing-energy-use-by-75-284929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I used sound waves to make espresso. It could cut coffee‑brewing energy use by ¾</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用声波冲煮浓缩咖啡，或能把冲煮能耗降低约75%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48514843' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lab-scale demonstration uses acoustic waves to improve espresso extraction, potentially slashing brewing energy by up to 75%. The approach challenges conventional thermal methods and points to broader opportunities for energy-efficient food and beverage processing. Real-world practicality and consumer taste remain questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项实验性研究用声波辅助提取浓缩咖啡，理论上可将冲煮能耗降低多达75%。该方法挑战传统的热传导依赖，并提示在食品饮料加工领域的潜在节能机会。实际可用性与口味影响仍待验证。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48573435' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ghostinthedata.info/posts/2026/2026-06-13-human-connection-moat/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Competitive Moat That AI Can&#x27;t Replicate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 无法复制的竞争护城河</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48573435' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that the true moat AI cannot replicate is human connection and trust, which underpin networks, communities, and consumer loyalty. The piece suggests social capital remains a defensible advantage even as AI advances.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为人工智能难以复制的真正护城河在于人际连接和信任，这些社交资本支撑着网络、社区和用户忠诚。即便 AI 继续发展，这些非技术要素仍具有不可替代性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ghostinthedata.info/images/author/chris-hillman.png' data-full='https://ghostinthedata.info/images/author/chris-hillman.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cleantechnica.com/2026/06/16/shipping-freight-energy-fossil-cargo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fossil Fuels Are 40% of Freight Shipping Tonnage, but Half Its Fuel Use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>化石燃料占货运吨位的40%，却贡献一半燃料消耗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48619394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A revealing look at freight shipping energy use shows fossil fuels account for a substantial share of both tonnage and fuel consumption, underscoring efficiency gaps and decarbonization opportunities such as alternative fuels and wind-assisted propulsion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对货运能源使用的揭示性分析显示，化石燃料在吨位和燃料消耗中占比相当大，揭示了效率差距和可通过替代燃料、风力辅助推进等方式实现的脱碳机会。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>距最初提出已有二十多年后，文章认为这八大谬论仍在现代分布式系统中占据影响力，影响工程师对延迟、分区和可靠性的判断。并给出更新的示例与应对思路，帮助构建更具弹性的云原生架构。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48610841' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48610841' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/oven-sh/WebKit/pull/249' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bun has an open PR adding shared-memory threads to JavaScriptCore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bun 在 JavaScriptCore 中为共享内存线程打开了 PR</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48610841' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bun, a web runtime, submitted an open PR to add shared-memory threading to JavaScriptCore. If merged, this could enable parallel execution across cores, potentially boosting performance for CPU-bound workloads and altering the WebKit/Bun ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun 这个网络运行时向 JavaScriptCore 提交了一个开放的 PR，旨在增加共享内存线程支持。若合并，可能实现跨核心的并行执行，提升 CPU 密集型工作负载的性能，并影响 WebKit 与 Bun 的生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7f905d915e01398962324a4ff1a6bc9365bc7bf50f9fc7b84d05a365c338aae7/oven-sh/WebKit/pull/249' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7f905d915e01398962324a4ff1a6bc9365bc7bf50f9fc7b84d05a365c338aae7/oven-sh/WebKit/pull/249' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48593093' hidden>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48543392' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48543392' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kobzol.github.io/rust/2026/06/15/how-memory-safety-cves-differ-between-rust-and-c-cpp.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How memory safety CVEs differ between Rust and C/C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 与 C/C++ 的内存安全 CVE 差异</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48543392' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Memory-safety CVEs reveal different risk profiles between Rust and C/C++. Rust&#x27;s ownership and safer abstractions reduce many classes of vulnerabilities, while C/C++ remain susceptible to use-after-free and unsafe dereferences; the post highlights practical implications for choosing languages in security-sensitive projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内存安全漏洞展示了 Rust 与 C/C++ 在风险类型上的差异。Rust 的所有权与更安全的抽象降低了大量漏洞风险，而 C/C++ 仍易出现如使用后释放等问题；这对在安全敏感项目中选用语言具有实际意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/images/icons/emoji/unicode/1f606.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/images/icons/emoji/unicode/1f606.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48538833' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48538833' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/themartiano/luz' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I wrote a C++ ray tracer from scratch without AI</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48619140' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48619140' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevinak.se/blog/who-actually-owns-your-atproto-identity-hint-its-probably-not-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who owns your ATProto identity?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的 ATProto 身份归谁？线索：很可能不是你</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48619140' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ATProto identity is not owned by the user; it&#x27;s controlled by the homeserver and the identity provider, with data portability and trust considerations. The piece explains ownership implications, how portability across servers is limited, and why users should care about server trust and data access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ATProto 身份并非由用户直接掌控，而是由宿主服务器及身份提供方控制，数据移植性与信任问题并存。文中分析了所有权的含义、跨服务器迁移的局限，以及用户为何需要关注服务器信任和数据访问权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wsrv.nl/?url=https://cdn.satellite.earth/982124c8576aeb2bcddeec69f0bec1eb4e93d601318113272791c9f817ab5430.png' data-full='https://wsrv.nl/?url=https://cdn.satellite.earth/982124c8576aeb2bcddeec69f0bec1eb4e93d601318113272791c9f817ab5430.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48541014' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe explores training frontier AI models using compute it owns, touching on sovereignty, data governance, and talent investment. The effort signals strategic interest in reducing dependence on US-based hyperscalers for critical AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲探索用自己掌握的算力训练前沿 AI 模型，涉及主权、数据治理与人才投资等议题，显示在关键 AI 能力上降低对美资云厂商依赖的战略意图。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48616173' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent Windows UI tweaks aim to make handling files without a default app less painful, offering clearer prompts and quicker options to pick an app. These changes reflect a broader focus on UX resiliency when dealing with file associations across OS versions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 的用户界面改进正在减轻在没有默认应用的情况下点击文件时的痛点，提供更清晰的提示和快速选择应用的选项。这些改动体现了操作系统在文件关联方面的用户体验韧性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tester.army' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: TesterArmy (YC P26) – Agents that test web and mobile apps</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48586299' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TesterArmy provides autonomous agents that simulate real-user interactions to test web and mobile apps, enabling scalable, continuous QA. By combining AI with automation, it aims to catch flaky tests and regressions earlier in development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TesterArmy 提供可自主运行的代理，模拟用户行为来测试网页和移动应用，支持持续规模化的质量保证。通过将 AI 与自动化结合，旨在更早发现不稳定测试与回归问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zeux.io/2026/06/17/zigzag-decoding-avx512/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zigzag Decoding with AVX-512</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 AVX-512 的 Zigzag 解码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48573902' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A vectorized zigzag decoding routine built with AVX-512 delivers meaningful speedups over scalar code, demonstrating how wide SIMD can accelerate data-reordering steps in codecs. The post emphasizes careful data layout and alignment to squeeze throughput.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于 AVX-512 的 Zigzag 解码实现对标量版本实现了显著加速，展示了宽 SIMD 如何提升编解码中数据重排步骤的吞吐量。文中强调通过对齐和数据布局来最大化向量化性能。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗要求经霍尔木兹海峡航行的船舶投保，未来可能征收费用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48604584' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iran now requires mandatory insurance for ships transiting the Strait of Hormuz, which could raise shipping costs and reshape insurance markets. The policy signals tighter controls and potential ripple effects across global trade and vessel routing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗如今要求经霍尔木兹海峡航行的船舶投保，可能提高运费并影响保险市场。该政策体现对航运活动更严格的监管以及全球贸易的潜在连锁反应。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>裸机启动 Linux</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48543269' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A minimalist approach to booting Linux, stripping away optional initramfs or init-time dependencies to showcase a lean, fast startup path. The project emphasizes educational value and potential security benefits of a naked-boot approach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以极简方式启动 Linux，去除可选的 initramfs 与初始化依赖，展示一种更精简、启动更快的路径。该项目强调此类裸机启动在教育与潜在安全方面的价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shadycharacters.co.uk/2026/06/ollie-wagner/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An interview with an Apple emoji designer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一次对苹果表情符号设计师的访谈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48519723' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ollie Wagner discusses the constraints and philosophy behind Apple emoji design, revealing how designers balance consistency, expressiveness, and cross-platform compatibility. The interview sheds light on a tiny but influential design language.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ollie Wagner 讨论苹果表情符号设计背后的约束与理念，揭示设计师如何在保持一致性、表达力与跨平台兼容性之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shadycharacters.co.uk/image/3xLrx816hd-619.jpeg' data-full='https://shadycharacters.co.uk/image/3xLrx816hd-619.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48620462' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48620462' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openjsf.org/blog/burnout-is-real-for-open-source-maintainers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Burnout is real for open source maintainers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源维护者的倦怠问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620462' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores burnout among open-source maintainers driven by sustained workload, volunteer governance, and funding gaps. The piece advocates sustainable funding models, better recognition, and governance practices to keep OSS healthy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨开源维护者因长期工作量、志愿治理与资金短缺引发的倦怠。呼吁建立可持续的资助模式、改进对维护者的认可，以及更稳健的治理做法以维持 OSS 健康。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.prismic.io/openjsf/acRGrpGXnQHGY-L1_BlogPostImagesOpenJS-2-.png?auto=format,compress' data-full='https://images.prismic.io/openjsf/acRGrpGXnQHGY-L1_BlogPostImagesOpenJS-2-.png?auto=format,compress' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48548975' data-tags='Business,DevOps,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48548975' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.narracomm.com/amazon-announces-multibillion-dollar-data-center-in-missouri/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon Announces Multibillion-Dollar Data Center in Missouri</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊将在密苏里州建立数十亿美元数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48548975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48548975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48548975' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A multi-billion-dollar Amazon data center project in Missouri signals ongoing cloud and compute expansion, with implications for regional energy infrastructure, jobs, and connectivity. The investment points to continued demand for hyperscale capacity as developers migrate workloads to the cloud and as edge-like regions seek closer proximity to users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊在密苏里州宣布建设数十亿美元级别的数据中心，显示云计算与大规模计算基础设施的持续扩张。此举将影响当地能源、就业和网络连通性，并反映出对雾计算式近端服务需求的增长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.narracomm.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/unnamed.jpg' data-full='https://www.narracomm.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/unnamed.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48617782' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48617782' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexkritchevsky.com/2024/02/28/geometric-algebra.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case against geometric algebra (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反对几何代数的论证（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48617782' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical take on geometric algebra argues it offers limited practical advantages over conventional vector calculus and can complicate understanding. The piece weighs its claimed unifying power against real-world adoption in physics and engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对几何代数的质疑指出，它在实际应用中相较传统向量微积分并无明显优势，反而可能增加理解难度。文章权衡其所宣称的统一性与在物理和工程领域的实际应用证据。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48582180' data-tags='AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48582180' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adewynter.github.io/notes/aoe2-circuits' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Perceptron in Age of Empires II</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在《帝国时代 II》中实现感知机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48582180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48582180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48582180' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post shows how a simple perceptron can be mapped into the game’s logic circuits, effectively simulating a tiny neural net inside AoE2 setups. It discusses wiring, training, and limitations, illustrating how classic ML concepts can be demonstrated in a game environment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章演示如何将一个简单的感知机映射到游戏的电路逻辑中，在《帝国时代 II》的设定里模拟一个小型神经网络。内容涵盖连线、训练与局限性，展示了经典机器学习概念如何在游戏环境中得到直观体现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://adewynter.github.io/notes/img/nand_gate_running.gif' data-full='https://adewynter.github.io/notes/img/nand_gate_running.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48557768' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48557768' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reflex.dev/blog/why-ast-walk-when-you-can-ast-sprint/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making ast.walk 220x Faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 ast.walk 提速 220 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48557768' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A performance-focused post shows replacing costly AST walks with a faster approach, achieving up to a 220x speedup. It illustrates how targeted tooling and optimized traversal strategies can dramatically accelerate static analysis workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过改用更高效的 AST 遍历方法，实现高达 220 倍的速度提升。该文展示了为特定任务设计的遍历工具如何显著加速静态分析工作流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://web.reflex-assets.dev/blog/why-ast-walk-when-you-can-ast-sprint.webp' data-full='https://web.reflex-assets.dev/blog/why-ast-walk-when-you-can-ast-sprint.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48551876' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48551876' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260611-00/?p=112415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding the rationale behind a rule when trying to circumvent it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尝试绕过规则时理解背后原因</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48551876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48551876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48551876' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains why people try to circumvent rules and what that reveals about usability and policy design. It argues that robust systems anticipate common workarounds and provide safer, clearer alternatives rather than simply blocking actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解析人们为何试图绕过规则，以及这对可用性和政策设计的启示。作者主张，健全的系统应预见常见的变通方式，提供更安全、清晰的替代路径，而非单纯封锁行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48568652' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48568652' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ibm.com/think/news/discovery-changed-how-scientists-think-about-memory-kavli-prize' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The discovery that changed how scientists think about memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>改变科学家记忆观的发现（IBM）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48568652' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Kavli Prize-recognized finding reshapes how scientists view memory, suggesting memory relies on distributed networks rather than isolated storage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项获得 Kavli 奖的发现改变了科学家对记忆的理解，提示记忆可能分散在大脑网络中而非单一存储单元。它对神经科学、衰老研究和受 AI 启发的记忆模型具有深远影响，推动对记忆障碍的新策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ibm.com/content/dam/worldwide-content/stock-assets/adb-stk/ul/g/70/de/adobestock_714367383.jpeg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' data-full='https://www.ibm.com/content/dam/worldwide-content/stock-assets/adb-stk/ul/g/70/de/adobestock_714367383.jpeg/_jcr_content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48611942' data-tags='Data,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48611942' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clickhouse.com/blog/postgresbench' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PostgresBench: A Reproducible Benchmark for Postgres Services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgresBench：可复现的 Postgres 服务基准测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48611942' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public benchmark suite PostgresBench offers a reproducible framework to compare Postgres deployments across services and environments. It standardizes workloads and metrics, making it easier to isolate performance differences and optimize configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgresBench 提供一个可复现的基准框架，用于在不同服务和环境中对 Postgres 部署进行对比。它标准化工作负载和指标，便于定位性能差异并优化配置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clickhouse.com/_next/image?url=%2Fuploads%2Fpostgres_bench_banner_48e7472b90.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=80' data-full='https://clickhouse.com/_next/image?url=%2Fuploads%2Fpostgres_bench_banner_48e7472b90.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=80' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48608591' data-tags='Open Source,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48608591' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lrt.lt/en/news-in-english/19/2965205/lithuanian-startup-launches-open-source-network-to-detect-shahed-type-drones' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lithuanian startup launches open-source network to detect Shahed-type drones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>立陶宛初创公司推出开源网络以检测 Shahed 型无人机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48608591' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Lithuanian startup launches an open-source network to detect Shahed-type drones, inviting researchers and communities to contribute sensors and detections. It exemplifies crowdsourced defense tech and open standards in unmanned systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>立陶宛一家初创企业推出开源网络，用于检测 Shahed 型无人机，鼓励社区共享传感器和检测数据。这体现了以开源和众包方式提升无人机监测能力的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.lrt.lt/img/2026/05/25/2398157-937080-1287x836.jpg' data-full='https://www.lrt.lt/img/2026/05/25/2398157-937080-1287x836.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48536660' data-tags='Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48536660' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2022/03/a-brief-tour-of-the-pdp-11-the-most-influential-minicomputer-of-all-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A brief tour of the PDP-11, the most influential minicomputer of all time (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 PDP-11 的简要走访：有史以来最具影响力的微型计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48536660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48536660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48536660' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PDP-11 is highlighted as one of the most influential minicomputers, shaping Unix, operating systems design, and computer culture of its era. The tour covers its architecture, era, and enduring retrocomputing appeal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PDP-11 被视为最具影响力的微型计算机之一，深刻影响了 Unix、操作系统设计与当时的计算机文化。文章回顾了其架构、时代背景及至今的复古计算魅力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2021/10/Digital_PDP11-IMG_1498_cropped-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2021/10/Digital_PDP11-IMG_1498_cropped-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48548612' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48548612' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(287) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.plover.com/math/egyptian-fractions.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Egyptian Fractions (2006)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃及分数：将分数表示为单位分数的历史与方法</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对埃及分数进行数学探讨，将有理数表示为互不相同的单位分数之和，追溯历史背景、简单算法及与数论的关系。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cohere.com/blog/north-mini-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cohere&#x27;s First Model for Developers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cohere 为开发者推出首个模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48489934' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cohere launches its first developer-friendly model, designed to simplify access to powerful NLP capabilities via straightforward APIs and pricing. The move signals a shift toward more accessible, hosted LLM solutions for builders who want to integrate language capabilities without managing large models themselves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cohere 推出面向开发者的首个模型，提供易用的 API 与定价，降低接入强大自然语言处理能力的门槛。这一举措反映了更广泛的趋势：让开发者在不自建大模型的情况下即可嵌入语言能力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.elastic.co/search-labs/blog/agent-memory-elasticsearch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We built a persistent agent memory layer on Elasticsearch with 0.89 recall</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48583703' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Elastic builds a persistent agent memory layer atop Elasticsearch to improve long-term context retention and recall in AI agents. The approach demonstrates practical memory-augmented search and automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Elastic 在 Elasticsearch 上构建了一个持久化的代理记忆层，提升 AI 代理的长期上下文保留与召回能力，展示了记忆增强的搜索与自动化的实际应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.johnnovak.net/2016/09/21/what-every-coder-should-know-about-gamma/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What every coder should know about gamma (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每个程序员都应该知道的伽玛校正</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48521925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48521925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48521925' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gamma correction is essential for accurate color rendering in images and displays, yet it&#x27;s often misunderstood in software. Understanding these concepts prevents subtle visual bugs in apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伽玛校正是图像和显示器色彩正确呈现的关键，但在工程实践中常被误解。文章讲解伽玛曲线、sRGB 等色彩空间的直觉，以及感知线性的重要性，帮助开发者避免界面和图形管线中的颜色失真。理解要点有助于避免应用中的细微视觉错误。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://ernesernesto.github.io/writes/portingmatchmorphosistowasm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ported my C game to WASM, here&#x27;s every bug that I hit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把我的 C 游戏移植到 WASM：遇到的所有问题</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical tour of porting a C game to WebAssembly, cataloging design gotchas and debugging woes—from memory layout and linking to browser I/O and performance traps. The post offers concrete tips for developers considering WASM for existing C projects and highlights gaps between native and web runtimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一段将 C 游戏移植到 WebAssembly 的实用笔记，记录从内存布局、链接、浏览器 IO 到性能陷阱等一系列要点和调试难题。为考虑将现有 C 项目转到 WASM 的开发者提供了具体建议，并指出了原生与网页环境之间的差距。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48602571' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/1d37cc08-e0aa-45a4-a45d-4ad282529314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Companies rein in AI usage as costs strain budgets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成本压力下，企业收紧AI的使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48602571' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As budgets tighten, companies are dialing back AI investments, favoring cheaper, more focused deployments and closer vendor scrutiny. The trend may slow innovation and broaden reliance on existing tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在预算紧缩之际，企业收紧AI投入，偏向成本更低、聚焦的部署，并加强对供应商的审查。这一趋势可能放缓创新并加深对现有工具的依赖。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48489163' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fata applies spaced repetition to counteract skill rot from heavy reliance on AI coding assistants. The project suggests a productivity approach that blends AI-assisted coding with deliberate practice to retain real proficiency.</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620342' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reframes product scope toward selling the smallest units of software rather than chasing a traditional MVP. This helps with pricing, packaging, and avoiding feature creep, guiding teams to ship tangible value sooner.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把产品范围聚焦在可销售的最小单位，而不是追求传统 MVP。这样有助于定价、包装、避免功能堆叠，并促使团队尽快交付可验证的价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://brandur.org/assets/images/minimum-viable-unit/twitter@2x.jpg' data-full='https://brandur.org/assets/images/minimum-viable-unit/twitter@2x.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mindgard.ai/blog/chatgpt-spontaneously-generated-violent-images-from-a-viral-prompt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT&#x27;s image generator can be manipulated to produce violent, sexual content</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 图像生成器可能被操纵生成暴力/性内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48578894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48578894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48578894' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security research shows prompts or prompts-based workflows can coerce image generators to produce disallowed content, highlighting risks and the need for robust content moderation in multimodal AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现 ChatGPT 的图像生成器可通过特定提示生成暴力或性内容，暴露多模态 AI 内容过滤的风险，强调加强安全措施的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TerraPower 与 Meta 就建设八座 Natrium 345 MW 的先进核电厂达成协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48586648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48586648' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TerraPower and Meta strike a deal to deploy eight Natrium 345 MW advanced nuclear plants, signaling renewed interest in compact, safe nuclear power for data centers and large-scale computing. The collaboration hints at how tech ecosystems may accelerate clean baseload capacity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TerraPower 与 Meta 就部署八座 Natrium 345 MW 的先进核电厂达成协议，表明对紧凑且安全的核电在数据中心与大规模计算中的兴趣回升。此举揭示科技生态系统可能推动清洁基础电力的扩展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sfgate.com/tech/article/sundar-pichai-stanford-commencement-22304888.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Around 200 Stanford students walk out as Google CEO takes stage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯坦福学生在谷歌CEO登台时约200人退场抗议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48545260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48545260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48545260' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Around 200 Stanford students staged a walkout as Google&#x27;s CEO took the stage at a campus event, signaling student concerns over the company&#x27;s AI ethics, data practices, or labor issues. The moment underscores growing student activism at top universities around tech giants and the broader debate over responsible AI deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在谷歌 CEO 登台的斯坦福毕业典礼现场，约有200名学生退场示威，表达对公司在 AI 伦理、劳动与数据等议题的关注。这一事件凸显科技巨头在高校中的学生行动力，以及关于负责任的 AI 部署的广泛辩论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sfgate.com/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' data-full='https://www.sfgate.com/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48583407' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48583407' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://konradreiche.com/blog/excessive-nil-pointer-checks-in-go/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Excessive nil pointer checks in Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 语言中过度 nil 指针检查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48583407' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that overusing nil-pointer checks in Go can bloat code and obscure logic, recommending a balance between defensive checks and relying on proper nil-safe patterns or error handling. Practical tips include leveraging early returns and natural zero values to reduce noise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在 Go 语言中过度使用 nil 指针检查会让代码臃肿、降低可读性，建议在防御性检查和使用正确的 nil 安全模式或错误处理之间取得平衡。实用建议包括提前返回、利用零值自然避免冗余检查等。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Trinket.io 将停运，社区将部分项目保存并迁移至新域名以继续使用。此举保留了大量教育性代码示例和交互式教程，凸显社区在学习工具保护方面的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fractalkitty.com/there-is-3-such-that/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There Is(Ǝ) – Such That (∋)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>There Is(Ǝ) – Such That (∋)</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48490649' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how existential quantification and such-that notation appear in logic, offering intuition for how constraints shape reasoning in algorithms and proofs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨存在量词（There exists）与“使得”符号在逻辑中的互动，结合例子直观展示约束如何影响算法与证明中的推理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/1b/2e/1b2e7dbb-ac74-48f3-b18d-86f3e8bdb643/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/te-st.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/1b/2e/1b2e7dbb-ac74-48f3-b18d-86f3e8bdb643/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/te-st.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48587619' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spotlights Commodore&#x27;s retro-styled &#x27;Callback 8020&#x27; flip phone pitched as a digital detox device with modern capabilities. It exemplifies nostalgia-driven hardware that emphasizes simplicity over feature bloat.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/DatanoiseTV/ember-hackernews' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Ember, a native iOS Hacker News reader I built around accessibility</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ember：我为无障碍设计打造的原生 iOS Hacker News 阅读器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48610816' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ember is a native iOS Hacker News reader focused on accessibility. It prioritizes VoiceOver compatibility, large tap targets, and clean reading modes, showing how accessible-first design can broaden who can consume tech news on mobile.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ember 是一款原生 iOS 的 Hacker News 阅读器，聚焦无障碍体验。它优先考虑屏幕阅读器兼容性、易点击区域和简洁的阅读模式，展示了以无障碍为先的设计如何让更多人访问科技新闻。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48520596' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48520596' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/programmes/w3ct8k70' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why does paper fold so well?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纸为何能折叠得这么好？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48520596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48520596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48520596' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An explainer delves into the physics of paper folding, showing how thickness, crease propagation, and layering govern how reliably a crease holds and how many folds you can make. The takeaway for makers is that geometry and material properties, not just technique, determine how a sheet behaves when folded.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一则科普解说纸张折叠的物理原理，揭示厚度、皱褶传播与层叠等因素如何影响折痕的稳定性与可折叠次数。对创作者而言，关键在于几何结构与材料特性共同决定折叠时的表现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/images/ic/1200x675/p0nrcq42.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/images/ic/1200x675/p0nrcq42.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48503509' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48503509' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.trailofbits.com/2026/06/12/factoring-short-sleeve-rsa-keys-with-polynomials/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Factoring &quot;short-sleeve&quot; RSA keys with polynomials</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用多项式分解短小 RSA 密钥</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48503509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48503509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48503509' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A recent post demonstrates factoring RSA keys with polynomial techniques, highlighting vulnerabilities when keys are insufficiently sized or poorly generated. The result underscores the importance of appropriate key sizes and safe parameter choices to prevent practical attacks. It serves as a reminder that cryptography remains highly dependent on careful implementation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究演示了利用多项式方法分解 RSA 密钥，暴露在密钥尺寸不足或参数生成不当时的脆弱性。此结果强调了选取合适密钥长度与安全参数的重要性，以及对现代密码学安全实践的持续警示。提醒开发者在实现中保持谨慎。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.trailofbits.com/2026/06/12/factoring-short-sleeve-rsa-keys-with-polynomials/shortsleevekeys_figure1.png' data-full='https://blog.trailofbits.com/2026/06/12/factoring-short-sleeve-rsa-keys-with-polynomials/shortsleevekeys_figure1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48531449' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48531449' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tumbleweed.nu/r/lm-3/uv/amber.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chaosnet (1981)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chaosnet（1981年）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48531449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48531449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48531449' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chaosnet was an early campus network and a before-TCP/IP experiment in network design. This 1981 lookback highlights its protocol choices, goals, and why such projects shaped how researchers thought about interoperability and distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chaosnet 是早期校园网络的一次 TCP/IP 之前的实验。本文回顾 1981 年的设计要点、目标及其如何影响研究者对互联互通与分布式系统的思考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apertvs.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apertus – Open Foundation Model for Sovereign AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apertus：面向主权 AI 的开放基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622778' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apertus unveils an open foundation model designed for sovereign AI deployment with data sovereignty baked in. It supports open weights and configurable safety controls to reduce dependence on centralized providers, enabling in-country hosting and governance. This matters for governments and organizations seeking transparency and autonomy in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apertus 发布一个开放的基础模型，支持主权 AI 的部署与数据自主管控。提供开源权重和可配置的安全策略，旨在降低对大型厂商的依赖，使其能在本地或受监管环境中运行。此举对政府与重视数据主权与透明度的机构尤为重要。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hbr.org/2026/06/ai-has-broken-hiring-heres-how-to-fix-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The early hiring funnel is now breaking on both ends</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620142' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-driven changes are disrupting the early hiring funnel, creating bottlenecks on both applicant and assessment sides. The article proposes remedies like structured screening, improved candidate experience, and closer human–machine collaboration to restore flow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 驱动的变革正在扰乱早期招聘漏斗，造成申请端与评估端的瓶颈。文章建议通过结构化筛选、提升候选人体验，以及加强人机协作等方式来恢复招聘流程的畅通。</p>

But How Many Sloppy Ones?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>七次完整洗牌就能实现充分乱序？但还要多少次粗糙的洗牌才够？</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seven perfect shuffles suffice to randomize a deck; the article questions how many sloppy, imperfect shuffles are needed to achieve true randomness, using entropy and probability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>七次完整洗牌就能实现充分乱序？文章用熵与概率探讨需要多少次‘不完美’洗牌才能达到真正的随机性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ironicsans.ghost.io/the-color-strike/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 1969 camera operators&#x27; strike created Upstairs Downstairs multiverse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1969 年摄影机操作员罢工催生的 Upstairs Downstairs 平行宇宙</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48559729' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>During a 1969 strike by camera operators, TV production color handling diverged, effectively creating multiple visual variants of Upstairs, Downstairs. The piece uses this historical quirk to illustrate how production constraints can shape archival color and viewer perception, producing a kind of &#x27;color multiverse&#x27; in classic television.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1969 年摄影机操作员罢工影响了电视制作，导致 Upstairs, Downstairs 出现了不同版本的色彩处理，形成视觉上的‘平行宇宙’。文章借此历史趣事揭示制作约束如何影响档案色彩与观众感知，以及色彩在电视史中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/tv-and-radio/2026/jun/16/alternative-3-mockumentary-missing-scientists-conspiracy-50-years-later' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Wow, it really worked &#x27;: 70s TV show causing worldwide panic today</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 1977 mockumentary Alternative 3 is trending again, with people misinterpreting it as real and panic spreading worldwide decades after its release. The piece explains how media hoaxes can resurface and influence public perception.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>70 年代伪纪录片《Alternative 3》再次走热，因部分人误以为其为真实事件而在全球引发恐慌。文章解析媒体骗局如何反复出现、影响公众认知。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sogen.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sogen – High-performance Windows and Linux userspace emulator</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 72</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48537481' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sogen offers a high-performance userspace emulator that runs on Windows and Linux, enabling cross-OS testing without full virtualization. It emphasizes speed and low overhead, making it easier to test software across architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sogen 提供一款在 Windows 与 Linux 上运行的高性能用户态模拟器，便于在不进行完整虚拟化的情况下进行跨操作系统的测试。该项目强调速度和低开销，便于在不同架构上测试软件。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(338) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/16/doj-claims-xais-unpermitted-gas-turbines-are-a-matter-of-national-economic-and-energy-security/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOJ claims xAI&#x27;s gas turbines are a matter of &#x27;national and energy security&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>司法部称 xAI 的燃气涡轮机事关国家与能源安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48565429' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The DOJ positions xAI&#x27;s gas turbines as critical to national and energy security, signaling potential regulatory and export-control actions around high-end AI infrastructure. This framing suggests hardware used to power large AI models could become a focal point for policy enforcement and compliance requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>司法部将 xAI 的燃气涡轮机定位为关乎国家与能源安全的关键资产，预示可能加强对高端 AI 基础设施的出口管制与监管行动。这一表态凸显用于支撑大规模 AI 模型的硬件正成为政策执行的重点对象，企业需关注合规与风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/GettyImages-2217198328.jpeg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/GettyImages-2217198328.jpeg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

It’s a pragmatic dive into user-space filesystem development for hobbyists and learners.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/uks-new-under-16-social-media-ban-will-cause-more-harm-it-prevents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The UK&#x27;s new under-16 social media ban will cause more harm than it prevents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国未满16岁社媒禁令将弊大于利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48603761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48603761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48603761' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Britain plans to block or restrict social media access for under-16s, with platforms bearing penalties for non-compliance. Critics warn the policy may push teens toward unregulated spaces, undermining safety benefits and raising privacy concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国拟对未满16岁的用户实施社交媒体访问限制，违规平台将面临处罚。批评者认为此举可能促使青少年转向未监管的区域，削弱安全效益并引发隐私问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ageverification-banner2-3a.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ageverification-banner2-3a.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理式资源发现规范</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48573268' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new specification for agentic resource discovery outlines how autonomous agents should locate and reference resources safely and efficiently. It aims to standardize interactions, improving interoperability and reducing risk in multi-agent systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份代理式资源发现的新规范提出了自治代理如何安全、高效地定位和引用资源的标准做法，旨在提升跨系统的互操作性并降低多代理环境的风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48614311' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48614311' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/project-fetch-phase-two' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Fetch: Phase Two</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fetch 项目：第二阶段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48614311' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Project Fetch Phase Two expands research into alignment and capability safety, detailing new experiments, evaluation methods, and how to make AI systems more robust and controllable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fetch 项目第二阶段扩展了对对齐与能力安全的研究，介绍了新的实验、评估方法，以及如何让 AI 系统更稳健、可控。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/oraziorillo/microcrad' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Microcrad – Micrograd Reimplemented in C</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48570364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48570364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48570364' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microcrad is a C reimplementation of Micrograd, a tiny autograd library. The project demonstrates portability and performance benefits of a low-level autodiff toolkit and invites comparisons with Python-based originals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Microcrad 是对 Micrograd 的 C 语言重新实现，展示了低级自动微分工具的可移植性与性能潜力，并与原有的 Python 实现进行对比。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(344) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://flickey.site' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Made a free macOS menu bar app that fixes typing in the wrong keyboard layout</a></p>
