</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48575878' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A free macOS menu-bar utility helps correct typing when the active keyboard layout mismatches the expected layout, reducing ergonomic friction for multilingual users. It’s lightweight, easy to install, and saves time on workflow interruptions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个免费的 macOS 菜单栏小工具，能在键盘布局错配时自动纠正输入，减少多语言用户的工作中断和切换成本。体积小、易安装，提升工作流流畅度。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48566727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48566727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48566727' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay reframes Chesterton’s Fence as Chesterton’s Gap, urging readers to question whether a precautionary rule should block progress or enable deliberate experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章把 Chesterton 的 Fence 重塑为 Gap，主张在保守规则与创新之间做出有意的试验，不应让恐惧阻止前进。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48548883' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://ishmeetbindra.com/posts/reviews-have-become-expensive-rewrites-have-become-cheap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reviews have become expensive, rewrites have become cheap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>评论变得昂贵，改写变得便宜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48548883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48548883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48548883' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise typology of how modern editing workflows shift heavy burden from reviewers to rewrite steps, noting that while human reviews are expensive, content rewrites—often aided by AI—are becoming cheap and fast.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出编辑与审核的成本正在向前移：人工评审成本高，而在 AI 辅助下的改写却越来越便宜、迅速。它探讨了对出版、软件开发与质量控制的影响，并呼吁在人工智能时代重新设计评审流程。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48527946' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48527946' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ponytail – make your AI agent think like the laziest senior dev in the room</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ponytail：让 AI 助手像最懒的资深开发者一样思考</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48527946' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ponytail is a tool to steer AI agents toward the pragmatic, low-effort reasoning style of a senior developer, emphasizing efficient prompts and sensible defaults. It aims to improve reliability and speed of AI-assisted coding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ponytail 是一个工具，帮助 AI 代理模仿资深开发者的务实、低成本思维方式，强调高效提示与合理默认。目标是提升 AI 辅助编码的可靠性与速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1266797999/b20d9cb8-11f0-4db8-965a-7e22ab2b6bb6' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1266797999/b20d9cb8-11f0-4db8-965a-7e22ab2b6bb6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48571811' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48571811' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/evanklem/polypore' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agentic coding deserves more than a chat box bolted onto VS Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理式编码不应只是挂在 VS Code 上的一个聊天框</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48571811' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agentic coding argues for more than a chatbot embedded in VS Code. It advocates building autonomous AI agents that actively assist coding tasks, with better UX, reliability, and governance to avoid brittle, chat-only interfaces. The idea pushes for a richer integration of agency in development tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代理式编码的主张不仅限于在 VS Code 中嵌入一个聊天机器人，而是让 AI 代理真实参与编码工作，并提升用户体验、可靠性与治理，避免只靠聊天界面的脆弱性。这一思路推动开发工具更充分地集成“代理能力”。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c009bd883cb6b722c1c8a9eb8720baa5aff951b32eebca746a2bb5dbf0ab453c/evanklem/polypore' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c009bd883cb6b722c1c8a9eb8720baa5aff951b32eebca746a2bb5dbf0ab453c/evanklem/polypore' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A veterinarian-turned-founder launches an AI-powered lawn diagnosis product, illustrating how domain expertise can spawn niche ML startups. The story covers the pivot, product development, and early lessons in customer validation.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48565326' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48565326' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Semiclassical Gravity Efficiently Solves NP-Complete Problems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>半经典引力高效解决 NP-完全问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48565326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48565326' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A theoretical paper claims a semiclassical gravity framework can efficiently solve NP-complete problems, challenging conventional views on computational hardness. The claim invites scrutiny and replication in the physics and CS communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇理论论文声称半经典引力框架能高效解决 NP-完全问题，这一主张挑战了传统的计算难度观。该结论需要在物理学与计算机科学界进一步评估和复现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48579013' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48579013' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/jun/17/hpv-jabs-reduce-risk-dying-cervical-cancer-before-30-zero-study-finds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HPV jabs cut risk of dying from cervical cancer before 30 to almost zero</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HPV 疫苗显著降低30岁前宫颈癌死亡风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48579013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48579013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48579013' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New study shows HPV vaccination before age 30 dramatically lowers risk of death from cervical cancer, approaching near-zero. The result reinforces the public health value of early vaccination and supports policies to boost coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究显示30岁前接种 HPV 疫苗能显著降低因宫颈癌死亡的风险，风险接近零。这一结果进一步强调早期疫苗接种的公共卫生价值，并支持提升覆盖率的政策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/685f41829c749a62194ae60d91f61656c5aed27c/293_0_2915_2333/master/2915.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=02cd8c8f5812565b5ce68c4cfedef5d2' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/685f41829c749a62194ae60d91f61656c5aed27c/293_0_2915_2333/master/2915.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=02cd8c8f5812565b5ce68c4cfedef5d2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：通过在打印中注入环来强化 Z 轴，成本低且易于实施，能减少晃动、提高刚性，适合爱好者尝试。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622626' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622626' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexkritchevsky.com/2026/05/25/everything-is-logarithms.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything Is Logarithms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一切都是对数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622626' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many real-world relationships reveal linear structure when plotted on a log scale, making comparisons across orders of magnitude practical. The piece connects this idea to data visualization, ML, and decision-making, arguing that the right scale reveals hidden patterns. Embracing logarithmic thinking can sharpen models and dashboards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>许多现实世界的关系在对数坐标下呈现线性，使跨数量级的比较更加实用。本文将这一观念与数据可视化、机器学习和决策制定联系起来，强调选择合适的尺度能揭示隐藏的模式。掌握对数思维有助于提升建模与仪表盘的清晰度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(363) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/YusufB5/ASCILINE' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asciline – real-time ASCII video rendering engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asciline：实时 ASCII 视频渲染引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48499993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48499993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48499993' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asciline is a real-time ASCII video renderer that converts video frames into ASCII art on the fly, enabling lightweight, terminal-friendly previews or retro aesthetics. The project demonstrates efficient streaming and clever text-based rendering techniques that can run with minimal dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asciline 是一个实时的 ASCII 视频渲染引擎，能够在传输过程中将视频帧转换成 ASCII 艺术，适合轻量级终端预览和复古风格。该项目展示了高效流式处理和文本渲染技术，依赖极少。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48568181' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48568181' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexwlchan.net/2021/slashes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A tale of two path separators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两个路径分隔符的故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48568181' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece contrasts Windows-style backslashes with POSIX forward slashes, highlighting pitfalls in cross‑platform path handling and the importance of using standard library path utilities to normalize paths. It also touches on historical and design reasons behind the divergence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对 Windows 的反斜杠和 POSIX 的正斜杠进行了对照，指出跨平台路径处理中的坑点以及使用标准库路径工具进行归一化的重要性，并简要解释分歧背后的历史与设计原因。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexwlchan.net/images/cards/2021/slashes.png' data-full='https://alexwlchan.net/images/cards/2021/slashes.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48561402' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48561402' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://words.filippo.io/frood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Frood, an Alpine Initramfs NAS (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Frood：一个基于 Alpine 的 Initramfs NAS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48561402' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Frood is a compact NAS built as an Alpine-based initramfs image, aiming for quick boot and a tiny footprint. It showcases how initramfs can underpin lightweight, portable NAS deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Frood 是一个基于 Alpine 的 Initramfs NAS，以极小镜像实现快速启动和轻量运行，便于便携部署。它展示了用 initramfs 构建轻量级网络存储的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/94d90085-c14a-46c6-860c-fc8c0418d33a.jpeg' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/94d90085-c14a-46c6-860c-fc8c0418d33a.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The TD4 4-bit CPU guide walks through a tiny processor design, including instruction set basics and construction hints for hobbyists. It serves as a hands-on primer on how simple CPUs operate and why architectural decisions matter even at small scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TD4 是一个4位处理器：本指南介绍基础指令集、架构要点与爱好者搭建的实用建议。通过动手实践，帮助读者理解简单 CPU 的工作原理，以及为何小型规模的架构决策也很关键。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://david.newgas.net/did-my-old-job-only-exist-because-of-fraud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Did my old job only exist because of fraud?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的旧工作是不是只是因为欺诈而存在？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622867' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questioning whether some roles exist due to misaligned incentives or fraud, the piece explores the fragility of certain job markets and the potential for automation. It advocates for transparency, better verification, and data-driven evaluation of roles rather than anecdotal narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑某些岗位是否因激励错位或欺诈而存在，探讨了部分职业市场的脆弱性与自动化的潜在影响。倡导提高透明度、强化核验，并以数据驱动来评估岗位，而非仅凭主观叙述。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Commodore 推出翻盖手机，将复古外形与现代硬件结合，面向爱好者与收藏者。该机型借助怀旧品牌，试图在现今市场复兴对该类硬件的兴趣。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48550465' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wheresyoured.at/exclusive-openai-financials/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI Losses Increased Nearly 8X in 2025, with Spending Hitting $34B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 损失在 2025 年近增八倍，支出达 340 亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48550465' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI&#x27;s financials indicate losses surged nearly eightfold in 2025, with expenditure around $34 billion as the company scales compute, talent, and R&amp;D. The numbers underscore the high cost of building leading AI systems and the ongoing pressure to monetize via products, partnerships, or enterprise offerings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开财报显示，OpenAI 2025 年亏损近八倍，支出约 340 亿美元，反映出在计算资源、人才与研发上的巨额投入。数字凸显打造前沿 AI 系统的高成本，以及通过产品化、伙伴关系等方式实现盈利的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/24/d8/24d8fcec-dfba-4f40-a467-823d2941ae46/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/24/d8/24d8fcec-dfba-4f40-a467-823d2941ae46/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48564480' data-tags='Science,Culture,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48564480' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-cold-wars-accidental-whale-observatory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Cold War&#x27;s Accidental Whale Observatory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>冷战时期的意外鲸鱼观测站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48564480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48564480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48564480' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Long-baseline acoustic arrays built to track Soviet submarines accidentally yielded whale data, enabling scientists to study migrations and acoustics. This shows how military infrastructure can spur unexpected scientific insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>冷战时期用于追踪苏联潜艇的声学阵列意外为鲸类研究提供了数据，推动了对鲸群迁徙和声学行为的理解。这展示了军事基础设施在科学研究中的意外价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Drafted 提供面向住宅建筑的模型，帮助建立 AI 辅助的设计工作流程。这体现了将 AI 应用于住宅设计与规划的日益增长的兴趣和潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/06/20/world/live-news/iran-war-trump-israel-lebanon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran says it&#x27;s closing Strait of Hormuz, accusing Israel, US of violating truce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗宣布关闭霍尔木兹海峡，指控以色列与美国违反停火</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48610591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48610591' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iran claims to close the Strait of Hormuz and accuses Israel and the US of violating a truce. The move risks disrupting global oil shipments and heightens regional tensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗宣布关闭霍尔木兹海峡，指控以色列与美国违反停火。此举将增加全球原油运输中断的风险，并加剧区域紧张局势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/2026-06-19t081319z-152177580-rc2twlarzwm4-rtrmadp-3-iran-crisis-oman-hormuz.JPG?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/2026-06-19t081319z-152177580-rc2twlarzwm4-rtrmadp-3-iran-crisis-oman-hormuz.JPG?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dancemagazine.com/pointe-shoe-innovation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why has the pointe shoe been so resistant to change?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 pointe 鞋在变革上如此迟缓？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48605310' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece explains why pointe shoes have resisted modernisation, highlighting safety concerns, tradition, and manufacturing constraints that slow adoption of new designs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解释了 pointe 鞋为何难以实现现代化，强调安全性、传统观念和制造工艺的限制导致新设计的采用进展缓慢。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dancemagazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-186831014_Web_R1.jpg' data-full='https://dancemagazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-186831014_Web_R1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48571851' data-tags='AI,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48571851' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(374) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.greptile.com/blog/trex-code-execution' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TREX: An AI code reviewer that runs your code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TREX：一个能实际运行代码的 AI 代码评审工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48571851' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TREX is an AI code reviewer that actually runs your code in a sandbox to validate behavior, not just static analysis. This dynamic approach helps catch runtime bugs and security issues that static reviews can miss, though it introduces execution-safety considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TREX 是一个能实际运行代码来进行审查的 AI 代码评审工具，通过在沙箱中执行代码来发现运行时问题与安全隐患，超越了静态分析的范围。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://greptile.com/opengraph/greptile-blog.png' data-full='https://greptile.com/opengraph/greptile-blog.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48563047' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48563047' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(375) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://digitalorientalist.com/2017/08/21/digital-printing-of-arabic-explaining-the-problem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Digital Printing of Arabic: explaining the problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿拉伯文印刷的难题：问题解析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48563047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48563047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48563047' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article outlines the core challenges of rendering Arabic text in digital printing, including contextual shaping, ligatures, bidirectional layout, and diacritics. It explains why Arabic typesetting requires sophisticated shaping engines to preserve correct forms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释了在数字印刷中呈现阿拉伯文时的独特挑战，包括字形的上下文变化、连字、双向排版、元音标记以及字体排版引擎的工作原理。还讨论了排版系统需要如何处理上下文与书法规范以呈现正确的字形。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/digitalorientalist.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/07/movable2.jpg?fit=257%2C257&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/digitalorientalist.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/07/movable2.jpg?fit=257%2C257&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48541777' data-tags='Energy,Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48541777' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/william-heronemus-wind-energy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 1976 university experiment spun up the U.S. wind industry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1976 年的一项大学研究推动了美国现代风能产业</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48541777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48541777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48541777' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1976 university study catalyzed the modern U.S. wind industry by highlighting feasibility and policy momentum at the time. It helped attract investment and policy attention during the energy crisis era. The piece underscores how academic work can spark a national-scale clean energy sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1976 年的一项大学研究推动了美国现代风能产业的发展，凸显了风电的可行性并推动当时的政策与投资热潮。在能源危机背景下，这项工作被视为点燃国家级清洁能源产业的重要催化剂。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66894045&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66894045&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48490578' data-tags='Data,Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48490578' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shonczinner.github.io/posts/embedding-prediction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 90-year-old idea behind JEPA models: Canonical Correlation Analysis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JEPA 模型背后的 90 年前思想：Canonical Correlation Analysis</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48490578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48490578' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective explains that the core idea driving JEPA-style models—finding correlated representations across modalities—dates back to Canonical Correlation Analysis, a 90-year-old statistical method. The post shows how simple, elegant linear alignment ideas still underpin some modern embeddings, with implications for multimodal AI and interpretability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇回顾指出推动 JEPA 模型的核心思路——跨模态相关表示对齐，最早可追溯到 90 年前的 canonical correlation analysis（CCA）。文章展示了简单而优雅的线性对齐思想如何仍然支撑着现代多模态嵌入，并对多模态 AI 的可解释性提出启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shonczinner.github.io/posts/embedding-prediction/noisy_matrix_with_marginals.png' data-full='https://shonczinner.github.io/posts/embedding-prediction/noisy_matrix_with_marginals.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48503806' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48503806' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://feralui.vercel.app/#/captcha' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prove you&#x27;re human by winning a claw machine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过抓娃娃机证明你是人类</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48503806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48503806' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48503806' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful CAPTCHA concept replaces traditional challenges with a claw-machine game, letting humans prove themselves by winning a prize. The idea foregrounds gamified verification but raises questions about accessibility and practicality at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的验证码设想用抓娃娃机游戏取代传统测试，让人类通过赢得奖品来证明身份。该思路强调了游戏化验证的趣味性，同时也提出了无障碍性与规模化应用的现实问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48489309' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48489309' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.aptiv.com/en/insights/article/usb-power-delivery-plugging-into-the-benefits' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>USB Power Delivery: Plugging into the Benefits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>USB Power Delivery：深入挖掘其优势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48489309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48489309' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>USB Power Delivery enables higher wattage and smarter power negotiation across devices, enabling faster charging and thinner devices. It also supports universal cables and better battery management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>USB Power Delivery 通过更高的供电能力和更智能的协商实现更快充电和更薄的设备设计。它推动通用数据线和更高效的电池管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.aptiv.com/images/default-source/feature-stories/usb-power-delivery-plugging-into-the-benefits-open-graph.webp?sfvrsn=98364a36_1' data-full='https://www.aptiv.com/images/default-source/feature-stories/usb-power-delivery-plugging-into-the-benefits-open-graph.webp?sfvrsn=98364a36_1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48594733' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pypi.org/project/prylint/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fable Converted Pylint to Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fable 将 Pylint 转换为 Rust</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48594733' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Rust rewrite of Pylint by Fable promises dramatically faster Python linting and lower CPU usage on large repos. The project demonstrates how rewriting a core tooling component in Rust can yield significant performance gains while staying compatible with Python tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fable 将 Pylint 重写为 Rust，承诺在大型代码库上实现显著更快的 Python 代码静态分析并降低 CPU 占用。该项目展示了将核心工具组件用 Rust 重实现如何带来可观的性能提升，同时保持与 Python 工具链的兼容性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pypi.org/static/images/twitter.abaf4b19.webp' data-full='https://pypi.org/static/images/twitter.abaf4b19.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kcsrk.info/ocaml/oxcaml/teaching/nptel/llm/2026/06/13/an-oxcaml-book-that-runs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An O(x)Caml book that runs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>An O(x)Caml 书籍能跑起来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48516964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48516964' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental OCaml book that runs, enabling readers to execute code examples in place. The project demonstrates how executable textbooks can improve learning and reduce friction for readers exploring functional programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个能够运行的 OCaml 书籍项目，读者可直接在书中执行代码示例。此举展示了可运行教科书在提升学习体验、降低门槛方面的潜力，特别是在函数式编程学习中。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48573757' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/ai-chemist-improves-reaction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using AI to improve a challenging reaction in medicinal chemistry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 改善药物化学中的一项复杂反应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48573757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48573757' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI is used to optimize a difficult medicinal-chemistry reaction, predicting conditions, yields, and possible catalysts to accelerate lab work. The approach demonstrates how AI can shorten discovery timelines and reduce costly trial-and-error experiments in drug development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 AI 来优化药物化学中的一项复杂反应，预测条件、产率和潜在催化剂，加速实验室工作流程。这一做法展示了 AI 如何缩短药物发现周期，降低昂贵的试错成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/4NBFdbusKgYSDZQu73MAR9/2f84206eba72f43384d4bb005b97f8c3/molecule-one-SEO-Card.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/4NBFdbusKgYSDZQu73MAR9/2f84206eba72f43384d4bb005b97f8c3/molecule-one-SEO-Card.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1078160/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KDE Plasma 6.7 Released</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KDE Plasma 6.7 已发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48555755' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plasma 6.7 ships with usability refinements, performance improvements, and stability fixes across Wayland and X11. It sports UI tweaks and ongoing modernization of KDE’s desktop experience, with better multi-monitor support and smoother animations. The update continues KDE&#x27;s cadence of regular, incremental improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Plasma 6.7 对 KDE 桌面进行了可用性细化、性能改进和稳定性修复，涵盖 Wayland 与 X11 的表现。新版本在用户界面和桌面体验上继续推陈出新，提升多显示器支持和动画流畅度，体现 KDE 桌面生态的持续更新节奏。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://analogtv.net/mechanical-lab' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Nipkow Disk Mechanical TV Simulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尼普科盘机械电视模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48526256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48526256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48526256' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project simulates early mechanical television using a Nipkow disk, illustrating how a rotating disk scanned images line by line. It’s a practical demonstration of historical tech and a gateway for hands-on learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目通过尼普科盘模拟早期机械电视，展示了如何用旋转盘逐行扫描图像。这是对历史技术的实际演示，也是动手学习的入门。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48484711' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48484711' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.markhorrell.com/blog/2012/a-short-history-of-cerro-torre/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A short history of Cerro Torre, the most controversial mountain (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cerro Torre 的简史：最具争议的山峰（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48484711' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise history of Cerro Torre, the Patagonian peak famed for its dramatic climbs and controversial ascent disputed by climbers and guidebooks, tracing expeditions, contested routes, and the ethics of style in extreme mountaineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简要回顾巴塔哥尼亚 Cerro Torre 的历史——因极端攀登、被质疑的攀登记录以及对“风格与伦理”的持续辩论而著称。文章梳理了多次探险、被争议的路线，以及极限攀登中的风格问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/www.markhorrell.com/wp-content/uploads/PICT0277.jpg?fit=1200%2C900&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/www.markhorrell.com/wp-content/uploads/PICT0277.jpg?fit=1200%2C900&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48476539' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48476539' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/meta-pytorch/torchcodec/releases/tag/v0.14.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TorchCodec 0.14: HDR Video Decoding for CPU and CUDA, and Fast Wav Decoder</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TorchCodec 0.14：CPU 与 CUDA 的 HDR 视频解码，以及快速 WAV 解码器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48476539' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TorchCodec 0.14 adds HDR video decoding support on CPU and CUDA, expanding hardware-accelerated media processing for PyTorch workflows, plus a fast WAV decoder to speed audio paths. The update targets ML and media apps needing efficient, high-quality video and audio handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TorchCodec 0.14 增加了对 CPU 与 CUDA 的 HDR 视频解码支持，提升 PyTorch 工作流中的多媒体处理能力，并提供一个快速 WAV 解码器以加速音频路径。此次更新面向需要高效、质量并重的视频与音频处理的机器学习与多媒体应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e805b3af370cf933ce7ed7d80d8eddd15caab2f05e0f472aef5c4e1755529218/meta-pytorch/torchcodec/releases/tag/v0.14.0' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e805b3af370cf933ce7ed7d80d8eddd15caab2f05e0f472aef5c4e1755529218/meta-pytorch/torchcodec/releases/tag/v0.14.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48536167' data-tags='Space,Data,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48536167' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://horizons.lambda-combine.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Horizons JPL Solar System Data Demo and NASA DSN Updates: Datastar, Common Lisp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Horizons JPL 太阳系数据演示及 NASA DSN 更新：Datastar 与 Common Lisp</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48536167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48536167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48536167' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Horizons data demo showcases solar-system ephemerides and NASA DSN status, with updates to Datastar. It also notes the use of Common Lisp in workflows, illustrating the tooling ecosystem behind space missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Horizons 数据演示展示太阳系历书数据和 NASA 深空网络状态，提及 Datastar 的更新，以及在工作流中使用 Common Lisp，体现太空任务背后的工具生态。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48594090' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48594090' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/w84death/floppinux' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Embedded Linux on a Single Floppy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单一软盘上的嵌入式 Linux</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48594090' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates a tiny embedded Linux that can run from a single floppy disk, illustrating constraints-driven design and the feasibility of ultra-small systems. A useful reference for retro computing and constrained environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示可从单张软盘启动的极小型嵌入式 Linux，体现了在资源受限环境下的设计可行性。对复古计算和约束驱动的嵌入式项目具有参考意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/93e6f28b7f0b6e1f956a7edb19f5f13ca9806725064f5fad79494367a9c09b94/w84death/floppinux' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/93e6f28b7f0b6e1f956a7edb19f5f13ca9806725064f5fad79494367a9c09b94/w84death/floppinux' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48474982' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48474982' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/posts/quivers-a-year-of-linear-algebra-by-drawing-arrows.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quivers: A year of linear algebra by drawing arrows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Quivers：用画箭头来学习线性代数的一年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48474982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48474982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48474982' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quivers uses arrows and directed graphs to visualize linear algebra concepts over a year-long exploration. The approach aims to build intuition for vector spaces, linear maps, and transformations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过用箭头和有向图来直观呈现线性代数概念，这是一年中的学习尝试。该方法帮助理解向量空间、线性映射与变换。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/media/quivers-a-year-of-linear-algebra-by-drawing-arrows/cover.png' data-full='https://lisyarus.github.io/blog/media/quivers-a-year-of-linear-algebra-by-drawing-arrows/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48604558' data-tags='Politics,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48604558' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elizabethginexi.substack.com/p/summary-of-key-changes-in-ombs-proposed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Russell Vought is going to destroy American Science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Russell Vought 将毁掉美国科学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48604558' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This provocative piece argues that the proposed reforms to federal science policy under the Office of Management and Budget will undermine scientific independence, funding, and accountability. It highlights potential policy shifts affecting research funding, grant processes, and regulatory oversight, with implications for researchers and tech startups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇有争议的文章认为，预算办公室拟议的联邦科学政策改革将削弱科学独立性、资助和问责制，带来潜在负面影响。文章讨论了对研究经费、资助流程及监管的可能变动，以及对科研人员和科技创业公司的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48591562' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48591562' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bomwiki.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything Is BOM: Bill of Materials Encyclopedia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一切都是 BOM：物料清单百科全书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48591562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48591562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48591562' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Everything Is BOM argues that software and hardware products rely on a comprehensive bill of materials, mapping components, licenses, and provenance. Visible BOMs improve software supply chain security, license compliance, and risk management in modern tech stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Everything Is BOM 指出软件和硬件产品背后都依赖完整的物料清单，涵盖组件、许可和溯源信息。对 BOM 的可视化提升了软件供应链安全、许可合规性与风险管理，是现代技术栈的重要组成部分。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bomwiki.com/og-card-v2.png' data-full='https://bomwiki.com/og-card-v2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48580504' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://conf.nim-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nim Conf 2026 (Online, Sat June 20)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nim Conf 2026（线上，6月20日周六）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48580504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48580504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48580504' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Online Nim Conf 2026 gathers language enthusiasts for talks on language features, tooling, and ecosystem progress. Expect updates on performance, compile-time improvements, and cross-platform support. The event is a good barometer for where Nim is headed next.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>线上 Nim Conf 2026 汇聚语言爱好者，讨论语言特性、工具链与生态进展。预计涵盖性能、编译时间改进和跨平台支持等内容，是了解 Nim 未来走向的风向标。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48497873' data-tags='Science,Data,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48497873' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.construction-physics.com/p/biological-evolution-and-information' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Biological evolution and information acquisition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生物进化与信息获取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48497873' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that biological evolution can be understood through information acquisition, linking natural selection to data processing and measurement. The piece uses physics-inspired framing to explain how organisms encode, transmit, and optimize information for survival.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论证生物进化可以通过信息获取来理解，将自然选择与数据处理和测量联系起来。文章采用物理学启发的框架，解释生物如何编码、传递并优化信息以实现生存。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!v8fH!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F46bf94ca-109b-4884-aaac-4e04fafb2dd2_989x446.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!v8fH!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F46bf94ca-109b-4884-aaac-4e04fafb2dd2_989x446.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48620776' data-tags='Health,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48620776' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.randalolson.com/2026/06/16/aca-insurer-claim-denial-rates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Health insurance claim denial rates range from 13% to 35% by insurer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不同保险公司健康保险理赔拒付率差异显著</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620776' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at insurer-by-insurer denial rates reveals wide variation in how often claims are rejected, underscoring administrative complexity in US healthcare. The data invites discussion on transparency, policy reform, and patient impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对各保险公司拒付率的差异进行观察，发现存在显著变异，这反映出美国医疗账单处理的复杂性。数据引发对透明度、政策改革和患者影响的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.randalolson.com/assets/2026/06/aca-insurer-claim-denial-rates.png' data-full='https://www.randalolson.com/assets/2026/06/aca-insurer-claim-denial-rates.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48615776' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48615776' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.satisfice.com/blog/archives/488148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don’t use AI to write things that you present as your own work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别把 AI 写作当作自己的作品来展示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48615776' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues against presenting AI-generated text as one’s own work, outlining ethical guidelines and potential plagiarism risks. Encourages attribution and transparency when using AI for writing tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>反对将 AI 生成的文本当作自己的作品来展示，提出使用 AI 写作时的伦理准则与潜在的抄袭风险，鼓励在写作任务中保持署名与透明。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.satisfice.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/ttscopy2.jpg' data-full='https://www.satisfice.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/ttscopy2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48548695' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48548695' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ide.mit.edu/sites/default/files/publications/jcurve.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Productivity J-Curve [pdf] (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生产力 J 曲线 [pdf]（2018）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48548695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48548695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48548695' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The J-curve describes a temporary dip in productivity during the initial adoption of a new tool or process, followed by long-term gains as learning and optimization occur. It offers actionable guidance for managers on timing, experimentation, and metrics to distinguish short-term dips from long-run gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>J 曲线揭示在采用新工具或流程初期生产力常常短暂下降，随后随着学习和优化而提升。本文为管理者提供可执行的建议，帮助把握时机、设计试验并用正确的指标区分短期低谷与长期收益。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622788' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622788' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/health/2026/06/fda-advisors-unanimously-vote-to-approve-modernas-mrna-after-agency-drama/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FDA advisors unanimously vote to approve Moderna&#x27;s mRNA after agency drama</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FDA 顾问因此一致通过 Moderna 的 mRNA 疫苗，经历机构风波</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622788' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FDA advisory panel unanimously approved Moderna&#x27;s mRNA vaccine after a period of regulatory drama, signaling progress for the company&#x27;s mRNA portfolio. The outcome illustrates the tension between science advisory processes and agency decisions, with implications for rollout timelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FDA 专家组在经历监管风波后的一致意见通过了 Moderna 的 mRNA 疫苗审批，标志着其 mRNA 组合的进展。此结果反映了科学顾问过程与监管决策之间的博弈，对后续推广时间表具有影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2020/12/GettyImages-1290558683.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2020/12/GettyImages-1290558683.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48549883' data-tags='Politics,Finance,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48549883' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thehill.com/homenews/administration/5924963-vance-iran-300b-reconstruction-fund/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vance: Iran can have access to $300B reconstruction fund</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vance：伊朗可以获得 3,000 亿美元的重建基金</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>参议员 Vance 提出让伊朗获得 3000 亿美元重建基金的设想，凸显在制裁豁免与推动伊朗重建之间的政治博弈，以及经济激励与区域安全之间的微妙关系。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48569677' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48569677' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.brownplt.org/2026/06/09/pick.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human Judgment as a Specification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将人类判断作为规范</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48569677' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that human judgments can serve as a specification for AI behavior, enabling scalable alignment by turning preferences into concrete constraints. It discusses practical implications for human-in-the-loop AI design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人类判断可以被用作 AI 行为的规范，将偏好转化为具体约束，从而实现可扩展的对齐。这一思路对人机协作型 AI 设计有重要启示。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48609422' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look back at experiments to run web browsers on PDAs reveals the severe constraints of early mobile devices. The discussion highlights how memory, CPU, and storage limits shaped the first mobile web and set the stage for today’s browsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾在 PDA 上运行网页浏览器的尝试，揭示早期移动设备的资源限制——内存、CPU、存储的约束如何塑造了最初的移动网页体验，并奠定今日浏览器的演进。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48551069' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48551069' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sigarch.org/the-return-of-rigorous-full-system-timing-simulation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Return of Rigorous Full-System Timing Simulation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>重新兴起的严格全系统时序仿真</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48551069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48551069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48551069' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Full-system timing simulation is making a rigorous comeback, enabling precise evaluation of hardware-software interactions across CPUs, memory, and interconnects. New methodologies and tooling are reviving this niche, helping architects predict performance and verify designs earlier in the cycle. It’s about realism over coarse estimates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全系统时序仿真正重新崛起，使得对处理器、内存与互连之间的硬件–软件交互进行更精确的性能评估成为可能。通过新的方法和工具，架构师能够在设计早期就预测性能、验证实现，提升真实感与可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sigarch.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/ChatGPT-Image-May-30-2026-at-07_00_29-PM.png' data-full='https://www.sigarch.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/ChatGPT-Image-May-30-2026-at-07_00_29-PM.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The trend could reshape agriculture, food security, and sustainability narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Noema 的文章讨论了在 2024 年实现‘空气中制造食物’的突破，可能涉及新型合成技术、生物工艺或大气碳利用在食品生产中的应用。此趋势可能改变农业、粮食安全与可持续性议题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modeloop.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Modeloop – From visual algorithms to microcontroller C code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Modeloop——从可视化算法到微控制器 C 代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48543968' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modeloop translates visual, block-based algorithms into microcontroller C code, bridging the gap between design and embedded deployment. It enables rapid prototyping for hardware projects where performance and resource constraints matter. This approach foreshadows a low-code pathway for hardware-software co-design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Modeloop 将可视化的块状算法转换为微控制器的 C 代码，缩短设计到嵌入式实现的距离。它让在资源受限的硬件上进行快速原型开发成为现实，提升性能与效率的权衡。此类低代码路径将推动软硬件协同设计的创新。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>VoiceDraw converts spoken system design ideas into live diagrams, letting teams visualize architecture as they talk. It reduces friction in early design reviews and captures ideas before they fade. The approach demonstrates how talk-to-diagram tools can accelerate design workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VoiceDraw 将口头的系统设计思路实时转化为图表，帮助团队在讨论时就可直观看到架构。它降低了早期设计评审的摩擦，便于捕捉易逝的想法。该工具展示了将口述转化为图形的设计协作方式如何加速工作流程。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heise.de/en/news/Report-EU-to-soon-classify-AWS-and-Azure-as-gatekeepers-under-DSA-11337873.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU to soon classify AWS and Azure as gatekeepers under DSA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟将很快把 AWS 与 Azure 列为 DSA 下的守门人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48596622' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU regulators are set to classify AWS and Azure as gatekeepers under the DSA, signaling that dominant cloud platforms will face stricter obligations around data access, interoperability, and anti-competitive practices. The move could reshape cloud competition and push major providers toward more open ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟监管机构拟将 AWS 与 Azure列为 DSA 下的守门人，要求在数据访问、互操作性和反竞争方面承担更严格的义务。此举或将改变云服务市场的竞争格局，推动建立更开放的生态系统。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Soccer arcade games have evolved from mechanical, coin-op machines to modern digital titles with licensed teams and physics-based gameplay. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>足球街机游戏经历了从机械装置到现代数字游戏的演变，涉及许可、玩法与物理仿真等方面的进步。文章追溯数十年的发展，强调游戏设计、易用性与怀旧情感如何与足球文化交织。它展示了街机在不同时代的硬件演进和玩家体验的变化。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48547271' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://economist.com/the-americas/2026/06/11/techno-libertarians-are-flocking-to-the-caribbean' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Techno-libertarians are flocking to the Caribbean</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技自由主义者蜂拥加勒比</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48547271' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Techno-libertarians are migrating to Caribbean jurisdictions offering crypto-friendly, low-regulation environments, seeking freedom from perceived surveillance and heavy taxation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科技自由主义者正涌向加勒比地区的数字友好、低监管环境，寻求摆脱监控与高税负。文章探讨此趋势对当地经济、治理及科技创业生态的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48615529' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48615529' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Armstrong_effect' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Armstrong Effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿姆斯特朗效应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48615529' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In electronics and radio history, the Armstrong effect refers to positive feedback in regenerative circuits that enables oscillation, a foundational concept behind early radios. The note highlights how such effects shaped design choices in RF amplifiers and receivers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在电子学和无线电史上，阿姆斯特朗效应指的是再生电路中的正反馈，使振荡成为可能，是早期无线电的基础概念之一。该文点出这一效应如何影响射频放大器与接收机的设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/3/3d/Meyers_b5_s0529_b3.png' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/3/3d/Meyers_b5_s0529_b3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sabela.datahaskell.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Sabela – A Reactive Notebook for Haskell</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48525340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48525340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48525340' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sabela provides a reactive notebook tailored for Haskell, enabling live feedback and reactivity when code or data changes. It demonstrates how language-native notebooks can improve experimentation and teaching for functional programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sabela 为 Haskell 提供了一个反应式笔记本，代码与数据变更时可获得即时反馈。它展示了将笔记本式工作流与函数式编程结合的可行性，适合教学与探索性编程。</p>
