<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48570585' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eli.thegreenplace.net/2012/07/12/computed-goto-for-efficient-dispatch-tables' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computed goto for efficient dispatch tables (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于高效派发表的计算 goto（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48570585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48570585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48570585' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains how computed goto can speed up dispatch tables in interpreters by reducing branches and improving cache locality. It’s a practical trick for building high-performance language runtimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讲解了计算 goto 如何通过减少分支预测失败并提升缓存局部性来加速解释器的派发表，是构建高性能语言运行时的实用技巧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eli.thegreenplace.net/images/logosmall.png' data-full='https://eli.thegreenplace.net/images/logosmall.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zengm.com/blog/2026/06/vibecoded-games/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Being an old school web-based sports sim dev in the era of vibe coded games</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post reflects on building web-based sports simulations in an era driven by vibe-coded games, emphasizing data-driven simulations, deterministic results, and maintainable code over flashy effects. It offers lessons for long‑lived game logic, scaling, and tooling in a browser-first ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文回顾在 vibe 编码游戏盛行的时代，如何开发基于网页的体育模拟，强调数据驱动的仿真、确定性结果和可维护的代码，而非炫酷特效。为在浏览器优先的生态中构建长期可扩展的游戏逻辑、工具链提供了经验教训。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://0xkrt26.github.io/math_behind_security/2026/06/09/the-story-of-the-hash-function.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chopped, Stored, Secured – The Story of the Hash Function</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>哈希函数的故事：切碎、存储、安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48482951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48482951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48482951' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Charts the history of hash functions from early constructions to modern cryptographic primitives, explaining how they enable data integrity checks, quick lookups, and secure authentication. The piece traces the lifecycle from input processing to storage and comparison, highlighting security pitfalls and best practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梳理哈希函数的历史，从早期构造到现代密码学原语，解释哈希在数据完整性、快速查找与身份认证中的作用。文章回顾从输入处理、存储到比对的全过程，强调安全要点与最佳实践。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48579913' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48579913' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(428) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://x86ecosystem.org/resource/ai-compute-extensions-ace-specification/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Compute Extensions (ACE) Specification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 计算扩展（ACE）规范</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48579913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48579913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48579913' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ACE defines standardized interfaces for AI compute resources, enabling better interoperability between CPUs, accelerators, and runtimes. The spec aims to streamline AI offload, memory coherence, and driver integration, reducing vendor lock-in. Adoption could accelerate AI workloads across platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ACE 定义了 AI 计算资源的标准化接口，提升与 CPU、加速器及运行时之间的互操作性。该规范旨在简化 AI 加载、内存一致性和驱动集成，降低厂商锁定。普及可能加速各平台的 AI 工作负载。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://x86ecosystem.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/x86-logo-recolor-300x183.png' data-full='https://x86ecosystem.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/x86-logo-recolor-300x183.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622590' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622590' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(429) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/raiyanyahya/recall' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop wasting tokens and re explaining your project between sessions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>停止浪费令牌，在会话之间避免重复解释你的项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622590' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recall-enabled tooling highlights how persisting context across sessions can cut token waste and avoid reintroducing project details. It helps developers and chatbots keep conversations coherent, reducing redundant prompts and friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>可记忆上下文的工具展示了跨会话保存上下文以减少令牌消耗、避免重复介绍项目信息的方式。对开发者和聊天机器人有利，有助维持对话连贯性，降低重复提问与摩擦。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e18024e0b374fdd2af950cb7363cfb27e2add6c47f12767697871067e8a92657/raiyanyahya/recall' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e18024e0b374fdd2af950cb7363cfb27e2add6c47f12767697871067e8a92657/raiyanyahya/recall' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带 Web 仪表板的 SMTP 中继</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48547658' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This project provides a self-hosted SMTP relay with a web dashboard for monitoring and management. It appeals to small teams needing simple, observable mail routing, while covering security basics like TLS, authentication, and anti-spam controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目提供一个带 Web 仪表板的自托管 SMTP 中继，便于监控和管理邮箱路由。适合小型团队使用，强调 TLS、认证与反垃圾等安全要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48527210' data-tags='Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48527210' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://ibm1130.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All about the IBM 1130 Computing System</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IBM 1130 计算系统全解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48527210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48527210' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This deep dive covers the IBM 1130’s 16-bit architecture, peripherals, and programming environment, shedding light on its role as an affordable minicomputer that broadened access to computing. Its ecosystem helped shape education and early software development in the 1960s.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇深入介绍 IBM 1130 的 16 位架构、外设与编程环境，揭示其作为经济型微型计算机如何让更多人接触到计算。其生态系统在 1960 年代推动了教育与早期软件开发的发展。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美空军B-52轰炸机起飞后坠毁，爱德华空军基地称</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48546306' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US Air Force B-52 bomber crashed shortly after takeoff at Edwards Air Force Base, with preliminary details pending investigation. The incident prompts safety reviews and could affect training and operations at the base, as investigators inspect airframe, systems, and weather factors. It also highlights ongoing concerns about aging strategic aircraft reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美空军一架B-52轰炸机在爱德华空军基地起飞后不久坠毁，尚在调查中。事件将促使基地进行安全评估，影响相关训练与任务，并由调查人员检查机体与系统以及气象因素。也反映出对老旧战略机型可靠性的持续关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NSA leadership warns that AI-enabled tools like Mythos could infiltrate classified networks within hours, underscoring the need for stronger security, auditing, and policy responses.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(438) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/06/16/the-uks-teen-social-media-ban-is-political-theater-not-child-safety-policy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The UK&#x27;s Teen Social Media Ban Is Political Theater, Not Child Safety Policy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国青少年社交媒体禁令是政治表演，并非儿童安全政策</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48562957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48562957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48562957' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Critics argue the UK teen social media ban is more about signaling political aims than delivering real safety benefits. The policy risks pushing teens toward less regulated spaces and ignores underlying fixes like digital literacy, parental controls, and mental health support. It shows how policy by spectacle can undermine data-driven safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评者认为英国对青少年的社交媒体禁令更多是传达政治信号，而非真正提高儿童安全的有效措施。该政策可能让青少年转向监管较少的平台，忽视数字素养、家长监管以及心理健康等根本性解决方案，反映出以作秀取代数据驱动治理的风险。</p>

It underscores the tension between secrecy and accountability in elite tech circles.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://securelist.com/dozens-of-malicious-wallpapers-found-on-steam-workshop/120186/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gamers beware: malicious wallpapers on Steam found stealing accounts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>玩家请警惕：Steam 上发现窃取账户的恶意壁纸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48558545' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found dozens of malicious wallpapers in Steam Workshop that exfiltrate credentials or token data when viewed or installed. The incident highlights threats hidden in user-generated content and the need for better moderation and user vigilance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在 Steam Workshop 发现多枚窃取凭据的恶意壁纸，安装或查看时可能窃取账户凭据或令牌。此事凸显用户生成内容中潜在的威胁，以及需要加强审核与用户警惕。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/us-government-reportedly-allowing-federal-data-center-rules-to-expire-2000772083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Government Reportedly Allowing Federal Data Center Rules to Expire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>据报道，美国政府允许联邦数据中心规则到期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48546358' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A government report suggests federal data-center rules are being allowed to expire, potentially before replacements or updates are in place. The move could affect energy efficiency, security standards, and cloud migration plans across agencies. Critics warn about loss of modernization momentum and resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，美国政府允许联邦数据中心相关规则到期，可能在未有替代或更新时出现空缺。此举可能影响各机构的能效、安全标准与云迁移计划。批评者担忧将减缓现代化与韧性提升的步伐。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/06/users-cry-foul-after-amd-stripped-memory-crypto-from-its-consumer-cpus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Users cry foul after AMD stripped memory crypto from its consumer CPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD 移除消费级 CPU 的内存加密功能，用户不满</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48559827' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD has stripped memory encryption features from its consumer CPUs, triggering user backlash over reduced hardware security options. The move affects security-conscious workloads and consumer trust, potentially pushing users toward enterprise SKUs or competing products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 将消费级 CPU 的内存加密相关功能移除，引发用户对硬件安全性的忧虑与强烈不满。这一举措可能影响对安全敏感工作负载的用户体验，甚至让部分用户转向企业级或竞争产品。</p>

The case may signal a chilling effect on open critique and could influence retention and AI strategy decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊正在调查公开批评其 AI 数据中心扩张的工程师，凸显企业内部异议与内部沟通政策执行之间的紧张关系。这起事件可能对公开批评产生震慑效应，并影响团队留任和 AI 战略决策。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/fguzmanai/status/2065832668172845209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GateGPT: 56k tokens per second Transformer (KV cache) on FPGA at 80 MHz</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GateGPT：在 80 MHz 的 FPGA 上实现 56k token/s 的 Transformer（KV 缓存）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48557535' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hardware-focused post demonstrates a Transformer KV-cache design on FPGA capable of 56k tokens per second at 80 MHz, illustrating how architectural choices and memory bandwidth enable high-throughput inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章展示在 80 MHz 的 FPGA 上实现的 Transformer KV-cache，吞吐达每秒 56k token，凸显架构优化与内存带宽对推理吞吐的推动作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fulcrum.inc/2026/06/09/inverse-rubric-optimization.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inverse Rubric Optimization: A testbed for agent science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向评量标准优化：代理科学的测试床</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48485277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48485277' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inverse Rubric Optimization presents a framework to frame agent behavior in terms of evaluative rubrics that can be inverted to study alignment, incentive design, and evaluation dynamics. It offers a testbed for experimenting with how goals translate into observable actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>逆向评量标准优化提供了一种将代理行为以评估性准则来框定的框架，并可对准则进行反演研究对齐、激励设计和评估动力学。它为研究目标如何转化为可观察行动提供了测试床。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48545293' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Alaska Server describes a rugged edge/server deployment in Alaska, highlighting considerations for remote connectivity, power supply, and maintenance. The piece sheds light on how extreme environments shape hardware choices and operational strategies for distributed computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《阿拉斯加服务器》介绍了在极端偏远环境中的边缘服务器部署，强调远程连通性、供电与维护的挑战。文中讨论了极端环境如何影响硬件选型和分布式计算的运维策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48597592' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48597592' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(466) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bunkum.us/2026/06/18/cocktail-ingredients-milp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cocktail Optimization, an Integer Programming Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鸡尾酒优化：一个整数规划问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48597592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48597592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48597592' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A MILP formulation for cocktail optimization under taste, color, and cost constraints. Demonstrates how integer programming can model combinatorial recipe optimization and even inform bar menus.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给出在口味、颜色与成本约束下的鸡尾酒配方优化的整数规划建模，展示了 MILP 如何将组合配方设计转化为可求解的问题，并为酒吧菜单提供决策支持。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48560886' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.databricks.com/company/newsroom/press-releases/databricks-launches-ltap-first-lake-transactionalanalytical' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Databricks Launches LTAP: A Unified OLAP/OLTP Data Architecture</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Databricks 推出 LTAP：统一的 OLAP/OLTP 数据架构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48560886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48560886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48560886' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Databricks unveils LTAP, a first unified OLAP/OLTP data architecture that blends transactional and analytical workloads on a data lakehouse. It signals a shift toward real-time analytics on lakehouses, with questions about performance, consistency, and cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Databricks 发布 LTAP，首次将 OLAP 与 OLTP 统一在同一数据架构中，借助数据湖实现事务性和分析性工作负载的并存。这表明在湖仓一体化上迈向实时分析，但也带来性能、一致性与成本的新挑战。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(468) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.darpa.mil/research/challenges/lift' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DARPA Heavy Life Challenge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DARPA 重生命挑战计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48594933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48594933' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DARPA&#x27;s Heavy Life Challenge (LIFT) seeks to push AI and robotics research toward robust, long-horizon learning in dynamic, real-world settings. The program outlines challenges and milestones to accelerate reliable, adaptable systems for critical tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DARPA 的重生命挑战计划（LIFT）推动 AI 与机器人研究在动态真实环境中实现长期、稳健的学习与适应能力。该计划设定挑战和里程碑，旨在加速构建可靠、可迁移的关键任务智能系统。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48550489' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Firefox-152-Download' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox 152 Now Available with JPEG-XL Support</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48550489' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 152 ships with JPEG-XL support, enabling superior image compression and quality compared to older formats while preserving lossless capabilities. The move is part of a broader push to modernize web media, reduce bandwidth, and improve user-perceived performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 152 引入 JPEG- XL 支持，提供比传统图像格式更高效的压缩与画质，同时支持无损特性。此举是推动网页多媒体现代化、降低带宽消耗、提升用户体验的一环。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security report reveals that China-linked spies remained embedded in medical and military networks for over a year, highlighting enduring threats to R&amp;D infrastructure and the need for stronger segmentation and monitoring.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Ohio policy limiting children&#x27;s access to social media was upheld by a US court, marking a notable endorsement of state-level controls for minors online. The ruling highlights ongoing debates over digital safety, parental authority, and platform responsibilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定俄亥俄州可以限制未成年人使用社交媒体，显示出州层面对青少年网络安全的潜在新方向。这一裁决凸显了数字安全、家长监管与平台在年龄限制方面的责任之间的持续辩论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(476) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.minecraft.net/en-us/article/minecraft-java-edition-26-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minecraft: Java Edition 26.2, the first version with Vulkan 1.2</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48567028' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Java Edition 26.2 adds Vulkan 1.2 support, enabling better GPU acceleration, reduced CPU bottlenecks, and improved cross-platform performance. The change signals continued graphics updates and performance improvements in a long-running title.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Java 版 26.2 首次加入 Vulkan 1.2 支持，提升 GPU 加速、减轻 CPU 瓶颈并改善跨平台性能。这一更新表明这款长期运行的游戏仍在持续推进图形与性能优化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48556335' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48556335' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mssv.net/2026/06/16/meet-nikolai-evreinov-the-19th-century-nathan-fielder/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meet Nikolai Evreinov, the 19th century Nathan Fielder</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>遇见尼古拉·埃夫列诺夫：19世纪的 Nathan Fielder</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48556335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48556335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48556335' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece profiles Nikolai Evreinov, a 19th-century theater innovator whose prankster, meta-theatrical stunts prefigure contemporary performance art and prank-based media. It draws parallels to a modern Nathan Fielder, showing how theatrical deception can critique society and reshape audience engagement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章介绍尼古拉·埃夫列诺夫，这位19世纪的戏剧革新者以其伪装与元剧场式的行为艺术先驱着称，与当代喜剧人 Nathan Fielder 风格相映成趣。通过对比，揭示戏剧欺骗如何对社会进行评论，并挑战观众的预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/mssv.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/stormningen_av_vinterpalatset-1.jpg?fit=1200%2C675&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/mssv.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/stormningen_av_vinterpalatset-1.jpg?fit=1200%2C675&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48560913' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48560913' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/i/status/2066825204207091926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is potentially a Dunning-Kruger effect amplifier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 可能是 Dunning-Kruger 效应的放大器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48560913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48560913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48560913' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A contention that AI can amplify the Dunning-Kruger effect, making less skilled users appear more confident due to plausible outputs. This has implications for UX, trust, and safety, warranting stronger guardrails and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 可能放大低技能者的自信偏误，因为其输出常显得可信，从而掩盖真实能力。对用户体验、信任和安全提出挑战，需要更强的引导、透明度和防护措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1364645619705511936/IGTT_tnL_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1364645619705511936/IGTT_tnL_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48545569' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative forecast that large language models will automate repetitive duplication tasks, reminiscent of 8-track era workflows, highlighting how AI shifts production paradigms. It reframes routine duplication work as ripe for automation and process improvement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章预言大型语言模型将自动化重复性拷贝工作，类似于 8 轨道时代的工作流程，凸显 AI 将改变生产范式。它把常规的拷贝工作重新定位为自动化与流程改进的对象。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622987' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rent collections in New York are down, with economists and landlords searching for explanations amid data gaps and shifting rental markets. The drop could signal broader macro trends or local policy or economic shifts affecting cash flow for landlords and tenants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约的租金收取率下降，数据不清与市场变化使经济学家和房东寻找原因。下滑可能反映宏观趋势、地方政策或经济因素对房东与租客现金流的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes AI policy under a Trump-influenced framework and asks whether France can still achieve digital sovereignty, discussing strategies like diversifying suppliers, data localization, and Europe-wide AI initiatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章在特朗普影响下的 AI 政策框架下探讨法国能否实现数字自主，提出通过多元化供应、数据本地化和欧洲级 AI 战略等途径来提高自主性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48602862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48602862' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes airgapped workflows to hide secrets during AI agent interactions and npm installs, reducing the risk of leakage. Practical guidance for developers handling sensitive credentials in AI-enabled environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出通过隔离工作流（Airgap）在 AI 代理交互与 NPM 安装中隐藏机密，降低数据泄露风险。为在AI驱动环境中处理敏感凭据的开发者提供实用建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48606199' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48606199' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(485) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/0ba6b511-649e-4a29-bb99-f824584ec93d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to lose a war in three easy steps</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48606199' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FT opinion piece distills three easy steps that can lead to military defeat, focusing on strategy, governance, and information management. It ties these ideas to modern tech-enabled warfare and geopolitics, underscoring how planning flaws can be fatal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FT 的专栏梳理了导致战争失败的三大易犯错误，聚焦战略、治理和信息管理。结合当代科技与地缘政治，指出规划失误、后勤不足或错误的信息战等因素如何致使作战能力快速下降。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48604801' data-tags='Data,Web,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48604801' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(486) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ascorbic/cirrus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cirrus: ATProto Personal Data Server That Runs on Cloudflare Workers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cirrus：在 Cloudflare Workers 上运行的 ATProto 个人数据服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48604801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48604801' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cirrus is a personal data server for AT Protocol that runs on Cloudflare Workers, enabling users to host their own data with decentralized protocol support. It demonstrates feasibility and limits of edge runtimes for personal data sovereignty, including storage and privacy tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cirrus 是一个在 Cloudflare Workers 上运行的 AT Protocol 个人数据服务器，允许用户在边缘计算环境中托管自己的数据，并支持去中心化协议。它展示了边缘运行时在个人数据主权方面的可行性与局限性，以及存储和隐私权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9487d1b53cd1ff4449969d0e1dd5d5914504cf913bdcfd93cb063440394f2f78/ascorbic/cirrus' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9487d1b53cd1ff4449969d0e1dd5d5914504cf913bdcfd93cb063440394f2f78/ascorbic/cirrus' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48595251' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48595251' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(487) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bangalore-property-buy-vs-rent.pagey.site/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Renting vs. Buying – A case study for Bangalore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>班加罗尔租房 vs 买房：案例研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48595251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48595251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48595251' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This Bangalore case study weighs renting versus buying for tech workers, factoring in price-to-rent ratios, cap rates, and total cost of ownership. It highlights how local market conditions, mobility, and career plans shape housing decisions in a fast-growing tech hub.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本案例研究比较了班加罗尔科技工作者的租房与买房成本，考虑价格与租金比、投资回报率以及总拥有成本。研究指出本地市场、流动性需求和职业规划如何影响这一全球增长的科技中心的住房决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622922' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(489) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spacedaily.com/t-the-notebooks-marie-curie-used-in-her-research-remain-radioactively-contaminated-more-than-a-hundred-years-later-some-curie-papers-have-been-found-contaminated-with-radium-226-an-isotope-with-a-half/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The notebooks Marie Curie filled with her research are still dangerous to touch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>居里夫人研究笔记至今仍具放射性污染风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 7</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622922' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Marie Curie’s notebooks remain dangerously contaminated with radium-226 even after a century, forcing strict handling restrictions. The enduring hazard underscores challenges of preserving scientific history while ensuring safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>居里夫人的笔记在一百多年后仍含有放射性镭-226，需严格的处理与保存规定。这一长期风险凸显在保护科学史与保障安全之间需要权衡与长期投入。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spacedaily.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/curie-radioactive-notebooks.jpg' data-full='https://spacedaily.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/curie-radioactive-notebooks.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48621908' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(490) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cen.acs.org/materials/2-d-materials/graphene-2D-materials-reproducibility-industry/104/web/2026/06' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Graphene experts are trying to close reproducibility gap in 2D materials</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>石墨烯领域专家努力缩小二维材料的可重复性差距</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48621908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 7</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48621908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48621908' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Graphene researchers are working to close reproducibility gaps in 2D materials by standardizing synthesis and measurement methods. Progress could accelerate industrial adoption and ensure consistent material quality across labs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>石墨烯及其他二维材料的可重复性差距正在通过标准化合成与测量方法来缩小。进展可能加速产业化并提升跨实验室的一致性质量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/image/CENODS/Graphene-stakeholders-want-better-reproducibility---307850:Widescreen?$responsive$&amp;qlt=90,0&amp;resMode=sharp2&amp;fmt=webp' data-full='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/image/CENODS/Graphene-stakeholders-want-better-reproducibility---307850:Widescreen?$responsive$&amp;qlt=90,0&amp;resMode=sharp2&amp;fmt=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shnatsel.medium.com/safe-simd-in-rust-even-on-the-inside-c6f1ff381828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Safe SIMD in Rust, Even on the Inside – By Sergey &quot;Shnatsel&quot; Davidoff</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 的安全 SIMD：内部实现也能保持内存安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 7</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48618427' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into safe SIMD usage in Rust by Sergey Davidoff explains how to use SIMD without compromising safety, including architectural caveats and compiler-friendly patterns. It shows practical ways to keep performance gains while preserving correctness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shnatsel（Sergey Davidoff）深入讲解在 Rust 中如何安全使用 SIMD，包括架构注意事项与对编译器友好的实现模式。提供保持高性能同时确保正确性的实用做法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48577601' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48577601' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/06/17/books/carlo-ginzburg-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Carlo Ginzburg, Who Told the History of the Obscure, Dies at 87</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卡洛·金茨堡：讲述隐秘历史的学者去世，享年87岁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48577601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 7</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48577601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48577601' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Carlo Ginzburg, a pivotal historian known for microhistory and the study of obscure pasts, has died at 87. His work illuminated marginalized perspectives and showed how small, detailed investigations can reshape our understanding of medieval and early modern life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>卡洛·金茨堡是以微观史学著称的历史学家，专注研究被忽视的历史细节。去世时享年87岁，他的研究展示了如何通过细微的史料重新塑造对中世纪和早期现代生活的理解。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622061' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622061' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/harihkk/Debug-Brief' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: DebugBrief – turn debugging sessions into reports, no AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DebugBrief：将调试会话转化为报告，无需 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622061' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DebugBrief turns debugging sessions into readable reports without relying on AI, helping engineers document issues, steps, and fixes efficiently. It streamlines post-mortems and onboarding for new maintainers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DebugBrief 将调试会话整理成可读报告，无需依赖 AI，帮助工程师高效记录问题、步骤与修复。提升事后总结和新维护者的上手效率。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48617059' data-tags='Startups,Design,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48617059' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(494) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/gadgets/953066/nothing-cmf-phone-delayed-ram-prices' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nothing&#x27;s co-founder says CMF can&#x27;t build a phone &#x27;at a price that makes sense&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nothing 联合创始人称 CMF 无法在可承受的价格打造手机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48617059' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nothing&#x27;s co-founder argues that CMF-focused design can&#x27;t deliver a phone at a price that makes sense given current supply chains and material costs. The piece hints at the ongoing design-versus-cost tension hardware startups face, and what it means for future affordable devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nothing 的联合创始人表示，在当前供应链和材料成本压力下，CMF 为主的设计难以以合理价格打造手机。此言揭示了硬件创业在设计与成本之间的持续博弈，以及对未来更实惠设备的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/04/nothing-cmf-phone-2-pro-01.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.741906587151%2C100%2C78.516186825698&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/04/nothing-cmf-phone-2-pro-01.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.741906587151%2C100%2C78.516186825698&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
